0⃣1⃣


*Itafa waiwaye akoda yaushe tana zama adon tafiya ne...*💃💃




*Bismillahi rahamanir rahim*

,,,,,, Cikin sauri take har haɗa littattafan dake zube akasan ɗakin, cikin mintina ƙalilan, duk ta gama harhaɗawa ,ta ije kowanne a ma'adanar shi, tana fitowa suka hada ido lokaci daya suka sakarwa junan su murmushi.

Tace"aunty kingani Sai yanzu nagama.

"Alhamdulillah dama ALLAH ALLAH nake naji kin gama,  domin yanzun duk inda yake ina da tabbacin yana kan hanya,saiki shiga ciki kiyi wanka kizo kici abinci.

"To aunty bani minti goma IN SHA ALLAH.

"Jeki nabaki minti talatin.

Aikuwa bata jira ta karasa maganar ta ba, tanufi ɗakin ta, ta sakar wa kanta ruwa tana lumshe ido zuciyar ta cike da fargaban abinda zata tarar agun Yayan nata... 

cikin mintina goma harta gama shiri.
Bawani kwaliya tayi ba, Amma tayi kyau ta sa riga da zani na atamfa sannan ta dauko hijabin ta, harkasa ta saka sannan tafito. 

Tana fitowa suka hada ido da ya'yan nata, Yana cin abinci." ta durkusa harkasa ta gaishe shi , sannan ta nufi kitchin ta debo abinci ta zauna tana ci.

Babu wanda yayiwa wani magana acikin su harsuka gama, ta kwashe kayan abincin tashiga dashi kichin.
Harzata buɗe kofar dakin ta, taji ankira sunan ta Wanda ko amafarki taji, muryan Nan tasan ma'mallakin ta

Cikin natsuwa take tafiya har takai inda yake zaune tunshigowar shi tace"ya'ya gani.

Saida suka kusa mintina uku zuwa biyar baice mata kumai ba,daga bisani yadago Kai, Yana kallon ta sannan yace"wai saboda ALLAH maryam sai yaushe zakiyi hankali, ki daina wawta? 

Cikin rashin fahimtar inda yadosa take kallon shi, Dan itah a'iya saninta babu abinda ya haɗasu  bata yi komai ba...  Tsawar daya daka mata shiya dawo da itah daga duniyar tunanin da tashilla.... 

Zaki bace min dagani ko saina rufe ki da duka kije ki dauko han out ɗin nagani.
Jikin ta har kerma yake tanufi dakin tana shiga ta fashe da matsanan cin kuka,cikin kukan tace,O Ni maryam na shiga uku wanan wani irin ya'ya ne?  dakayi abin fada dawan da bana faɗa ba sai ya hau ka da masifa.

Nan dai tashare hawaye ta fita dantasan,ko giyar wake take sha bazata fita da hawaye afuskar ta ba.

Yanda ta barshi haka ta dawo tasame shi,ta mika mishi Yana karba,ta sunkuyar dakan ta babu abinda takeyi aranta sai addu'ar ALLAH yasa ta rubuta daidai yanda yace.

Cikin tsawa taji ya kira sunan ta,saboda tsora ta har saida tayi fitsari a'inda take zaune.

"Na'am 

"abinda nace ki rubuto min kenan?
Kayi hakuri, ya'ya zan sake rubuto maka wani IN SHA ALLAH.

"Tashi kibani guri sakarya kawai.
Cikin sauri take tafiya,harta shiga dakinta tana shiga tafaɗa kan gado tana kuka Mai tsuma zuciyar duk wani mai sauraro ahaka dai harbarci barawo ya dauke ta.

Sai misalin karfe shida ta farka tana farkawa ta nufi toilet  tayi alwala tayi sallah tana gamawa tanufi kitchen Dan taya auntin ta,aiki Dan tana da lecture karfe 8am.

Tana sauka tanufi kitchen jinkarar ruwa shi ya tabbatar mata auntin nata tana ciki, "good morning aunty

"Good morning my beautiful sister,kin tashi lafiya?
Lafiya kalau.
Cikin sauri suke aiki basufi awa daya ba suka gama kammala duk abinda suke da bukata.
Suna gamawa ta nufi dakin ta tayi wanka ta danyi make up Dan kukan datayi jiya yasa duk idon ta sun shige ciki, doguwar rigar  jallabiya ta dauko ruwan madara ta saka, sannan  tadauko veil baka ta daura ta ɗauko  flat shoo baka ta saka aƙafarta.

Haka dai taji zuciyar ta na bugawa batasan dalili ba saikawai tayi addu'a ta nufi falo.

Tafiya take cikin natsuwa har'ta'iso falon tana Isa taga harsun Fara cin abincin."inah kwana ya'ya.

Lafiya Lau.
Nandanan tadauki plet ta zuba iya abinda zata iya ci, tana gamawa ta mike tsaye tace"aunty saina dawo.yaya sai... tunkafin takarasa ya daga mata hannu.

Koda takai makaranta jiki asanyaye ta Isa aji tana shiga lectural Yana shigowa ,ahaka dai har akagama lecture Bata fahimci komai ba. 

Tana fitowa daga ajin,ta nufi hanyar da zata sadata da gindin bishiyar, da ta saba Zama akoda yaushe.

tana zaune tayi zurfi cikin tunani taji anata kwalla mata kira,ɗagowar da zatayi ta hangi Yusuf mutumin da ya takurawa rayuwar ta.

Yana isowa inda take ya zauna suna fuskantar juna yace"
Haba Maryam kina ji inata kiranki kikayi banza Dani,kodai nayi miki laifi ne?

girgiza Kai tayi tace," a a wallahi babu abinda kamin kawai kai nane keɗan min ciwo.
"Ok babu matsala Amma dai kinsha magani ko?

Cikin kosawa da maganar shi tace"Yusuf bara natafi gida sai mun haɗu gobe.... 

Bata jira jin abinda zaice ba ta miƙe tana tafiya kamar ance ta juya baya kawai Sai ganin mutum atsaye tayi yana kallon ta.... 

nandanan ta tsaya cak ko ɗaga kafarta bazata iya ba,  saboda tsanani tsora ta. 

Ganin haka yasa yanufe ta, gadan gadan Yana isowa inda take ya kalle ta cikin bacin rai.

"yace Daman Maryam abinda kike Yi kenan ko? to kisa aranki,,  Inda annabi yabar duniya haka kema kinbar makarantar Nan Kenan har'abadan.

Yana gama faɗan mata haka yajuya yanufi motarsa yana shiga ya fizgi motar... 

ganin fitar shi yasa ,itama ta nufi motar ta  tayi hanyan gida cikin  tashin hakali.

      ***
Hashim tunda yabar harabar makarantar tuki kawai yake kamar zai tashi sama ,kirjinsa kuwa tafasa take kamar zata faso kirjinsa,ikon ALLAH ne kawai yakai shi gida.
Yana shiga yasamu guri yayi parking.

Yana sauko wa yashiga cikin gidan ko sallama babu.... zainab tana zaune a fallon Yana shigowa tanufe shi tana 
Mishi sannu da zuwa.

Ko kallon inda take baiyi ba ganin haka yasa Tani me guri ta zauna,,  domin tasan idan Yana cikin fushi irin haka bayaso ana mishi magana.

Ba'afi mintina biyar ba,tashigo gidan cikin kuka tafaɗa jikin aunty zainab tana cewa, "aunty ki taimakeni, ki ceci rayuwa ta,wallahi babu abinda ke tsakani na dashi.

Cikin Rashin fahimtar inda maganar t ta dosa "tace wai meya faru ne?

Cikin kuka ta faɗa mata duk abinda ya faru.

"To ki daina kukan,Nan Kar ki sakarwa kanki zazzabi Kinga yanzu Yana cikin fushi,amma nayi miki alkawari zanbashi hakuri ke kuma ki kiyaye.

"IN SHA ALLAH.

Suna cikin haka kawai sai ganin shi ta yi yanufo gurin su hannun sa rike da bulala.
aikuwa tana ganin haka ta tashi da sauri ta boye abayan aunty zainab.

, "Dan ALLAH kayi hakuri wallahi ta faɗa  min babu abinda ke tsakanin su.

wallahi idan baki matsaba zan hada dake.

Sanin hali irin nashi yasa ta zame jikin ta

aikuwa tunda yafara dukan ta babu sa sauci tuntana ihun niman agaji,har ta sadakar ganin haka yasa zainab tanufo shi cikin kuka tace.

"Hashim  kasheta kakeson yine?

Jin haka yasa yadawo cikin haiyacin shi yanuna ta da Dan yatsar hannun shi Yana cewa" kadan kinan.

Aikuwa tunkan yakarasa magana tayi kasa sumammiya.
Ganin haka yasa aunty zainab kwalla kara atare suka nufo gurin ta ko motsi batayi..... 

*Komai yasa Hashim fusata haka Dan yaga Maryam da yusuf?*

*Bismillahi rahamanir rahim* 

0⃣2⃣



```Prayer is the key that opens your day to every blessing. It also is a lock that closes the night to keep you safe while sleeping. So what you have to do is “use your key all the time.```




,,, Cikin sauri yanufi motarsa da ita....  gudu yake Yana isa yafita da gudu Yana Kiran dr,Yana kallo akashiga da ita cikin.

"meyasa nakasa jurewa? meyasa meyasa?hakadai yarinka jerowa kansa tambayuyi,,, Har dr ya fito... 

"Ka kwantar da hankali ka,ta farraɗo,,  saidai Naga duk jikin ta shatin bulala,
Matar kace ko kanwar ka ?

Tambayar tazo mishi, abazata shiyasa yarasa amsar da zai bashi.... 

Kwanan su biyu a asibitin aka sallame su.
Cikin kwana biyun kuwa, duk wanda yakalli Hashim yasan cewa Yana cikin tashin hankali..... 

Ranar da aka sallamesu,suna
Isa gida duk kansu suka zauna afalon gida.... 
Hashim juyowa yayi Yana fuskantar Maryam yace.. 

"Maryam.

"Na'am ya'ya.

"Dama bawani abu yasa na bukaci idan muka dawo nayi miki magana da keba,, saidan inbaki damar ki.. 

Cikin sauri ta ɗago Kai ta kalle shi sannan ta maida Kai kasa.

"Shin Maryam aure kikeso ko karatu?

Saida tayi shiru nadan mintina biyu zuwa uku,,, sannan ta ɗago Kai tace"ya'ya wallahi karatu nakeso,wan...

Tunkafin takarasa ya dakatar da ita.

"To wancan Dana ganku tare wanene shi?

"Ya'ya Department dinmu ne daya,bani da wata alaka dashi.

Hashim saida yayi wata buyayyiyar ajiyar zuciya yace" to shikenan tunda shi wancan ba saurayin ki bane to wanene saurayin ki?

Cikin razana ta ɗago Kai tana kallon sa tace,"wallahi (ALLAH) ya'ya banda saurayi Kuma ,bazan auri kuwa ba sai wanda kazabar min, Dan inada tabbacin banida wanda yake so dabkauna ta irin ka,duk fadin duniyar Nan, Dan haka nake da tabbacin bazaka zabamin wanda zai cutar da niba. 

Hashim baisan lokacin da ya miƙe  tsaye ba Yana murmushi yace,"(ALLAH)yayi miki albarka,dayar Dar (ALLAH) zanzabar Miki, miji na gari IN SHA (ALLAH).
Haka dai suka watse kuwa yanufi shashinsa.

Hashim Yana shiga daki yafaɗa kan katifar sa Yana murmushi.... shidai yana ma'makin sanyin hali irin na Maryam, gashi dai shine ya daki ta, Amma Kuma itace ke bashi hakuri da haka haryayi barci.

Washa garin ranar aunty zainab ta tashi da matsanan cin zazabi, sai kuka take jikin ta yayi zafi ganin haka yasa Hashim ya kaita asibiti gojin farko aka tabbatar musu da tana dauke da juna biyu.

Farin ciki agurin Hashim baxai misaltu ba,domin kuwa kudi yarinka rabawa Yan asibiti....  

Farin ciki da Maryam tayi harya wuce wanda Hashim yayi... 

Haka dai suka cigaba da rainun cikin aunty zainab...  hargashi yau tana cikin wata na tara,tun kafin lokacin haihuwar tazo, duk lokacin da Maryam tashiga kasuwa saita sayawa jaririyar da aunty zainab zata haifa kaya tana buye wa.

Ranar juma'a aunty zainab ta haifo ƙyakyawar ɗiyar ta mace,murna agurin Maryam bazai kwatantu ba.... 

kayan da ta dade tana tarawa, tafitar aka sakawa yarinyar.

ranar suna yarinya taci sunan mahaifiyar Hashim Khadija,,,, Amma suna kiran ta, da ikram.

Tunbayan da sukayi arba'in ikram takoma ahannun Maryam, tsakanin ta, da aunty zainab saidai, Tasha nono ta koma hannun Maryam.

Ahaka hartakai shekara daya aduniya, zuwa lokacin babu Wanda tasani sai Maryam, idan Maryam tafita ta rinka kuka kenan har'sai tayi barci.

Tunbayan faruwan wanna lamarin,
Maryam bata Kuma zuwa makaranta ba,,, , hakan ba ƙaramar damin Yusuf
yake ba,, ,gashi baisan gidan suba,
kullum da maganar ta yake kwana
Yake tashi. 


Kana ganin shi,saika tausaya mishi duk yafita daga haiyacin sa,ganin haka yasa mahaifin shi ya zaunar dashi Yana tambayar sa.

"Aiya sanina Yusuf bakataɓa niman wani Abu agurin mu karasa ba,
Idan Kuma akwai kafaɗa min.

Shiru kamar bazai bada amsa ba,ya Dan ɗago Kai yace"a a Abba babu duk abinda nake so Kuna min. 

"To menene damuwar ka, kaga duk yanda karami.

Cikin kuka umman shi take magana"Yusuf kasan cewa duk duniya bamu da wani sauran Jin dadi daya wuce ganin ka cikin walwala da annashuwa,.,,, bayan Kai wa muke dashi? 

" kai kafada mana damuwar ka,ko wace iri ce zamu yi maka;maganim ta.

Shiɗinma kuka yake, saidai mara sauti yace"wallahi umma nasani kuma ina alfahari da kasancewar ku iyaye agare ni. 

Nandai ya labarta musu labarin Maryam ,da rashin sanin inda zaiganta.

Duk kansu sun jajanta mishi da alkawari duk inda maryam take saisun nemeta.

Hakan bakaramar dadi yayiwa Yusuf ba.




Haka dai iyayan yusuf suka cigaba da niman gidan su Maryam,har suka samu ta hannun ƙawar ta ikram.

Tun bayan wannan Rikicin daya faru tsakanin Maryam da Hashim,basu sake samun sabani ba Dan dama shi matsalar shi daya ne,bayaso yaga Maryam da saurayi.... 

to dama Kuma itah wannan ma baya gabanta.

Hashim Yana kwance a falon gidan Mai gadi yazo yace,"yallabai wasu mutane ne suke sallama dakai.

"DAGA inah?

"Ban tanbaye suba...  Amma daga gani mutanan kirkine.

"Niban tambaye ka ko mutanan kirki bane ko na banza,daɗi na dakai saurin yarda da mutani kaje kashigo dasu.

bayan  sunshigo ne 

Hashim ya miƙa musu hannu sukayi musabaha,......  sannan yasa  Maryam takawo musu ruwa.

Tana zuwa ta gaishe su,suka amsa mahaifin yusuf yace,"mun gode Maryam.

Cike da ma'maki afuskar ta, tace bakomai....  saidai kallon mutumin datayi  kamar ta sanshi idan kuwa bashi bane to ita dai ta taba ganin irin  fuskar nan,,,, haka dai ta haura sama cike da mamaki.

Hashim Jin ankira sunan Maryam yasa gabaki daya yarikice,,,,  cikin ranshi kuwa addu'a yake ALLAH yasa ba niman auran ta suka zoba.

maganar da mahaifin yusuf yayine yadawo dashi daga duniyar tunanin da ya shillah.

"Da farko dai  sunana alhaji bala jawabi dankasuwa ne ni,wayannan Kuma kannena ne.... 

"Ok ALLAH sarki Ni sunana Hashim abubakar sadiq,,,  saidai bangane kuba.... abinda ya iya faɗa  kenan,,,  domin shi akage yake yaji abinda yakawo su.

"Ok dama bawani abubane Dan waje nane yaga ƙanwan ka Yana so,shine yace muzo Nima masa izini.

"Cikin ma'maki yace ƙanwata ainah?  "ni banda ƙanwa agarin nan.

"A a bawata bace illah Maryam.

"Wacce Maryam?

"Wacce takawo Mana ruwa idan banyi kuskure ba .

Tsananin fusata Hashim baisan lokacin daya tashi tsaye ba yace,"Dan ALLAH ku tashi kufita tunkan Raina yabaci.

"Mahaifin yusuf yace"malam Hashim niman aure muka zo ba tashin hankali ba.

"Niman auran matar tawa? 

Jin haka yasa gabaki ɗaya  suka rikice....  suka bashi hakuri dacewa,"wallahi Hashim ba musan cewa matar aure bace,ai iya sanin mu  cemana akayi , gidan ya'yan tane Nan ,kayi hakuri.

Da haka harsuka fita agidan suna Kara bashi hakuri.... 



Shikuwa mahaifin yusuf duk inda ranshi yayi dubu to yabace..... shi dama shi kaɗaine abin bazai dameshi ba,hakan Nan yaje ya dauko mahaifin yarinyan nan yumna wacce akabawa yusuf yace baya so,shikuwa yace babu maibyiwa ɗan shi auran dole.....  haka dai suka hakura da magana auran.

Wanda haryanzu tana Nan Batayi aure ba,,,  saboda tsananin son da takewa Yusuf.... 
To gashi yaje yajawowa kanshi abin gori.




*** 
Shikuwa Yusuf tun bayan tafiyar su gidan su Maryam yake ta Addu'a ALLAH yasa kar'asamu wata matsala,,,, haka kawai yaji zuciyar sa tana bugawa.

Mahaifiyar sa tana lura da yadda yake ta kaiwa da komuwa tace,"Yusuf ka kwantar da hankalin ka,da yardar ALLAH babu abinda zaifaru sai alkairi..... 

"ALLAH yasa haka.

"Amin.

Ba"afi mintuna biyar ba yashigo gidan kana ganin sa kasan ransa aɓace yake.... yusuf naganin shi yatashi yanufi gurin shi Abba Kun....Marin da yajine yasashi kasa karasa maganar... 

Kallon abban nashi yake cike da ma'maki,Dan shi a iya sanin shi tunda yake mahaifin shi baitaba zaginshi ba bare akai ga mari.

hajiya Fatima ma haka tsaya tana ma'makin abinda zaisa alhaji ya mari Yusuf,,, saidai tasan koma menene to bakaramar abubane .

Cike da bacin rai ya kalli Yusuf Yana cewa,"ni zaka maida karamin mutum? ni zaka sa ayimin rashin mutun ci? nizaka sa ayimin gori agari?

" to bara kaji,duk abinda kake so ina kukarin Naga namaka,,,  Amma kasani,hakan bazaisa na tsalake iyakokin ALLAH ba ,Don haka daga rana mai kamar ta yau bana son sake jin zancan maryam acikin gidan nan.

"ALLAH yawuci zuciyar ka alhaji wani abinne yafaru?

"Wannan yarinyar ashe matar aure ce Yusuf yasa muje Nima masa auran ta hargidan mijin ta?    "inbadan ALLAH yasa mijin Mai hankali bane da wayasan irin cin mutun cin dazai Mana?

, "sannan nanriga na yanke shawaran zan aurawa Yusuf yarinyar Nan yumna....  "har na biya sadaki Nan da wata ɗaya, Don haka kuje kufara shirye shirye.

Yusuf wanda tunda mahaifin shi yafara magana yake daskare aguri ɗaya, ko ƙwaƙƙorar motsi bai iya ba...  sai asannan yaga wata farar haske ta gift ta idon shi,harya kusa shigewa cikin dakin shi.

juyuwar da mahaifin yusuf zaiyi kawai sai ganin Yusuf yayi yazuɓe kasa  babu alamun Rai atare dashi,dagudu ya nufi gurin shi,batare da bata lokaci ba suka nufi asbiti dashi Rai ahannun ALLAH.

****

Shikuwa Hashim Yana raka su ya dawo cikin gida inbanda zufa babu abinda yake yi ,axuciyarsa Yana tunanin ta inah zai bullowa lamarin nan, haka dai yarinka tufka da warwara har yasamarwa kansa mafita...  

Cikin sauri yaja motar sa yafita ,gudu kawai yakeyi ikon ALLAH ne kawai yakai shi kaduna. Yana Isa maigadi yabude mishi gate yanemi guri yayi parking.

Da sauri maigadin yanufo shi Yana Mai sannu dazuwa,"alhaji ba'ba Yana gida? abinda ya iya  tambayar sa kenan batare daya amsa gaisuwar Saba.

Cikin sauri ya amsa da "eh Amma saidai akwai baki acikin gidan.

Da sallama yashigo gidan,suka amsa alhaji ba'ba cike da ma'maki yace,"Hashim lafiya naganka awannan lokacin?

"Lafiya kalau abba .

"To Naga ɗazu mukayi waya baka faɗamin  zaka zo ba.

Cike da murmushi afuskar sa yace,"kawai dai ina zaune naji inaso naganka shiyasa nazo
.... Amma gobe zan koma IN SHA ALLAH .

Sosai maganar sa ta faranta ran mahaifinsa yace,"To ALLAH ya kaimu,samu guri ka zauna garama da kazo,,,, domin kuwa waƴannan da kake gani sunzo niman auran ƙanwar kane safnah.

Zama sukayi gabaki ɗayan su aka  tattauna zancan auran,,, ,cikin ikon ALLAH aka saka ranar auran Nanda wata daya mai zuwa,,,  Dan angon zaitafi karatu India Kuma so yake yatafi da matar sa.

Bayan tafiyar su alhaji ba'ba yake tambayar Hashim dalilin zuwan shi kaduna da yamma haka....  Don kuwa yasan tabbas haka kawai bazai taso yabar aiyukan Saba .

Cike da natsuwa yace "dama bawani abu bane alkawarin da akayi shekara goma Sha tara nakeson atayar dashi  inaga lokacin yayi.

Saukar da ajiyar zuciya Alhji ba'ba yayi yace,"Hashim kana ganin babu wata matsala?

"cikin ladabi yace, "IN SHA ALLAH.

"Babu damuwa ALLAH ya taimaka....  zanyi magana da Usman naji yanda za'ayi, Danni tunani nake kamar ahada da na safnah aiyi lokaci daya koya kagani?

"Sunkuyar da Kainsa yayi yace "to abba duk yanda kace.

Godiya Hashim yayi sannan yatashi yashige gida,danufin gobe da asuba zai koma Kano.❄❄❄❄❄❄❄

0⃣3⃣


,,,,, Yana shiga cikin gidan....  shashin mahaifiyar sa ya nufa, Yana shiga yayi sallama aka amsa hada ido sukayi cikin sauri ta tashi tanufe shi daniyar rungumi shi dakatar da ita yayi Yana cewa,ke sai yaushe Zaki girma?

Cikin shagwaɓa tashiga bubuga ƙafar ta  akasa tana cewa,"waini ya'ya kwata kwata shekaruna nawa ne?bafa wani yawane dasu ba. 

daga can bayan ta aka Bata amsa dacewar"ashirin.

Cikin sauri suka juya suna kallon ta Dan basusan lokacin da tafito ba.

Rike hannun mahaifiyar su tayi tace,"shekara ashirin shine girma ammi .

Da murmushi Hashim yace,"KARSHAN girma ma Nanda wata ɗaya zamu kaiki dakin mijin ki.

Aikuwa bata bari ya karasa maganar ba da sauri ta tashi ta haye sama adole tana Jin kunya.

Yana murmushi yace,"safnah sarkin rigima.

Lokaci daya suka kalli juna sai alokacin ya gaishi ta,ta amsa cike da Jin dadin ganin sa.

Kana ganin ta basai anfaɗa maka cewa mahaifiyar su bace.... domin kuwa kamar su ta ɓaci.

Doguwa ce fara akallah zata Kai shekara hamsin, saidai inba faɗa maka akayi ba bazaka ce takai hakan ba.

Ciki da murmushi ta kalle shi tace," yasu ikram.

Cikin Jin kunya yace,"lafiya lau suke.

"To ina fatan dai lafiya na ganka babu ko sanarwa.

"Lafiya lau.

Haka dai sukayi ta hira...  asannan ne yafaɗa mata abinda yakawo shi da yanda sukayi da alhaji ba'ba.

Hajiya Khadija wacce suke Kira da ummi ta ɗanyi murmushi tace,"ALLAH yasa haka shine mafi alkairi,Dan Nima banida wani buri daya wuce Naga ka ciki alkawarin da ka dauka shekara goma shi tara baya.

Haka dai suka yita hira har Hashim yafara hamma ganin haka yasa ummi tace,"katashi kashiga ciki ka huta tunda sammako zakayi.

Babu musu yatashi yashiga cikin.





****
Yusuf kuwa tunbayan sumar da yayi har yanzu bai dawo cikin haiyacin saba,likitocin sunfi karfin awa hudu akansa kafin suka samu ya sauko.

Shikuwa alhaji bala wato mahaifin yusuf banda kaiwa da komuwa babu abinda yake...  Ahaka har likitan yafito wanda shine babba cikin likitocin.

harya iso gurin alhaji baisan ya zoba Dan yayi zurfi cikin tunani.

Likitan ne yataɓa shi,,  saukar da wata ajiyar zuciya yace,"likita ina fatan anci nasara?

girgiza masa Kai yayi yace,"alhamdulillah mun samu ya dawo cikin hai yacinsa,,,  saidai munyi masa allurar barci,Nanda awa ɗaya dayar dar ALLAH zai farfaɗo.

Alhamdulillah yace sannan. Likitan yasake cewa,"saidai wani hanzari ba gudu ba,yusuf yakamu da buguwar zuciya,Wanda hakan nada nasaba da firgita da yayi haryayi sanadiyar sakashi cikin wannan halin.

"ina mai shawar tarbku da kunema Masa duk wani abinda kukasan zai sakashi cikin farin ciki,ta haka ne kawai zai'iya samun sauki cikin kankanin lokaci .

Batare da ya tsaya Jin me alhaji zai faɗa  ba Kai tsaye yawuce office dinsa danganawa da sauran marasa lafiyar dake jiran sa.

Alhaji kuwa tunda likitan ya fara magana banda salati da sallalami dayake yi azuciyar sa babu abinda yakeyi.

shiga cikin ɗakin Yayi kallon ɗaya  yayi masa ya ɗauke  Kai, domin tausayi da dana sani ne suka mamayi shi,,,, sanin cewar balale bana yasamu abinda yake so ba tunda ta riga tayi aure.

Haka yayita tufka da warwara.... amma ina babu mafita.


===========
Hashim kuwa Yana gama Karin kumallo ya lula garin kano...  Yana sauka office yawuce dan yabar aiki dayawa akasa.

Yana shiga yafara aiki ba'akama hannun yaro cikin kankanin lokaci ya kammala, yatashi yanufi gida.
Yana isah yayi parking daidai lokacin Maryam zata raka ƙawayan ta da suka zo dubata ko lafiya kwana biyu bata zuwa makaranta.

Yana ganinsu ya ɗauke Kai kamar baima gansu ba,daya daga ciki tace,"Ke maryam wannan wani handsome guy ne haka?  Kai gaskiya ya haɗu who is he?

Cike da ma'makin tambayar tace,"ya'ya nane wani abunne? 

Maimuna ce ta sake jefo mata wata tabayar tace,"uwan ku ɗaya ubanku ɗaya?

Cikin kusawa da tambayar tace," eh.

Da sauri maimuna ta riƙe  hannun ta cikin sigar roko tace,"ki taimakeni Dan ALLAH Maryam,,,  wallahi bantaba ganin mutum Mai kyaun ya'yannan naki ba, ki taimaka kiban number shi gaskiya inah sonshi.

Kallo kawai Maryam ta bita dashi tana ma'maki yanda mata keson ya Hashim.

ita dai Kam koda ba ya'yan ta bane babu abinda zaisa taso mutum Mai hali irin wannan, ace mutum kullum cikin fushi babu sakin fuska gaskiya itah dai bata ga abinso anan ba.

Daidai lokacin yaƙaraso gurin su da sauri ta suguna ta gaishi shi,,, ganin haka yasa sauran ƙawayan ma sukayi yanda tayi cikin sakin fuska yace."

"maryam suwaye wa'yannan ?

Baki na rawa tace ,"class mate Dina ne sunzo duba nine sunga kwana biyu shiru ban zoba,  takarashi maganar kamar zatayi kuka.

Sai'asannan ya dube su ɗaya bayan ɗaya yace,"nagode da nuna kulawar ku akan rashin ganin ƙanwata daba kuyi ba harkuka zo duba ta.
Suka amsa da babu komai.

saka hannu yayi a aljihunsa yazaro kudin da baima San adadin suba yamika wa maimuna kasancewar itace akusa dashi.

ganin maimuna bata amshi kudin bane yasa Maryam kallon ta...  cikin sauri ta Dan daki bayan ta,sai asannan maimuna ta dawo cikin haiyacin ta Dan tun lokacin da yafara magana take kallon shi har ya miƙa mata kudi bata sani ba, gabaki ɗaya yatafi da hankalin ta akunyace ta karbi kudin tana wani sun sunkuyar dakai .

Shikuwa ma'maki ta bashi to metake kallo haka? tambayar dayayi wa kansa kenan yashige cikin gida.

Ita kuwa Maryam haushi maimuna tabata Dan bataso taga mata marasa aji haushi suke bata ba kanɗan ba.

sallama sukayi bayan Maryam ta Bata number Hashim, cikin zuciyar ta kuwa babu komai Dan sanin hali irin na Hashim ne yatabbatar mata da ko takira shi bai Zama dole ya dauka ba.

harzata shige ciki Taji ana kwalla mata Kira daga bakin geta ɗin gidan....  juyowar daza tayi ta hangi ikram tana ƙoƙarin tsalake titin zuwa kofan gidan,,,  haka tasaya tana jiran ta harta tsallako.

Tana zuwa ko gaisawa basuyi ba tafara masifa inda take shiga batanan take fita ba ,wani Karin abin ma'maki ita ke faɗa  Kuma ita ke kuka,Saida ta gama sannan Maryam tace,"kin gama?

Ikram ta kalle ta ciki da ma'maki tace,"o haka ma Zaki ci ko?

murmushi maryam tayi tace ,"mezance?" aiya sanina banyi miki komai ba ikram, Amma kinzo kin rufini da faɗa  kamar wata 'yarki .

Cikin bacin Rai tace,"in Ni baki min komai ba aikin yiwa Yusuf,,  tunda gashi kwance Rai ahannun ALLAH kuma duk saboda ke .

Affrigice tace,"nikuma menayi wa yusuf?

Ikram juyawa kawai tayi zata tafi dangani take kamar Raina mata hankali Maryam keson yi.

Cikin sauri Maryam tashiga gabanta zuwa yanzu hawaye yagama wanke mata fuska tace,"Dan ALLAH karki tafi ki barni bayan kin sakani cikin duhu,aiya sanina tunranar da ya Hashim yazo makaran ta yasamemu tare ya hanani zuwa makaranta bansake Jin Koda labarin yusuf ba.

Ganin dagaske Maryam batasan abinda yafaru ba,,,  yasa ikram ta labarta mata duk abinda yafaru tun bayan barinta makaranta da zuwan da iyayan Yusuf sukayi jiya da kaishi asibita Wanda ahalin yanzu babu Wanda yasan halin da yake ciki.

Aikuwa ikram na gama faɗa  mata da gudu tashige gida tana kuka,
Tana shiga daidai lokacin Hashim da zainab suna zaune wuce su tayi ta nufi dakin ta tasa key.

Da sauri Hashim yatashi Yana Kiran ta Amma ko juyowa Bata yiba....❄❄❄❄❄❄❄❄❄

4⃣


,,,,,, Cikin sauri Hashim ya biyu ta har kofar ɗakin,da karfi yake buga kofar haɗe  da kiran sunan ta cewa yake,"Maryam ki bude kofar na. 

ganin batada niyar budewa ne yasa yaje ya dauko ragowar keys din gidan,...  anyi sa'a kuwa Dan bayan ta kolle kofar bata bar key ajiki ba cirewa tayi,aikuwa Yana sawa kofar ta buɗe  cikin sauri suka shiga dakin.

aunty zainab ce tafara kaiwa inda take cikin kulawa ta fara rarashinta,....  Dan ita duk tunanin ta rashin zuwan ta makaranta ne yasaka ta kuka,shikuma abangaran Hashim tunanin Shi kudai taji maganar alkawarin dayake niyar sauke wane yasaka ta kuka?, "Indai haka ne kuwa saidai tayi.... " Don duk duniyar nan banda ALLAH da mahaifinsa babu mahalukin daya isa ya Hana shi. 


Cikin rarrashi Aunty zainab tace,"kiyi hakuri kinji Maryam ki rungumi kaddarar ki haka ALLAH yaso.... ,idan Kuma da rabon zaki cigaba da karatun sai kiga idan kikayi aure mijin ki ya mayar da ke,Dan Haka ki daina kuka kinji ƙanwata .

Taso ta faɗawa  aunty zainab abinda yake faruwa.... , Amma sai tayi tunanin Hashim zaiji babu dadi.....  sannan ga alkawarin da ta dauka dacewa shizai zaba mata miji,tuna Haka yasata kara fashewa da wan irin matsanan cin kuka.

shidai Hashim tunda yaga bata ce komai ba yayi ficewar shi tunda bazatayi magana ba.

Saida tayi mai isar ta sannan ta bawa kanta hakuri dayiwa kanta tambaya Dan ita dai tasan cewa batajin son Yusuf,to meyasa ta kuka?wani bangare nazuciyar ta Kuma yace,"tausayin sa kike.

Nandai tayi na'am da cewa tausayi yake bata, nandanan ta yanke shawara ko ta halin ya'ya sai taje ta duba Yusuf.

*****

Shikuwa Yusuf Saida yayi awanni shida sannan ya farfado, kuda ya farfado da sunan ta abakin shi yatashi,saida yagama duba dakin gaba daya babu Maryam, sai mahaifin sa.

Nan danan yafara bubuga kanshi abangon ɗakin Yana sambatu Yana cewa,"gwara mana mutu na huta Dan ina da tabbacin bazan iya rayuwa babu ke ba Maryam.

Ganin haka yasa mahaifin sa fita da sauri..... ba'afi mintina biyu ba suka dawo tare da likitan,izuwa lokacin kuwa jini ya gama wanke mishi fuska ,Koda akazo mishi allura saida akakai ruwa Rana sannan aka samu akamishi.

aikuwa ko kwakwarar mintina biyu ba ba'ayi ba wata WAHALlLIYAR barci ta dauke shi.

Ganin haka yasa likitan yacewa alhaji ya biyo shi dan jin Karin bayani da niman mafita.

Suna shiga likitan yayi mishi nuni da kujerar dake fuskan tarshi sannan yace,"

, "Alhaji wato ɗanka Yusuf yakamu da ciwon zuciya kamar yanda nafaɗa maka da farko.... ,Kuma cutar tayi mishi ilah sosai.....  domin ahalin da ake ciki Yana mataki na karshe....  ina mai tabbatar maka da cewa idan harba'a samar mishi da abinda yakae da muradi ba ,to gaskiya da matsala Dan komai na iya faruwa.

Da sauri alhaji yatashi yarike hannun likitan Yana kuka kamar karamin yaro yace,"Dan ALLAH likita karka bari wani Abu yasami ɗana.....  wallahi shikadai kawai ALLAH ya 
Mallaka min, duk duniyan nan banda ALLAH da MANZON sa banida abinda nake so kamar sa likita zan biya ka kunawane.

haka dai yayita sambatu ganin Haka ne yasa likitan saurin zaunar dashi tareda kara kwantar mishi da hankali,..... ganin yafara komawa daidai yasa likitan yafara tambayar sa yace.

"Amma alhaji shikuwa meyanema yarasa dahar yayi sanadiyar faɗawar sa cikin wannan mumunar hali?

Batare da bata lokaci ba alhaji ya zai yana mishi duk abinda ke faruwa.

Tsananin tausayi saida shima yayi yar hawaye, " sannan yace,"IN SHA ALLAH zanyi iya bakin ƙokari na.

Ahaka alhaji yayi mishi sallama yanufi dakin Yusuf.

S

shikuwa  mahaifin yumna tsabar murna ko sallama bai yiba Yana shigowa yafara kollawa matar sa kira ,kana ganin ta kasan arazane take tana zuwa tace," alhaji lafiya kuwa?

Cike da zumudin son yafaɗa mata labarin yake tambayar ta yace"ke ina yumna?

Da sauri ta amsa tace,"tana ciki.

"Jeki ki kira ta.

Cikin sauri ta nufi hanyar ɗakin yumna wace tunda aka tafi nemawa Yusuf auran Maryam ta kulle kanta cikin daki ,banda kuka babu abinda takeyi mahaifiyar ta babu irin magiyar da bata mata ba....  amma haka tayi buris da ita.

Tana zuwa murda kofar kawai tayi ta ganta abude da sauri ta karasa cikin ɗakin,ganin halin da take ciki bakaramar tayar mata da hankali yayi ba,cikin sauri takaraso inda take zaune tace,"haba yumna mekike son Zama ne iye?akan namiji kika mayar da kanka haka? haba yunma.

Cikin kuka tace ,"mummy narasa shi ko?
Shikenan KARSHAN rayuwa ta tazo Dan wallahi banzan iya rayuwa babu Yusuf ba.

Daga can bayan su,sukaji mahaifin ta Yana cewa,"ki kwantar da hankalin ki Yusuf yarigada yazama naki shalele na.

Cikin sauri tazo dab dashi ta rike hannun shi tana cewa,"daddy Dan ALLAH karka min haka karka faɗamin  abinda ba haka bane.....  ,wallahi duk da nasan cewa bazan iya rayuwa babu Yusuf ba,Amma bazan lamunci Amin karya akan Yusuf b...  

Jin wata zafafan saukar mari fuskar tane yasa ta kasa karasa maganar ta, cikin sauri ta ɗago  ido tana kallon mahaifin nata da ma'maki afuskar ta, Dan tunda ta taso faɗa Mai karfi bai....  taba hadasu ba..... ,Amma yau ta sanadiyar Yusuf mahaifin ta har Marin ta yayi.

Cikin fushi yanuna ta da hannu Yana cewa," duk son danake miki bazan laminci raini daga gurin ki ba,harni ni zanfada miki magana ki karyata?

nan danan ya fito da kudin sadakin da mahaifin yusuf yabashi ya watso mata afuska yace,"wannan itace kudin sadakin ki.

Saboda tsanin ma'maki kasa motsi tayi Dan ko amafarki bata taba tunanin ganin Rana irin wannan ba.

Mahaifiyar ta nema tayi karfin halin tambayar shi yanda akayi hakan ta kasance.

Nandanan yafada mata duk yanda akayi.

Da gudu yunma ta rungumi mahaifin nata tana kukan farin ciki.

*****
Haka dai Maryam tayi ta tufka da warwara tana niman hanyar da zata samu tafita taje duba Yusuf..... haka dai harta samu mafita,.... tashi tayi tashiga wanka bayan tagama tayi Shirin ta tsaf,tana fituwa falo ta tarar da aunty zainab azaune tana yiwa ikram kitso.

Fuskarta ɗauke  da murmushi Aunty zainab take tambayar ta tace,"ƙanwata kinga yanda kikayi kyau kuwa?tamkar wata amarya kai wallahi kinma fi wata amaryar kyau.

Murmushi kawai tayi tace,"kai aunty banda sankai.... ,nidai bama wannan ba Aunty gidan su ikram nakeson zuwa nadubo ta.

Haka kawai aunty zainab ta sinci kanta da tsoron fitar da Maryam zatayi gashi Hashim yakusa dawowa tace,"Maryam ba Hana ki fita zanyi ba,..... saidai kisani Hashim yakusa dawowa Dan Haka Dan ALLAH karki zauna kinji .

Cikin sauri ta miƙe tana cewa,"dayar dar ALLAH bazan daɗe ba.

Tana fita ta kira ikram take tambayar ta inda asibitin yake ba'afi mintina biyar ba taturo mata da address din asibitin, nandanan tashiga motarta tayi gaba.

*****

Ita kuwa yumna sai aranan ake faɗa mata rashin lafiyar Yusuf.
Aikuwa babu shiri ta nufi asbiti .

Maryam tana parking motarta adaidai lokacin itama yunma tayi parking nata.

0⃣5⃣

,,,,,,, Yumna ce tafara shiga ɗakin kasancewar Maryam ta tsaya gaisawa da yan makaran tarsu.

yumna tana shiga da gudu ta faɗa  jikin mummyn nashi ta na kuka,"mummy ki taimake ni banaso komai ya samu amirul'Qalb,mummy idan wani Abu yasame shi kisani wallahi bazan iya rayuwa b....rufe mata baki da mummy tayine ya hanata karasa maganar.

hakan Kuma yayi daidai da shigowar Maryam tsaf taji abinda yumna take faɗa , ji tayi kamar ta Koma,,,  Amma ina sunriga da sunganta,daidaita natsuwar ta tayi sannan ta karasa da sallama abakin ta. 

mummy ce kawai ta amsa ita kuwa yumna babu abinda takeyi sai kuka.

Tsugunnawa tayi harkasa ta gaishe ta sannan tayi mata yamai jiki, ta samu kujerar dake gurin ta zauna tana kallon yanda Yusuf ke sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Ɗago  Kai Mummy tayi ta ɗankali Maryam tace, "baiwar ALLAH gashi har yanzu bai farka ba yau kusan kwana shida kenan baisan inda yake ba,amma idan ALLAH yasa ya farka wa za'acemasa?

Maryam wace tuni hawaye yagama wanke mata fuska tace"Maryam.

da sauri mummy ta karaso inda Maryam take zaune tace,"wakika ce?

Da ɗan tsoro afuskar ta ganin yanda take kallon ta tace,"Mar ..yam.

Aibata karasa maganar ba ta fisgo ta saukar mari da yumna taji ne yasa ta ɗagowa cikin sauri tana yiwa mahaifiyar Yusuf wace suke kira mummy kallo cike da ma'maki Dan kwatakwata bata maji abinda suke faɗa  ba.

Ita kuwa Maryam wace gabaki ɗaya hawaye yagama wanke mata fuska jikinta kuwa har rawa take tace, "Mena miki? 

sake kallon mummy tayi cike da fusata tace,"maza ki gaggauta barin asibitin Nan kafin ranki yayi mumunar bace,watau kinji anci Yana asibiti Rai ahannun ALLAH shiyasa kika dibi jiki kikanzo ki karasa shi ko?

"to ta ALLAH bataki ba,shaidaniya, to inba shedanci ba mezaisa matar aure ta rinka kula samari a waje? bayan kinsan kina da aure, ganin da tayi Maryam batada niyar fita shiya kara harzuka ta tace,"oo bazaki fita ba kenan to kijira ni.

Jikin ta har'rawa yake da sauri tace,"wallahi umma banda aure hasalima bantaba yin aure ba, ki yarda dani umma shidin yakasance ya'yana ne tamkar uba agare ni duk wani hukunci ahannun shi suk...

Kiran sunan ta dayayi ne yasa ta kasa karasa maganar, da sauri suka nufi gadon da yake kwance,kana ganin shi kasan ya jigata Dan gabaki ɗaya kaman nin sa sun sauya gaba daya idonuwan slsuma  sun sauya launi.

Maryam ce tafara magana cikin muryar kuka tace,"ya jikin naka Yusuf?

Nod da Kai kawai yayi

"tace ka gafartamin Yusuf wallahi bansan duk abinda ke faruwa ba sai jiya da ikram ta zo gida take sanar dani.

Girgiza mata kai yayi shidinma hawaye yakeyi yace,"bakida laifi Maryam laifina ne, kasancewar bantaba baiyana soyaya ta agare ki ba,Amma kisani bazan taba daina sonki ba,sannan naji kina cewa keba matar aure bace,Dan ALLAH karki yi karya don kina tunanin itace hanyar da zaki ceto rayuwa ta,ki tafi ki barni...  domin yin karyar itace zata kara karfin cutar Alokacin Dana tabbatar karyace kikayi.

Cikin kuka ta ɗago kai tace,"ka yarda dani Yusuf wallahi bantaba yin aure ba.

Cikin sauri ya tashi daga kwancan da yake ya zauna kamar bashi bane Yusuf dinda yayi kwana da kwanaki ana nimar yanda za'a ceto rayuwar saba yace,"kina sona Maryam zaki aure ni?

Lokaci daya yajero tambayar Yana Mai kallon cikin idon ta.

Ita kuwa sunkuyar da kai tayi,,, sannan tace," bantaba Jin bana sonka araina ba, Kuma bazance nataba jin sonka ba,saidai kasani tun lokacin da labarin rashin lafiyar ka ta riskeni dakuma dalilin Rashin lafiyar taka gabaki ɗa kasa sukuni nayi sai alokacin na tabbatar da cewa soyayar kata dade tana dawainiya dani.

"maganar aure kuwa baxan iya cema ka komai ayanzu ba ,domin bantaba yanke wa kaina hukunci ba. 

Nannauyar ajiyar zuciyar da mummy ta sauke shiya tunatar da su wanzuwar ta agurin, cikin sauri Maryam ta nufi kofar dakin daniyar fita .

cikin sauri mummy tace,"ina Kuma zaki tafi Maryam Dan ALLAH ki yafimin na yanke miki hukunci batari da bincike ba.

da sauri Maryam ta rike hannun ta tace,"umma ki daina bani hakuri nice nayi muku laifi nice sanadiyar  shigarki  cikin wannan halin, nasan cewa baki Yi barci ba, kin kasance cikin damuwa na tsawan kwana ki duk asanadiya ta ,kiyi hakuri kinji umma.

murmushi kawai tayi tana Jin kaunar yarinyar acikin ranta.

Itah kuwa yumna kasa motsi tayi Dan gabaki daya babu inda zata iya motsawa ajikin ta, bakaramar namijin kokari tayi ba hartasamu damar Jan jiki daniyar barin dakin, tambayar da Maryam tayi ne yasa ta tsaya cek. 

Maryam tace," mummy kanwar Yusuf ce ?

tayi maganar tana nuni da yumna.

Sai alokacin mummy ta ji duk hankali ta yatashi Dan ita tsakani da ALLAH tamance da wanzuwar yumna agurin.

yumna kuwa dama ta tsayane dantaji amsar da mummy zata bawa Maryam,jin mummy tayi shiru yasa tayi saurin bude kofar tafita da gudu tana wani irin kuka mai tsuma zuciyar duk wani mai sauraron ta.

Bayan fitar ta aka rasa wanda zaiyi wa wani magana acikin su, domin kuwa kowa da abinda yake aiyanawa aranshi, bangaran Maryam tunani biyu ke kaiwa da komuwa na farko shine, wacece yumna? kanwar Yusuf ce  ko kuwa?

Ita kuwa mummy tsoron halin da yumna zata shiga take don tasan irin son da takewa gudan jinin nata.

Jin shiru yayi yawa yasa Yusuf yadan kalli Maryam cikin  tsokana yace,"mairo dare fa yanayi mijinki bazai nemeki ba?

Maimakon yaga ta dan zumbura baki kamar yanda tasa ba masa amakaranta idan yakirata da mairo,kawai sai gani yayi ta tashi tsaya afirgice muryar ta takoma kalar kuka tace,"zantafi Yusuf ALLAH yakara sauki.

bata jira jinme zaice ba tafita da sauri tana guge hawaye sai asannan ta Dan duba wayar ta ganin miss call din aunty zainab kusan 20miss call shiya kara tsananta kukan da takeyi tana karasa gurin da tayi parking ɗin motarta tasa kye zata bude taji anrike mata hannun juyawa tayi da sauri mummunar ganin datayi ne yasata sumewa saboda tsabar firgita.

6⃣


" *Tolerance is The Highest Degree of your  Strength, And desire to  take Revenge is*
*The First Sign of Weakness. So no time for that🙅‍♀️*





Saurin riƙota yayi sannan ya buɗe  marfin motar ya kwantar da ita,fitowa yayi yanufi motarsa ya dauko ruwa ya zuba ahannun sa,,,,  sannan ya shafa mata afuska.

ahankali take boɗe idonta harta buɗe  su gabaki ɗaya.... suna haɗa  ido kuwa ta fara hawaye tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH kayi hakuri ya'ya wallahi... tsawar da ya daka mata shiya hanata ƙarasa maganar,,,,  sannan yafara magana cikin sigar lallashi,"Maryam ni zaki watsawa kasa a ido?ni zaki mayar karamin mutum a idon duniya? Bai jira jin meizata ceba ya zagaya dayan bangaran yashiga.

Tunda suka fara tafiya take kuka harsuka karasa gida shikuwa jin kukan nata yake harcikin zuciyar sa... 
Suna isah Mai gadi yabude masa get ya samu guri yayi parking cikin sauri ya sauka yanufi cikin gidan.

ita kuwa Saida tayi kuka mai isar ta sannan tafito jan jikin ta kawai takeyi, domin duk jikin ta yayi sanyi zuciyar ta ciki take tafda dana sani Dan duk duniya bataso taga bacin ran Hashim.

Tana shiga tasame aunty zainab azaune kallo ɗaya zaka mata ka tabbatar da taci kuka Dan idonwan ta har sun danyi tudu.

Cikin sauri ta nufita tafaɗa jikin ta tana kuka ita kuwa aunty zainab zameta tayi daga jikin ta tana cewa," kin kyauta Maryam tunda nake da Hashim bai taɓa barazanar rabuwa dani ba sai akanki,,  kin kyauta cikin sauri tabar gurin tana share hawaye.

Maryam kuwa gabaki daya ji tayi hawayen fuskar ta ta kafe Dan maganar ta girgiza ta ba kaɗan  ba, Haka dai ta tashi ta nufi ɗakin ta tana shiga tanufi toilet ta dauro alwala...  Saida ta Yi ramakon sallulin da akebinta sannna ta haye gado ba'afi mintina biyar da kwanciyar ta ba wata WAHALALLIYAR barci yayi awangaba da itah.

Shikuwa Hashim tunda yashiga ɗakin shi babu abinda yakeyi sai safa da marwa,kallo ɗaya kawai zaka mishi kasan ran Maza yabaci,,,  kome ya tuna da sauri ya fiddo da wayar sa yayi dailing number alhaji ba'ba ringing biyu aka dauka,gaisawa sukayi cikin girmamawa sannan ya ɗan rausayar da Kai kamar Yana ganin shi yace,"baba dama nakira ne naji yanda ake ciki".

Saida akayi mintina biyu kafin ayi magana daga dayan bangaran alhaji ba'ba yace,"ka kwantar da hankalin ka Hashim babu Wanda zai hanaka ciki alkawarin da ka dauka,saidai muna guje maka abinda zai faru nan gaba duk da kasancewar ba baki nake maka ba,,, amma to ALLAH yazabar Mana abinda yafi Zama alkairi,munriga da mun yanke maganar auran za'a hada ayi gabaki ɗaya Dana safnah IN SHA ALLAH.

Saida ya sauke wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya Wanda saida alhaji ba'ba yaji karar ta sannan yace,"ALLAH yasaka da alkairi yasa ayi damu", amsawa yayi da ameen sannan yace,"Hashim Ina fatan babu wata damuwa ko?

Cikin sauri Hashim yace,"akwai Amma bamai yawa ba.

"To inah sauraron ka.

Jinayi Y'a fara magana ciki ciki hakan yasa naga yakamata nafita nabawa daddy and his son guri suyi maganar su ta sirri hahahaha lol 😜😜😜

=========

Shikuwa Yusuf tun bayan fitar Maryam daga ɗakin yaketa tufka da warwara Dan shi so yake yagano abinda yasa Maryam dinsa zubda hawaye har tafita batare da sunyi sallamar kirki ba,wata zuciyar take sanar dashi cewan sunan daya kira ta dashi ne,wata kuma  cewa take dalilin kukun ta yanada nasa ba dacewa mijinta zaineme ta ne dayayi.

Haka zuciyar sa tafi aminta Dana biyun aikuwa hakan tasa ya Rikici saidai asallame shi dan shiya rigada ya warke ana cikin Haka mahaifin shi yashigo ganin Yusuf atsaye da kafafunshi shiyafi kumai daure Mai kai,,,, dan bai masan da yusuf ya warke ba.

Shi kuwa Yusuf Yana ganin mahaifin sa da sauri ya nufi bakin kofar inda mahaifin shi yake tsaya Yana zuwa rungume daddy yayi da karfi Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya yace,"Dan ALLAH daddy kayi wa likita magana ya sallami ni Nina warke daddy bazan zauna ba,,,,  yakara ke maganar cikin shagwaba.

Daddy magabaki ɗaya rasa ta inda zai fara magana yayi, saida suka kusa mintina biyar ahaka shidinma Yusuf baice komai ba Yana rungume ajikin daddy, ahankali daddy ya ɗago  fuskar sa sannan yace,"son.
Kai kawai Yusuf ya ɗaga sannan daddy ya cigaba da magana cikin sigar lallashi yace.

"okay babu matsala tunda ka warke me kuma zamutsaya yi a asibitin Nan? Amma kayi hakuri zuwa gobe kaga yanzu dari yayi kaji my one and only son.

Saida Yusuf ya ɗaga kai ya kalli mahafin sa da nufin kara yi masa magiya kallo daya yayi mishi ya sunkuyar da kai Dan gabakida mahaifin shi ya rame cikin kwanaki shida dayayi kamar yanda mummy sa take bashi labari,,,, fashewa yayi da wani irin kuka mai tsuma zuciyar duk wani mai sauraron ta tsugunnawa yayi harkasa yace.


"kuyi hakuri daddy nasan cewa banyi muku adalci ba Amma Ina mai niman yafiyar ku,, da sauri daddy ya ɗago shi ya rungume shi Yana shafa bayan sa,,,  kamar karamin yaro sannan yace,"ni bakamin komai ba son na yafa maka. 

Haka dai sukayi hira har mummy ta bashi labarin zuwan Maryam sosai labarin ya girgiza shi Dan yanda Hashim yayi musu magana awancan ranar babu mahalunkin da zaice ba gaskiya bane,,,,, saidai ya daukarwa kansa alkawarin sai ya sake bincike akan lamarin.

=====
Itah kuwa yumna ma'adanar motocin asibitin ta nufa ko ganin inda take saka kafa batayi tana zuwa ta buɗe  motar ta tashiga da gudu tabar harabar asibitin,Haka tayi ta gudu akan hanya saura kadan ta bigi wata babbar motar da tashigo batare da dubawa ba.

ikon ALLAH ne kawai ya kaita gida tana shiga tasami umman ta azaune tana kallon  tashar AREWA 24 batabi ta kantaba ,wucewa tayi hanyar kitchen ita kuwa umma tana ganin yanda yumna tashigo tasan akwai matsala shiyasa itama tayi saurin miƙewa  ta bi bayan ta.

Tana shiga kitchen din wuƙa ta dauka karar da umma ta jine yasata karasa kitchen din da gudu Dan ganin abinda ke faruwa.

*SAFNAH ALIYU JAWABI* 

                         
                                             

0⃣7⃣

*Bismillahi rahamanir  Rahim*







,,,,,, Da gudu tafita tana ihun Kiran drive...  da sauri yazo aka sakata cikin mota.  gudu yake bana wasa ba sakamakon jinin dake zuba ajikin ta,,  suna isa asibitin aka shigar da ita d
ɗakin taimakon gaggawa,,  saida likitocin suka dauke tsawan awa biyu akanta,,  sannan aka samu akayi mata ɗinki.. 

likitan Yana fitowa cikin sauri umma ta nufi gurin shi tana tambayar sa jikin yumna.

Saida yayi ajiyar zuciya sannan yace"garin yaya haka takasance hajiya?

Kuka mai karfine ya kubbuce mata kafin daga bisani tayi shiru,,,  sannan tafara bashi labari yanda abin yakasance.

Wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan yayi hamdala ga ALLAH azuciyar sa .domin kuwa tsawan shekaru da dama ya dauka Yana dakon soyayar ta acikin zuciyar sa Dan Haka bazai bari wani banza yarusa Masa tanadin daya daɗe yanayi ba.

Jin shirun yayi yawa yasa umma kara fashewa da kuka Dan duk tunanin ta yumna mutuwa tayi, karar kukan da umma keyi shiya dawo dashi daga duniyar tunanin daya lula cikin sauri yace"hajiya bafa mutuwa tayi ba tana raye saidai tana bukatar Karin jini.

Cikin sauri umma ta ciro wayar ta takira daddy Dan gabaki ɗaya ta mance bata kirashi ta faɗa masa abinda ke faruwa ba.

Ba'afi mintina talatin ba daddy yashigo asibitin kana ganin sa zakasan ba'acikin haiyacin sa Yake ba..  Dan gabaki daya kaman ninsa sun canza,yana zuwa yanufi office din likitan Yana shiga likitan yayi mishi bayanin yanda ake ciki Basu tsaya bata lokaci ba suka nufi dakin lab.

anyi sa'a domin group din jinin su ɗaya ne,,, aka diba suka koma dakin da yumna take kwance aka saka mata jinin.

****
Itah kuwa Maryam bata farkaba Saida taji Kiran sallah asuba sannan ta tashi ta nufi hanyar toilet rike da Kai domin wani irin ciwon kaine  ta tashi dashi.

adaddafe tashiga toilet ta dauro alwala tazo ta gabatar da sallah tana idarwa ta nufi kangado ta kwanta Dan zuwa yanzu Bata ma ganin gaban ta Haka dai ta kwanta tana cikin barci taji anbude kofar dakin.

Ɗagowa tayi da mamaki Dan aiya sanin ta Bata buɗe kofar dakin ba to ta yaya aka buɗe?  to ko dai tacire key dinne sai asannan ta tuno da cewa tacire key din, ƙara ɗago Kai tayi wazata gani Hashim ne da Aunty Zainab duk kansu idan ka duba zaka fahimci babu annuri ko kaɗan  akan fuskokin su saka makon abinda ya faru.

*Flash back*
Zainab tuda ta tashi take ta tunani abinda zatayiwa mijin ta don ta faranta mishi rai Dan ita dai bazata iyah da bacin rain Hashim ba,tsaki tayi lokacin da ta tuna Mai ta yata aikin ma fushi take da itah.

samun guri tayi ta zauna Dan itadai Banda ruwan tea da taliya bata iyah dafa komai ba duk sauran akin gidan Maryam keyi .

tana cikin tunani yanda zatayi taji ikram tana tsallah kuka cikin sauri taje dantasan babu Maitaya ta,,  tana zuwa ta samu ikram duk tayi Kashi tabata guri,,,  tsaki tayi sannan ta karasa shiga dakin, toilet ta shiga ta wanke ta tana yatsina fuska adole batason wanke wa yara kashi,tana gamawa cikin sauri ta nufi dakin Hashim.

tana zuwa tasame shi Yana barci sai murmushi yake 
Cikin sauri ta zo dafda shi tana cewa"daddy ikram Dan ALLAH katashi kasan fa ni babu abinda na iyah dafawa Kuma yunwa nakeji gashi Maryam taki fitowa kabani sauran keys dake gurin ka.

Shikuwa jin maganar yayi tamkar amafarki shiyasa yakara juyawa yayi kwanciyar sa,ta ɓashi tayi tana sake jaddada mishi maganar.
da sauri yatashi Yana sake tambayar ta lafiyar Maryam din kalau kuwa? Cikin rashin damuwa ta bashi amsa,"nidai ban sani ba Dan ALLAH muje yunwa nakeji.

fita tayi Yana binta abaya sai murmushi yake Dan mafarkin dayayi idan zaizama gaskiya to shidai yasan bashi da wata sauran damuwa arayuwa .
*Back to STORY*

Kara lumshe ido tayi Dan ganin su take suna rabuwa gida biyu,shikuwa Hashim ganin ta Haka bakaramar tayar maishi da hankali yayi ba..  Dan kayan barci ne ajikin ta cikin sauri yajuya yafara magana,"meke damunki? 

Tsawan daya daka mata shiyasa ta bude baki da kyar hawaye na zuba akan fuskar ta tace"wallahi ya'ya bakin fitowa nayi ba kainane ke...tun kafin takarasa maganar tuni Hashim harya kai inda take hannun yasa ya tabi kanta zafi yaji Wanda saida yayi saurin jenye hannun shi, juyawa yayi ya umarci aunty zainab data shirya ta su tafi asibiti.

ita kuwa zainab Banda haushi babu abinda ya cika ta Dan itah kadai tasan irin yunwar da take ji,zuwa tayi dafda Maryam ta rike mata hannun harta sauke kafarta daya,Amma inah jiri ya kwashi ta takoma cikin ƙankanin lokaci maryam ta fita daga haiyacin ta, daukar ta Hashim yayi *in brid style* duk jikin shi rawa yake sakamakon shook dayaji lokacin da ya hada jikin shi da nata.

gurin parking yanufa da sauri ya bude motar yasaka ta fizgar motar yayi daidai lokacin aunty zainab ta fito ganin yatafi yasata sauke ajiyar zuciya sannna tace.

, *nikam Naga takaina inbadan nasan cewa uwa daya uba daya suka fito  ba, da wallahi babu abinda zai hanani zargin Hashim son Maryam yake, saidai nasan babu aure atsakaninsu har abadan .

shigewa gida tayi abinta Dan samawa kanta abinda zata ci.
******
Shikuwa Hashim Banda gudu babu abinda yakeyi sauran kadan yabigi wata mata datazo tsallakawa karar da mutanan dake gurin sukayi ne yayi sanadiyar farfadowar Maryam cikin sauri ta bude idonta Wanda tuni sun chanza kala daga fari zuwa ja.

Cikin sauri Hashim ya tsayar da motar ya nufi gurin matan Yana Bata hakuri sannna ya taimaka Mata ta tsallake titi,dawowa yayi cikin motar duba Maryam da zaiyi sai ganin ta yayi azaune.

cikin sauri yakira sunan ta Yana tambayar ta ya jikin nata?cikin maganar ta maikama da wace take shagwaba tace,"da sauki ya'ya, ya'ya ina zamu tafi ne?

"Asibiti yabata amsa atakaice. Yana faɗan  haka tashiga bubbaga ƙafar ta kamar zatayi kuka tace,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya kama yar Dani gida wallahi bana son allura. Shiru kawai yayi Yana sauraro maganar ta Saida tagama sannan yace,"kintabbata kinsamu sauki?

Nod dakai kawai tayi.

Ganin ya cigaba da tafiya yasa takara fashewa da sabon kuka tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya ka mayar Dani gida ka.. 

tsawar daya daka mata shiya hanata karasa maganar yace,"karki sake inkarajin kin bude wannan kazamar bakin naki, asibitin zain kaiki,, Amma baza'amiki allura ba magunguna kawai za'arubuta miki.

Tun daga lokacin Bata Kuma yin magana ba harsuka isah asibitin.

Hashim ne yafara fitowa daga motar sannan yazagaya ya boɗe mata kofar harta fito Yana rike da marfin motar tana fitowa taji kamar andawo Mata da ciwon kan wannan karan harwani duhu duhu take ganin.

ganin yanda Hashim ke binta da kallo yasa ta Dan daidaita natsuwar ta Dan bataso yagane halin da take ciki,, Dan gabaki daya ta tsani allura, Amma ina tana karasa fitowa tayi baya rintsa idonta tayi Dan gabaki daya tagama sadaukarwa dantasan fadi kawai zatayi.

jin anrikota yasa ta bode idonta cikin sauri ganin ta ahannun Hashim yasa ta sauke ajiyar zuciya ta mike dagowar dazatayi suka hada ido watake ganin kamar.......

8⃣➖9⃣

*Losing a mother is a pain that cannot be described in words.it is impossible to move on from the memory of losing the woman who sacrificed  happiness in her own life so that you could have a better one.A mother love is truly irerplaceable.i miss you so much mother 😭😭😭😭may ALLAH grant  you jannatul firdaus.*

*Bismillahi rahamanin Rahim*





,,,,, Yusuf ta gani atsaye ko ƙwaƙkorar   motsi bayayi idanuwan sa kuwa sunyi ja kamar wanda aka aikowa da sakon mutuwa.  Hashim kuwa ganin ta tsaya tana kallon guri ɗaya yasa yabi inda take kallon Dan ganin abinda ya dauke mata hankali aikuwa yayi mumunar gani.

binshi da ido yayi duk da sau ɗaya yataba ganin sa,, Amma bazai sa idan yasake ganin shi yakasa gane shiba,jiyayi wani Abu yatokara Masa maƙoshi shibai fito ba,  koma  bai wuce ba,saboda tsananin bacin Rai baisan lokacin da yatura ta mota ba sannan shima yazagaya,  fizgar motar yayi Saida kura yatashi aguri gabaki ɗaya Yama mance dalilin zuwan su asibitin.
****
Shikuwa Yusuf Koda suka bar gurin kasa motsi yayi Dan gabaki ɗaya jiyayi bazai iya motsa jikinsa ba. To bara muji dalilin zuwan Yusuf wannan asibitin... 


*Flash back*


Yusuf ne zaune akan gadon asibitin lokaci zuwa lokaci yake duba agogon dakin kamar maijiran albashi Haka dai har agogon dakin yanuna karfi 5 30am cikin sauri ya sauko toilet yanufa ya dauro alwala sannan yagabatar da sallah,,  Yana idarwa ya fara safa da marwa atsakiyar ɗakin ahar saida agogon dakin ta sake boga 6am bogawar agogon yayi daidai da shigowar likitan cikin sauri Yusuf yatari gaban shi yace"

Ina kwana likita.. 


Cikin sauri likita ya mika mishi hannu sukayi musabaha sannan yasake kallon shi a karo na biyu  yace"yaudai zaku tafi gida ko?

Tambayar tayi daidai da shigowar dady ciki fara'a yace"aikuwa dan jiya da lallashi muka samu yayi barci.
Haka dai sukayi ta tsukakar sa,  dubashi dr yayi  ya tabbatar musu da yanzu dai babu abinda yake damunsa saidai akiyaye duk abinda zaisake bata masa rai.da Haka suka samu aka sallame su Yusuf kowa jiyake kamar daga asibitin su zarce gidan su Maryam sanin halin daddyn nashi yasa baiyi gigin faɗa masa buƙatar sa ba.

Haka suka shiga falon gida Yusuf da da daddy suka zauna afalon mummy kuma ta shige kitchen Dan sama musu abinda zasuyi breakfast dashi,shigarta tayi daidai da ringing din wayar daddy ciki da murmushi ya daga wayar tare dayin sallama.

Maganar dayaji shiya sashi salati cikin daga murya sannan yace, " gamunan zuwa ya kashe wayar. 
Jin salatin da daddy yayi ne yasa mummy fitowa cikin sauri take tambayar sa abinda yasashi salatin "yumna ce take asibiti ta chakawa kanta wuka tunjiya take asibiti.
Aibai kaga karasa magana ba mummy ta fashe da kuka tana cewa"wallahi nasan hakan zata kasance ganin yanda ta fita daga asibiti jiya.




Haka suka fita suka nufi asibitin harda Yusuf saidai wani bangare na zuciyar sa ciki take da tunanin maryam.

Suna isa daddy yakira mahaifin yumna yayi musu kwatancan ɗakin suna zuwa yanda sukaga yumna kuwa bakaramar daga musu hankali lamarin yayi ba Dan gabaki daya tafita ahaiyacin ta gabaki ɗaya idan ka ganta saika dauka tayi shekara tana ciwo.

Cikin sauri Yusuf yakarasa bakin gadon hada hannun ta cikin nashi yayi yace"kiyi hakuri yumna wallahi ba laifina bane ina sonki,,,  saidai ba irin wace kike minba zuciya ta tayi nisa cikin San Maryam ni kaina abin Yana ban tsoro Dan jinake idan akayi rashin sa'a wata kaddara ta rabani da Maryam to babu makawa wallahi mutuwa zanyi,,,  Yana kaiwa daidai nan ya fashe da wani irin kuka mai tsuma zuciyar duk Mai saurarun ta.

Jan jiki yumna ta rinkayi harta tashi ta zauna ta daura kanta samar kafadar shi hawaye nabin fuskar ta tace"ya Yusuf karka damu Dani kaji gun maryam nabar mata kai *ALLAH* yasa Haka shiyafi Zama alkairi.



Duk kansu cike da ma'maki suke binta da kallo musamman ma Yusuf kafin yayi magana Daddyn shiyace"a a baza'ayi haka ba ai Yusuf mijin mace hudu ne,, dan  Yana son Maryam ai Haka bazai hanashi auran kiba kinji ki kwanta da hankalin ki maganar Auran ku tananan yau sauran sati uku ko harkin manta?

Cikin sauri ta girgiza Kai tana Dan share hawayan.
Shikuwa Yusuf jiyayi kamar anbuga mishi guduma akai,,  Amma ganin halin da yumna take ciki yasa yayi shiru baice komai ba,daya ga tunanin yana niman ya dameshi tashi yayi fita daga ɗakin. 

cikin sauri daddy yace"ina kuma zaka je?
Da Dan murmushi afuskar sa yace"babu ko ina zafi nakeji ne.

Saida daddy ya kalle dakin yaga ac Yana aiki sannan ga fan girgiza Kai kawai yayi sannan yace"to kardai kayi nisa.nod dakai kawai yayi yafice Yana fita yaji bugun zuciyar sa ta karu haka dai yarinka ji harya kai inda ake ajiyar motoci kamar ance ya juyo aikuwa
Yayi mumunar gani daidai lokacin Hashim Yana buɗe mata motar.  da faduwar datayi akan hannun sa duk Akan idonshi akayi.


*Back to story*


Wani irin yunkuri yayi sannu ahankali ya dinga Jan jikin shi har yakai kofar ɗakin izuwa lokacin kuwa yagama yankewa kansa shawara,Yana shiga yaji mahaifin yumna Yana yiwa daddyn shi magana akan hukuncin da ya yanke, "alhaji kana ganin hukuncin daka yanke bazai dami yaron Nan ba ? murmushi yayi sannan yace"karka damu nasan Yusuf yaro ne Mai biyayya  inada tabbacin bazai...

Jin maganar da Yusuf yayi shiya hanashi karasa maganar yace"dayar dar ALLAH bazan baku kunya ba sannan na sauke maganar  auran Maryam Dan yanzu na tabbatar da yaudara ta kawai takeyi,,  ko kuma ince tausayi na Dan yanzu naganta tare da mijinta,Dan Haka na yarda da auran yumna sannan bana bukatar yakai sati uku sati daya ma ya isa Yana kaiwa Nan yafashi da wani irin kuka.

Daddy ne yazo ya rungume shi Yana shafa bayan shi dahaka har'yadaina kukan.
*******
Ita kuwa maryan bayan sunbar asibiti Hashim gudu kawai yakeyi ikon ALLAH ne kawai ya kawo su gida ita kuwa gaba ki ɗaya  niman ciwan Kan tayi gabaki daya tarasa,, saboda tsananin tashin hakalin da tagani acikin idon Hashim gabaki ɗaya jikin ta Banda rawa babu abinda yakeyi suna isa yabode motar ya fito zagayawa yayi yabuɗe  bangaran da take fizgarta yayi da karfi har saida ta kai akasa tana kuka kaman ranta zai fita shikuwa kulli motar sa yayi sannan yabita da wani mugun kallo ai babu shiri ta tashi tashige ciki Yana biye da ita.

Harzata shige dakinta taji ya daka Mata tsawa yace"kiyi maza ki shiga ciki ki ɗaiko kayanbki gabaki ɗaya Dan wallahi yau a kaduna Zaki kwana.

Cikin kuka ta dawo ta durkusa agabanshi tace"kamin Rai ya'ya wallahi bana kaunar rabuwa da Kai Kona miskala zarrati ka taimakamin.


hashim ɓasan lokacin da yasake wata murmushi Jin daɗi ba hakan kuwa yayi daidai da fitowa aunty zainab tsayawa tayi tana kallon su Dan itadai lamarin nasu yafara bata ma'maki Yana ganin ta yakara hada girar sama da kasa Dan gabaki ɗaya bayaso tayi gigin tambayar shi abinda ya faru.

Tsawa ya sake dakawa Maryam akaro na biyu sannan yace"wallahi Maryam idan kika sake na rufe idanuwa na na buɗe  baki bace min da ganin kinyi abinda na umarce kiba saina mugum baki ma'maki.

Cikin sauri ta tashi tayi hanyar ɗakin ta tana kuka tunani biyu kesaka ta kuka, na farko batasan awani Hali Yusuf yake ba na biyu Kuma komar da ita kaduna da Hashim yake Shirin yi Haka dai tavrinka haɗa kayanta tana kuka .



Shikuwa hashim shigewa ɗaki yayi yasa key sannan ya dannan number alhaji ba'ba ringing biyu yayi aka dauka dabsallama Hashim ya fara sannan gaisuwa ya biyu baya.

tunkan Hashim yakarasa Gaisuwar alhaji ba'ba yarufe shi da faɗa  Yana cewa,"Wai mekake nufi ne iya?kokana tsoran Maryam ɗinne? " to bara kaji wallahi karka kuskura ku kara kwana agarin nan tunkafin yayi wani yunkuri jikake kit anyanke wayar bin wayar da kallo yayi sannan yace"karka damu baba wallahi nafika bukatar Maryam akusa Dani, " ni kaɗai nasan halin da nake shiga aduk lokacin da take kusa dani......


 

Fita daga dakin yayi afalo suka haɗu  da aunty zainab daure fuskar sa yayi sannan yace"zamu tafida Maryam kaduna alhaji ba'ba yace na taho da ita yau basai gobe ba.


da ma'maki afuskar ta tana so ta tambaye shi saidai bataga fuskar yin Haka ba Haka tanaji tana gani suka tafi.


Jitayi kamar tasa hannu akai tayi ta kuka tsabar takaici,Dan itadai tasan kashinta yagama bushewa Dan gaskiya babu wani aiki da takeyi agidan nan komai maryam ce keyi.

*******
Tunda suka kama hanya babu Wanda yayiwa Dan uwan sa magana bangaran Hashim kuwa tunanin yanda lamarin zaikasan ce yake..  ita kowa Banda kuka babu abinda takeyi tuntanayi da hawaye harta koma na zuci Haka dai harsuka karaso suna zuwa Mai gadi yayi saurin buɗe musu get ganin motar hashim.

Cikin sauri yazo Yana musu barka da zuwa Hashim ne kawai yasamu damar amsa masa Gaisuwar. ita kuwa maryam tsintar kanta tayi da faduwar gaba Saida ta ɗan jingina da motar, shikuwa hashim wucewar shi yayi Dan baima lura da halin da take ciki ba.

Da gudu safnah tafito ta rungumi ta itama rungume ta tayi suna farin ciki sake ganin juna saidai ganin yanda idon ta sukayi ja alamar tayi kuka yasa safnah ta riketa tana tambayar ta abinda yasaka ta kuka", sister maryam meke damunki? Cikin sauri itama ta mayar mata da tambayar"me kika gani?

Kallon ma'maki tayi mata sannan tace"Kinga tunda bazaki faɗa  minba shikenan idan yayi wari maji,kindai San ke muke jira haryanzu bamu fitar da ankon bikin ba.

Cikin ma'maki take kallon ta tace"bikin wa za'ayi?

Ita dinma kallon ma'maki take mata zatayi magana daidai lokacin Hashim yafito yace"little sis bara nakawo muku ta barma saiku shimfiɗa kuyi hirar ku iya San ranku.

Murmushi tayi sannan taja hannun maryam suka nufi cikin gidan kowa da abinda yake sakawa Aran shi.
Suna shiga mummy ta taso cikin sauri ta rungume ta ita kuwa maryam fashewa tayi da kuka Wanda ita kanta batasan dalilin yin hakan ba.

Ahankali mummy take shafe bayan ta tana rarrashin ta sunfi karfin mintina talatin atsaye sannan mummy taja hannun ta zuwa ɗakin ta suna shiga tace"baby kishiga kiyi wanka kifito kici abinci  sai kizo kifaɗamin  dalililn kukan naki.

Ita kuwa safnah dama so take mummy da Maryam subar gurin ta samu damar tambayar Hashim dalilin dayasa Maryam kuka Dan daganin idon ta kasan tadade da fara kukan cikin sauri ta Koma inda yake tace," bros Dan ALLAH ka faɗamin  abinda kayiwa amaryar taka.

Cikin sauri yarufe Mata bakita reda lekawa ganin babu kowa yasa ya jata zuwa gurin mota bude motar yayi sannan ya umarce ta data shiga babu musu tashiga Jan motar yayi suka bar harabar gidan Saida sukayi nisa da gidan sannan yasamu guri yayi parking ita kuwa safnah binshi take da kallon ma'maki zatayi magana 
Yariga ta da cewa.

Little sister ke kaɗai kawai Zaki taimake Ni wallahi little sister dasonta narayu,,,  wallahi kowani fitar numfashi na da sonta yake fita Kuma wallahi batasan da maganar auran Nan ba intakaice Miki batasan da maganar alkawarin ba .

Ajiyar zuciya tayi Mai sauti sannan ta ɗanyi murmushi  tace"karka damu zan taimaka maka zanyi iya bakin kokari na kaji big bros.

Rike hannun ta yayi sannan yace,"kinyi alkawari?

Nod da Kai kawai tayi  tace"nayi Amma kaima saikamin alkawarin zaka rikemin sweet sister Dina amana.

Murmushi yayi Wanda saida safnah tayi ma'maki Dan Bata taba ganin yayi irin wannan murmushin ba.
Haka dai suka cigaba da shawarar su sannan suka juya zuwa gida.

Ita kuwa Maryam  tana gama wankan ta dauro alwala tayi sallah sannan ta bude akwatinta ,ta ciro doguwar rigar ta Mara hannun tasa Bata tsaya yin ƙwalliya  ba tafito ta nufi kitching ta ɗibe abinci daidai yanda zata'iya ci  dawowa tayi falon ganin babu kowa afalon  yasa ta Koma dakin mummy tana gama cin abinci ta haye gado tayi kwanciyar ta  Bata Jima da kwanciya ba barci yayi awan gaba da ita.



Koda mummy tashigo ta sameta tana barci murmushi tayi tana aiyana irin rikicin da za'ayi da Maryam idan taji halin da ake ciki gyarama ta kwanciya tayi  ta kunna Mata ac sannan tafita ta  rufe mata kofa.

Ba ita ta farka daga barci ba sai kusan karfe biyar cikin sauri ta mike toilet tafa ɗa ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da sallah Jin muryar safnah afalo yasa tafita ganin su ilham yasa takarasa gurin su cikin sauri faɗawa jiki ta tayi tana dariya ita ma ilham din dariya take tace,"aunty ilham yaushe kika zo ?inah Junior ?inah Uncle sadiq ?

Duk alokaci daya ta jero Mata tambayar dariya duk  kansu sukayi sannan ilham tace,"little sister duk ka wannan tambayar wani kaɗai kuma lokaci daya ?zumbura baki tayi sannan tace"nidai kiga ya min kinji aunty na.

Duk kansu dariya sukayi sannan ilham tabata amsa kamar Haka,"junior Yana barci adakin safnah Uncle sadiq Kuma sai sati Mai zuwa zaizo sannan yanzu muka iso .
Bakaramar murna tayi ba ganin ilham domin kuwa damuwar ta cikin Kashi dari hamsin ta wuce.

Haka suka rinka Hira ciki da kulawa da kaunar juna,ilham da safnah addu'a kawai suke ALLAH yasa Maryam tayi barci Dan su samu sufara Shirin yanda zasu  gabatar da bikin batare da tasan harda ita ba duk da sun faɗa Mata bikin safnah ce za'ayi tayi farin ciki sosai saidai harzuwa lokacin Bata daina jin faɗuwar  gaban ba.

Aikuwa  ALLAH yakarbi addu'a su Dan Bata Jima ba barci yayi awan gaba da ita,cikin sauri suka nufi falo suna zuwa suka tarar da mummy sai safa da marwa take,Tana ganin sun fito ta sauke wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya sannan tace,"fainaly tayi barci ko?

Nod da kawai kawai sukayi sannan suka samu guri suka zauna ahaka harsuka shirya yanda abin zai kasan ce batare da ansamu wata matsala ba.
Ahaka kuwa yanufi gurin kwanciyar shi danufin gobe zasu fara gabatar da komai.



***************""
Shikuwa Yusuf Yana gama faɗa  musu shawarar daya
yanke cikin sauri yabar asibitin gida ya nufa Yana zuwa dakin shi yashiga sannan yasa key toilet yashiga yasakar wa kanshi ruwa kozai daina jin azababban zafi dayake ji duk ilahirin jikin sa.



Saidai  ba'anan gizo ke saka ba dan jiyayi kamar ana zuba mishi garwashin wuta.

Cikin sauri yafito Yana tsane jikin  sa da karamar towel dake hannun shi lokaci daya abinda yafaru yake dawo mishi kamar yanzu abin yake faruwa lokaci daya ya tsinci kanshi da wani irin Dana sani to kodai ba mijin ta bane? " Dan inada tabbacin Maryam bazata min karya ba nasan maryam bazata taɓa zama  ɗaya daga  cikin masu karya ba,,,  duk da bawata halaka Mai karfi suke ba saidai yasan tana faɗan gaskiya Koda kuwa tasan hakan bazai maka daɗi ba.

Buga kanshi yayi  Dakarfi yace ,"sheet 
Agurguje yashirya cikin riga da wando kayan bakaramar kyau sukayi masa ba Yana fita yanufi motarsa daidai lokaci daddy yasako hancin motar sa.tsayawa Yusuf yayi harsaida da sukayi parking yabuɗe  wa mummyn shi cikin murmushi tafito shikuwa daddy murmushi yayi yace,"wato Yusuf kafison mummy ka ko?

Cike da Jin kunya ya sunkuyar da kanshi Yana wasa da yatsun hannun sha zagayuwa daddy yayi ya rungume shi kamar karamin yaro sannan yace,"come on boy wasa nake,  ina Mai tabbatar maka da kasani cikin farin cikin da ni kaina bazan iya misaltashi ba ina alfahari da kai.

Cike da ma'makin furucin daddyn nashi yake binshi da kallo baikai
da gama mamakin  ba mummy shi itama ta rungume shi tana cewa,"yanda kayi Mana biyayya ALLAH yabaka wayanda zasu Rama maka.

Basu jira jinme
zaifaɗa ba suka shige ciki suka barshi da tarin tambayoyi  Wanda suka ɗai keda amsoshin su.
Cikin sauri yanufi motarsa da gudu yabar harabar gidan Dan tunawa da dalilin dayasa yafita.

Kofar gidan su Maryam yanufa Yana zuwa Mai gadin yafito Yana tambaya Sa yace"yallabai wakake Nima?

Kafin yabashi amsar tambayar sa sukaji ana kullawa mai gadin kira cikin sauri yakoma ciki Dan amsar Kiran aunty zainab.

Yana zuwa da masifa tace"dawa kake magana?

Jikin shi har'rawa yake yace,"wani bawan ALLAH ne .

Tsawar da ta daka mishi shiya Hana shi karasa maganar sa tace,"niban tambaye ka wannan ba muje na ganshi.

Yusuf Yana ganin su cikin sauri yafito daga cikin motar sa  cikin girmamawa ya gaishita sannan ya gabatar Mata da kansa.
Cikin girmamawa ta amsa sannan ta sanarda shi matsayinta agidan, bayan haka  ta faɗami shi Maryam ta tafi kaduna bikin Yar uwar ta Amma Nan ba  dadadewa bazata dawo.

Cikin farin Ciki yabar kofar gida yanufi gida Yana zuwa yakira ikram yasanar da ita taimakon da yake  so tayi Masa so yake ta turomishi da  address din gidan su Maryam dake kaduna.

Haka kuwa akayi  ba'afi mintina biyar ba ta turomishi cikin awa daya har yagama shiri.

Ko sallamar  mahaifansa baiba  ya nufi garin kaduna danji yake bazai iya kwana bai saka maryamar sa a idanuwan sa ba...❄❄❄❄❄❄

__________Koda Yusuf  yafita bai tsaya ko ina ba sai garin kaduna...  gidan abokin sa ya nufa tunkafin yakara so ya sanar dashi zuwan nashi.

Koda iya isa gidan cike da murna suka rungume juna suna farin cikin sake haɗuwa, Saida Yusuf yayi wanka ya gabatar da sallah sannan ya sanar dashi dalilin zuwan nashi.

Jin jina maganar Aryan yayi sannan yace"Yusuf kana da tabbacin ba mijin ta bane kuwa?

Girgiza masa Kai yayi alamar a a.
Sannan yace," inaji ajiki na maryam tawa ce Ni kaɗai saboda ita aka halice Ni.... "wallah Aryan jinake idan ban mallake ta ba to komai na iya faruwa idan nace komai ina nufin komai.

Zauke ajiyar zuciya Aryan yayi sannan yace"to ALLAH ya sadamu da abinda yafi zama alkairi.

"Ameen yace cike da Jin dadin ardu'a.
Ahaka dai suka bar maganar akan gobe zasu tafi gidan su Maryam Dan Yusuf yanuna wa Aryan address din da ikram ta toromasa, duk da ba Haka Yusuf yaso ba yaso yaga maryamar sa yau Amma sannin Hali irin na Aryan yasa dole ya hakura.

******
Yumna kuwa Ananan ana shirye shiryan biki ba'akama hannun yaro anko kala bakwai suka fitar ita da Friends  ɗinta mummy ta kuwa  dauko Mai gyarar  jiki tayi kullum ana abu ɗaya  cikin lokaci daya gabaki daya ta canza  ɗan masu gyarar jiki na musamman mummy  ta dauko dansu gyara mata diyar ta kolli ɗaya.

Duk da kasancewar rashin zuwan Yusuf Yana damunta Amma Bata bari damuwar hakan tayi tasiri acikin zuciyar ta ba, Haka taci gaba da harkar gaban ta saidai idan taji tana Jin kewar ganin fuskar sa  ta dauki hutun sa tana kallo tana hawaye idan ta gama ta share hawayan ta fito kamar ba wacce tayi kuka ba.

*****
Su kuwa iyayan Yusuf rashin ganin dawowar shi gida jiya bakaramar tayar musu da hankali yayi ba,,, wani Karin tashin hankalin ma shine gabaki daya wayoyin sa akashe suke zaune suke afalo kowa da abinda yake tunani mummyn sane tayi karfin halin Fara magana tace.

"gaskiya nidai idan za'abi tawa shawarar gara a hakura da wannan auran Dan gaskiya bazan jure ganin Dana kolli ɗaya da ALLAH yabani cikin wannan halin ba.

Tsawa Mai karfi daddy yayi Mata tare da mikewa tsaye Yana nuna ta yace"dama nayi tunanin da sa hannun ki acikin wannan al'amarin to Bari kiji wallahi kinji na rantse koda Mai sunan baba zai mutu bayan an daura Mai aure da yarinyar Nan yumna to wallahi saidai ya mutu...  domin bazaku mayar Dani mutumin banza Mai magana biyu ba,aikin banza kawai Yana kaiwa daidai Nan bai tsaya Bata lokaci ba yayi waje abinsa Yana sambatu.

Ita kuwa tsabar shiga rudani kasa Zama tayi tana ta safa da marwa akarshe ma fashewa tayi da matsanan cin kuka ita damuwar ta daya rashin hada ido da gudan jinin ta.


*****
Ita kuwa maryam Bata farka ba sai da taji ankira sallar asuba cikin sauri ta faɗa toilet ta dauro alwala tana idarwa ta Koma ta kwata bata sake farkawa ba sai kusan 11 shiɗin ma aunty ilham ne suka tashi ta...  tashi tayi da sallati abakin ta idon ta bai sauka ako inah ba sai Kan agogo cikin sauri ta sauko toilet tashiga tayi wanka riga da siket Dan kanti tasaka...  sannan ta dauko hijabin ta ta saka bawani ƙwalliya tayi Mai yawa ba Amma tayi kyau.

Fitowa daga ɗakin tayi ta shaga ɗakin mum tana zuwa ta suguna harkasa ta gaishe da mum sannan ta tambayi ta safnah da aunty ilham"suna falo ke kawai suke jira.cikin sauri ta nufi Falon tana zuwa ta tarar dasu duk sun shirya kama kunnen ta tayi cikin sigar wasa tace"kiyi hakuri aunty wallahi barci ne yadauki Ni kinsan haryanzu da gajiya ajiki na .

Aunty ilham sake daure fuskar ta tayi Dan Yana daya daga cikin tsarin su na jiya tace,"Ni matsa kibani wuri bayan kin batawa mutane lokaci sannan kinzo Zaki ishe mu da surutu, Kinga idan kin shirya ki tashi mu tafi idan Kuma haryanzu baki shirya ba to mudai Kinga tafiyar mu Bata tsaya jiran jin abinda zata faɗa  ba taja hannun safnah sukayi gaba.
Ita kuwa maryam tsayawa tayi baki sake tana kallon aunty ilham ciki da ma'maki Dan aiyah sanin ta Banda so da nuna kulawa babu abinda Aunty ilham take nuna Mata to wani laifi nayi Haka waini yaushe nene zan daina shiga damuwa ne ? Cikin sauri tabi bayan su... 

Sukuwa suna fita aunty ilham ta kalli safnah tace "gaskiya sister bazan iya wannan aikin ba Kinga kuwa yanda jiki na ke Bari wallah jinake kamar na fashe da kuka.
Rike Mata hannu safnah tayi tace"Kinga aunty wannan itace kawai hanya mafi sauki da zamu samarwa bros farin ciki rayuwar sa ki daure kawai kinji? 

zata Kuma yin magana sukaga tafito Nanda Nan ilham ta canza fuska, aunty ilham ce ke  tukin motar safnah tana dayan bangaran Maryam kuma tana baya tana aikin zubda hawaye Dan hirar su suke Amma ko kusa basa sakata cikin zancan su.

Aunty ilham tana satar kallon ta ta side mirror jitayi bazata iya ci gaba da Hirar ba Dan Haka tabar safnah tana ta zuba...  ita ma dataga ilham ta daina amsawa ta kama bakin ta tayi shiru.

Ahaka har suka isah kasuwa duk shagon da suka shiga sai aunty ilham ta saka Maryam ta dauki duk abinda take so ahaka harsuka k gama sayaiyar kayan suka jibga abayan motar su suka nufi gida suna zuwa Maryam ta nufi dakin ta ita kuwa aunty ilham fadawa jikin mum tayi tana kuka ta ce"gaskiya mum bazan iya aiki Nan ba karki so kiga yanda kirjina kemin radadi.

Cikin sigar lallashi mum tafara rarrashin ta tana cewa,"nasan aiki ne Mai wahala gaske...  Amma kiyi hakuri kinji ilham nanda ɗan wani lokaci ne..  Kinga maza tashi ki Kira ta kinsan Mai gyarar jikin tana kan hanya ...

Da sallama aunty ilham ta shiga ɗakin kusan sau uku tana kiran sunan ta,, amma bata jiba,, saida ta kira suman ta da  karfi... 


Cikin sauri ta ɗaga Kai tana ganin aunty ilham da gudu ta faɗa jikin ta tana kuka tana cewa,"dan ALLAH aunty karki gujeni kamar yanda kowa ya gujeni karki kasance ɗaya daga cikin abinda zanrasa,   Kinga aunty na rasa makaranta na rasa ƙawata ikram na rasa samun sakin fuska daga ya'ya na rasa abinda nake mattukar kauna Yusuf.

Tana kaiwa nan ta Kara fashewa da kuka,ita ma shara hawaye take sannan tace,"Ni babu abinda kikayi min saidai idan kina so mu sherya to duk abinda nace kiyi zakiyi babu gardama Kuma babu tambaya kin yarda?

Nod dakai kawai tayi.

Aunty ilham ce ta share Mata hawaye sannan ta umarci ta da ta gabatar da sallar azahar idan ta idar tafito za'ayi musu gyarar jiki.

Kallon ma'maki take binta dashi da harzata tambaye ta dalilin gyarar jikin,sai kuma ta tuno da sharaɗin  da ta bata,hakan yasa tayi shiru.

Tashige warta toilet Dan gabatar da alwalla Amma zuciyar ta Banda sake sake babu abinda take yi.

Itah kuwa Aunty ilham tana fita ta rungume mummy tana kuka,
Mummy ta ture ta daga jikin ta tana cewa,"dallah Ni karki illata Ni dakinsan bazaki iyah ba miyasa kikace abarmiki kumai ahannun ki,sai yanzu dankin ga ya Koma Kano shine Zaki fito da sabon walakanci?to bara nakira she sai kifaɗa masa ba zaki iya ba kinfi mu son Maryam.

Tana kaiwa Nan Bata tsaya Jin me zata ceba ta dauko wayar ta.cikin sauri ilham ta kaibi wayar tana cewa"bawai bazan iya bane mummy nama rigada na gama shawo kan al'amarin .

Kallon ma'maki duk suke binta dashi mummy tace,"ban fahimce kiba.

Share hawayan idonta tayi sannan ta Bata labarin duk yanda sukayi da maryam.

Zauke ajiyar zuciya mummy tayi sannan tace"kinyi yanda akeso ilham ALLAH yayi Miki albarka.

Ameen tace.

Mummy ce ta saki kallon ta sannan tace,"kukan me kike ne ilham?kodai bakison auran Nan ne? 

Cikin sauri tace"a a  kawai dai tausayi take bani.

Tashe mummy tayi ta nufi ɗakin ta tana jinjina soyayar dake tsakanin ilham da maryam.

*****
Yusuf kuwa tun bayan sallar asuba bai kuma koma wa barci ba karshe ma komawa falo yayi ya zauna lokaci zuwa lokaci yake kallon agogon ɗakin. danshi gani yake kamar lokaci baya sauri Yana zaune ahaka har karfe 8:am tayi cikin sauri ya nufi ɗakin da Aryan ya kwana yana zuwa  ya samu Aryan yana sharara uwar barci. 

Cike da bacin rai ya shiga kiran  sunan sa... .

kamar daga sama Aryan yaji anakiran sunan shi ciki hanzari ya buɗe  ido Yana cewa lafiya kuwa  Yusuf ?waye bashida lafiya?

Lokaci ɗaya ya jeru masa tambayar shiyasa ya tsaya Yana kallon shi.shirun da Yusuf yayi shiya Kara tayar mishi da hankali cikin sauri ya mike Yana girgiza shi yace"Dan ALLAH ka faɗamin .

Saukar da hannun shi Yusuf yayi sannan yace"babu Wanda ya mutu sannan lafiyar kuwa kalau saidai, Banda Ni domin,mutuwa nake ina maimaitawa.

Cike da ma'makin kalaman aryan yace,"ban fahimci kaba Yusuf meke damunka Haka?

Shiɗin ma kallon ma'maki yake binshi da shi sannan yace,"kardai ace harka mance da dalilin zuwa na garin Nan ?

Bubbuga kanshi yashiga yi danshi gabaki ɗaya ya mance da zancan maryam.

Kallon agogon ɗakin yayi yaga karfe takwas da rabi, girgiza kanshi yayi sannan ya shige toilet Yana ma'makin wanna wace iriyar soyayya Yusuf yakewa wannan yarinyar....
*******


Ita kuwa yumna shirye shirye ya kankama Dan yaune zasu fara evet dinsu duk da taso boye sirrin ta Dana angon nata abin ya faskara...  Dan duk Wanda yaganta yasa tana cikin tashin hankali,tsabar kuka har"idon ta sun faɗa ,taro yayi taro guri yayi guri Amma babu ango Haka dai aka sirrinta rashin zuwan angon dacewa bashida lafiya ne.

Ga wayan da suka San da rashin lafiyar Yusuf din sun yarda,wa yanda Basu sani ba kuwa suka kama bakin su suka dinke 🤐🤐🤐saidai kasan zuciyar su kuwa suna kwankwanto akan lamarin.

*Haka aka gama aka watse, ni kuwa  saidai ince ALLAH yakaimu na gobe Dan yau nida maimuna aliyu munce bazamu daura girki agidajan mu  
Ba Dan munsamu gara basa* 😜😜😜😜.

*****
Hashim kuwa Yana sauka garin Kano  yasa aka tada gini gadan gadan kankace mi gida ya kammala,gini Mai kyau da tsari akayi, Pat uku aka mayar da gidan  idan bakasan gidan sosai ba to idan kazo zaka iya rantsiwa da ALLAH bagidan bane.

Babu irin tambayar da aunty zainab batayi mishi ba Akan dalilin chanza tsarin gidan akullum idan ta mishi irin tambayar Nan yakan cemata aure zai kara,,,  Amma sam saita mayar da abun kamar wasa.

Dan itah gani take bazai taba Mata kishiya ba kamar kullum yauma suna zaune tace,"Wai Ni kuwa daddy ikram yaushe Maryam zata dawo kaga kullum ikram sai tayi  kuka Dan ALLAH ka dawo da itah,idanma dan wannan yaron ne kabarta ta auri Wanda take so Dan wallahi harnan yaron yazo karkaso kaga yanda yafita daga haiyacin sa Dan Rashin ganin ta kwana biyu.

"Ni kuwa tausayin sa yakamani harna faɗa masa tayi tafiya...  jiya Kuma ija zaune saiga  ƙawarta  ikram taxo gidan Nan take faɗamin  Wai Yama tafi kadunan kuma itace ta bashi address din .....  
Gigitatciyar Marin data jine ya hanata karasa magana jitayi ya shako Mata wuya Yana huci yace,"bantaba tunanin ke sakarya bace sai yau matar da yau sauran kwana uku adaura auran ta kika bashi address din gidan mu,uwar me kikeso yaje ya Mata?

To bara kiji idan wani Abu yasami maryam wallahi kotu ce zata rabamu dake,sannan aure babu fashi,wurgar da itah yayi sannan ya nufi motar shi fisgar motar yayi yafita da gudu.
Bai tsaya ko inah ba sai airpot yayi sa"akuwa sauran tikiti na mutum ɗaya bai tsaya Bata lokaci ba ya biya cikin mintina kadan suka daga sai Kano.


*

*****

Shikuwa Yusuf  Banda yawon   niman anguwar su Maryam babu abinda sukeyi, da  Haka Aryan zaiyi ta yawo dashi daga wannan anguwar zuwa wancan ahaka harsuka shafe tsawan kwana hudu suna Abu ɗaya .

Abinda zai baka ma'maki anan shine Aryan yasan hargidan su maryam saka makon address din gidan da  aka bashi saidai yaki nuna mishi gidan ne danbin umarnin mahaifan shi.

*Flash back*

Bayan Aryan ya fito daga wanka Yana shafa Mai ya hangi wayar Yusuf ayashe akasa da kamar bazai dauka ba sai  kuma ya tsinci kanshi da San daukar wayar Yana dauka yaganta akashe,cikin  sauri ya kunna wayar kunnawar shi yayi daidai da shigowar kiran mummy daukar wayar yayi yasa akunne.

Cikin kuka ta fara magana tana cewa,"yanzu abinda kayi ka kyauta kenan son? karasa wayanda zaka watsawa kasa a ido sai iyayan
ka?  to babu matsala tunda ta hanyar da zaka saka Mana kenan.

Jin alamar zata kashe wayar yayi saurin cewa,"mummy ba Yusuf bane Aryan ne.

Gaisawa sukayi cikin mutumci sannan ya bukaci ta sanar dashi abinda yake faruwa.batayi kasa aguiwa ba ta sanar dashi duk abinda ke faruwa,shikuwa  ya daukar Mata alkawarin zaiyi iya bakin kokarin sa Dan yaga Basu haɗuba .

*Back to story*

Saidai ganin irin halin da yashiga yasa yake tunanin yau  zai kaishi yaga maryamarsa.tashi yayi daga inada yake zaune ya matso daidai inda Yusuf yake yace,"abokina kayi hakuri na cutar da Kai saidai kasani yanzu babu lokaci katashi na kaika gurin maryam.

Duk da bai fahimci abinda abokin nashi yake nufi ba saidai Jin ya ambaci zuwa gurin maryam yasa ya mike cikin sauri suka fice daga gidan.


******


Shikuwa Hashim yana sauka ajirgin yadauki shatar motar da zata kaishi unguwar rimi .
Shigowar shi yayi daidai da shigowar Yusuf da Aryan laying....
