1⃣3⃣➖1⃣4⃣   

*Bismillahi rahamanin Rahim*

Parking sukayi daidai kofar gidan...  sannan Aryan ya fito ya nufi Mai gadin dake tsaye abakin kofar sallama yayi Masa daidai lokacin motar Hashim  ta tsaya abayan nasu

Cikin sauri Mai gadin yace, " bawan ALLAH ka matsar da motar ka.

Cikin sauri ya koma motar ya janyi ta..  sannan yafito shikuwa Hashim ana bode kofar gidan da gudu ya shige ciki .

Aryan ya karasa gurin Mai gadin yace"dama bawani abu bane baba Maryam muke son gani.

Washe baki yayi sannan yace "to suwaye  za'ace mata.

Yusuf kasa hakuri yayi ya fito yace"Yusuf zaka ce mata.

Shigewa ciki Mai gadin yayi da niyar faɗa Mata sakon.


               Shikuwa Hashim cikin sauri ya shige gidan babu ko sallama Yana shiga kuwa yayi mummunar gani Wanda saida yakusa sume wa.



Maryam yagani tafito da gudu safnah na biye da ita daure da towal Wanda da kadan ya wuce ma zaunan ta suman kanta kuwa baki kirin ya zube agadan bayan ta sabanin safnah wace duguwar rigace ajikin ta.

Kasa motsi yayi har saida yaga sun doso inda yake  tsaye karfin Hale yayi yace,"ke safnah wannan wace iriyar wasa ce?

Duk kansu tsayawa sukayi suna kallon shi Dan Sam basuji shigowar saba.

Maryam ce tafara tsuguna wa harkasa ta gaishe shi ganin yanda Maryam tayi yasa ita ma safnah tayi yanda tayi sabanin da Idan ta ganshi da gudu take rungume shi.

Gabaki daya maryam ta mance da babu Kaya ajikin ta Jin sallamar Mai gadi yasa Hashim daka Mata wata razananniya tsawa yace"ke mahaukaciyar inace bakiji ana sallama bane.

Rashin Gane abinda yake nufi yasa duk kansu suka kasa Koda kwakwarar motsin Jin Mai gadi na ƙwakarin buɗe labulan falon yasa Hashim ya miƙe  cikin sauri ya fisgi Maryam bai dire ta koinah ba sai ɗaki.

Itah kuwa rintsa idonta tayi tana jira taji saukar duka ta koinah  Jin shirun yayi yawa yasa ta buɗe  idonta ahankali tana budewa kuwa suka Haka idon cikin sauri ta sunkuyar da kanta kasa.

Sai alokacin ta lura da babu Kaya ajikin ta cikin sauri ta shige cikin labulan dake dakin.
Shikam abin nata ma dariya ya bashi to mekoma na boyewa bayan tun ɗazu take ahaka

Fita yayi daga ɗakin Yana shiga falon daidai lokacin Mai gadi ke sanarwa safnah da sakon bakin, cikin sauri ya shake Mai gadin Yana huce kamar Wanda yayi gudun fanfalaki yace", wakace??

Mai gadi Wanda tuni ido yaraina fata yace,"wa..Wai su..sunan shi Yu..su.f.

Jifa yayi da Mai gaɗin ya nufi bakin get din yana zuwa ya same Yusuf Yana safa da marwa akofan  gidan baiyi aune ba yaji anrike Masa wuyar riga.
Wazai gani wannan mutumin ne dai Wanda yake so yaga yaraba shida abinda yafiso arayuwar sa baiyi wata wata ba shima yarike masa wuyar riga...  Aryan Yana daga motar ganan abinda yake faruwa yasa yafito cikin sauri yashiga tsakanin su.

Hashim ne yafara magana cikin fushi yace"wallahi idan ka Kuma zuwa kofar gidan Nan da sunan  kazo niman maryam sai nayi karar ka.

Aryan ne ya amshi maganar dacewa "haba bawan ALLAH aiba Haka akeyi ba daga zuwa niman arziki Kuma saika kama mutum da faɗa.

Cikin fushi yusuf yace"Kuma wallahi karka ga ka girme min wallahi wallahi Akan Maryam babu abinda bazan iyah yiba sakarai kawai.

Cikin azama da saurin hannun Hashim yasamu nasaran kaimai naushi ɗaya abaki kankace Mai Jini yafara zuba abakin yusuf cikin azama shima yafara azamar maiyar da martani.

Ana cikin Haka  motar alhaji ba'ba ya shigo harabar get din gidan ganin abinda ke faruwa yasa yafito cikin sauri.

Tsawar da ya daka wa Hashim shiyasa duk ka suka juya cike da ma'maki suna kallon shi Dan duk kan so basu san   da zuwan shi gurin ba .

Cikin sauri Hashim ya tsuguna Yana gaishe shi saidai Sam shi hankalin shi baya ma kanshi kallon yusuf Yake ganin Hashim ya tsuguna Suma suka tsugguna suka gaishe shi... cikin sakin fuska ya amsa sannan yakai duban shi gun Hashim yace"Kai Hashim da girman ka kake dambe abakin layi?

"Abinda bakayi ba tun kana yaro shine zakayi da girman ka?

Hashim zaiyi magana alhaji ba'ba ya dakatar da shi yace,"bansan Jin komai ko Kuma yara daga inah?

Aryan ne ya amsa da cewa Ni sunana Aryan  ni dan kabala ne.wannan Kuma Yusuf daga garin Kano Yake munzo gun maryam ne.

Murmushi alhaji ba'ba yayi Dan sai yanzu yagane musabbabin faɗan.

Sannan yace, "inah dai fata lafiya?yayi tambayar ne Dan yakara tabbatar da  zargin shi

Wannan karon ma Aryan ne yasake yin magana yace,"wannan Dan uwan nawane yazo gunta Dan tun a Kano suke soyaya.

Faɗan Haka yasa Hashim yayi saurin cewa"baba wallahi karya n..

Daka tarda shi alhaji ba'ba yayi sannan yace"to saidai kuyi hakuri musamman ma Kai Yusuf domin a takaice dai jibi asabar za'a daurawa Maryam aure.

Zaman dirshi Yusuf yayi cikin karfin Hali Aryan yace,"baba wazata aura.

Babu ko gizau yanuna Hashim yace,"ga mijin ta nan.

Da mamaki Yusuf yake nuna shi da Dan yatsa yace ya'yan ta Koma mijin ta aibai karasa maganar ba yafaɗi  ko alamar motsi babu atatare da shi.

Duk ka sukayo kanshi Banda kuka da girgiza shi babu abinda Aryan Yake yi.
Hashim yayi sauri fitar da mota yace asakashi suje asibiti.

Dakatar dashi ba'ba yayi sannan yashiga motar ya dauko ruwa ya watsa Masa ciki ikon ALLAH ya bude ido saidai yakasa cewa komai .

Ganin Haka yasa  alhaji ba'ba yayi Mishi ardu'a cikin ruwa ya bashi...  yabuɗi baki yace,"Aryan Wai da gaske maryama ta aure zatayi kuma ya'yan ta zata aura?


Cikin sauri alhaji ba'ba yace,"ya'yan tane Amma ba Uwa daya ba koma ba uba daya ba.... zanfaɗa maka wannan sirrin ne saboda ganin halin da kake
ciki Wanda ita kanta Maryam Bata sani ba.


Duk kansu kallon sa suke ciki da ma'maki furucin nashi.

Ganin irin kallon dasuke masa yasa yajuya zai shiga gida haryanzu dai kallon sa suke.

Juyowa yayi yace,"idan Kuna da bukatar Jin yanda lamarin ya kasance to ko Kushigo ciki.



Aibai gama rufe baki ba duk kansu suka mike suka bi bayan shi..



Falon baƙi Hashim ya kaisu sannan ya shiga ciki ya umarci safnah da ta Kai musu abin motsa baki bayan yafaɗa Mata ya nufi dakin shi dake gidan.

Toilet ya faɗa  ya sakarwa kanshi ruwa ko zaidan ji sanyi duk da kasancewar Yusuf da maryam Basu haɗu ba... Amma ji yake zuciyar sa na mishi Kuna da raɗaɗi .

Saida ya kwashe mintina ashirin sannan ya fito, ya danji daɗi kasancewar kasu hamsin cikin ɗari na damuwar ya ragu.

Mai ya shafa sannan ya dauko rigar sa body hug ya dauko Fara,sannan ya dauko Wanda sa baka yasa kasancewar sa fari bakaramin kyau kayan suka yi Mishi ba.

Cikin mintina kalilan Hashim yagama Shirin sa tsaf sannan ya nufi Falon baƙi.

Yanda ya barsu Haka ya dawo ya tadda su,mika musu hannu yayi suka sake yin musabaha sannan ya nemi guri ya zauna Dan jiran fitowar abban nashi.

Ba'afi mintina biyar ba alhaji ba'ba ya shigo da murmushi akan fusakar sa yace"kuyi hakuri Yan samari dawowa ta kenan daga zariya Kunga akwai bukatar na watsa ruwa.

Nod dakai kawai yusuf yayi sabanin Aryan da ya amsa dacewa gaskiya Kam.

Muskutawa alhaji ba'ba yayi sannan yafara da.

"ASSALAMUN ALAIKUM "
Duk suka amsa da 
"WA'ALAIKUMUN SALAM"

To kamar yanda nayi muku alkawarin zanfaɗa  muku yanda dangantakarmu ta kasance da iyayan maryam

*FLASH BACK*

Ahalin yunusa Mai kanti sun taso cikin so da kaunar juna... yunusa Yana da Mata ɗaya wacce ake Kira Khadija. Yana matukar son matar sa itama Haka duk wani Abu da tasan zai kawo sabanin atsakanin su to ko hanyar Bata bi.

Hakan kuwa ba karamin kima da daraja ya karawa Khadija agun mijin ta ba Kai harma da dangi baki ɗaya kuwa nasan faranta ran Khadija,Dan acewar su idan kaga yunusa na Fara'a to fa an faran ta ran Khadija.

Kasancewar sa Mai ƙyautata wa dangin sa yasa kuwa kesan faranta ran yunusa Mai kanti.

Babu wata damuwa dake damun dangin sa dashi kansa ma baki ɗaya daya wuce rashin samun haihuwar Khadija. Dan idan ban manta ba shakaran su biyar da aure Amma ko batar wata Bata taba yiba.

Hakan bakaramar daga musu hankali yake ba musamman ma Khadija wace ko wani dare saita tashi tayi ta ardu'a tana kaiwa ALLAH kukan ta.

Khadija ta kasance salahar mace Bata da yawan magana ko kaɗan...  duk abinda takeyi tana yin shine ciki natsuwa, shiyasa koda ta taso batada kawaye barkatai irin na yan zamani.

kawarta ɗayace Indo wacce suka taso tare ita dinma ta dade da rasuwa gurin haihuwa cikin ikon ALLAH dan ma baizo da rai ba.

To tun daga lokacin Bata sakeyin wata kawar ba.

Tana zaune wata alhamis misalin karfe biyu na Rana tana kallon tashar AREWA 24 akayi sallama, amsawa tayi sannan ta bada izinin shigowa.

Wata matace wace akalla zata girme Mata ta shigo,gaisawa sukayi cikin girmamawa sannan Khadija ta ɗanyi murmushi tace,"saidai kiyi hakuri wallahi ban Ganeki ba.

Ita dinma murmushi tayi sannan tace,"gaskiya ne ni dinma yauce Rana na farko Dana Fara ganin ki,awancan sabon gidan nake yau kwanan mu uku da tare wa shine MIJINA yace nashiga mu gaisa saboda hakin makota ka.

"Aiya aikuwa mun gode kuma naji dadi wallahi Kinga ko babu komai nasamu Yar uwa.

Murmushi tayi tana yaba saukin Kai irin na wannan matar, sannan tace"Ni sunan suwaiba.

Ni kuma Khadija.

Haka dai suka Dan taba hira kasancewar duk kansu ba gwanaye bane gurin surutu.

Swaiba tayi wa Khadija sallama ta tafi haka kawai Khadija taji suwaiba ta kwanta Mata arai..  Koda yunusa Mai kanti ya dawo Khadija ta sanar dashi ba karamin farin ciki yayi ba... Dan ya fuskaci Khadija tana son matar to dama shi bashi da abinso da yawuce Khadija.

Cikin kankanin lokaci shakuwa Mai karfi yashiga tsakanin Khadija da suwaiba haka afanin mazajan su.

Shakuwa ce mai karfi Wanda babu Wanda yake boyewa Dan uwan sa damuwar sa.cikin Haka Khadija ta faɗawa  suwaiba damuwar ta na rashin samun haihuwa.

Hakuri ta Bata da Kara jaddada Mata taci gaba da ardu'a da yardar ALLAH Komai zai Zama labari.

Wata ranar asaba Rana CE wacce bazaso taba mancewa da ita ba,ranar da Khadija ta tashi da matsanan cin ciwan Kai da jiri duk da tana jin matsanan cin ciwan hakan bai Hana ta tashi tayi musu kalaci ba ita da mijin ta.

Tana gamawa ta shirya musu suka zauna daniyar yin kalacan,sai me Khadija tana bude kular 
kamshin abincin ya daki hancin ta kankace me tafara yunkurin amai .

Shikuwa yunusa Yana gani Haka ya kankame ta tayi tayi ya saketa Dan ta samu damar zuwa toilet Amma Ina sai sake kankame ta yake.

Bata da wata mafita face tayi ajikin shi Haka kuwa tayi ta Amai kamar zata amayar da hanjin cikin ta.. Saida yaga Aman yadan tsagaita sannan ya dauke ta cak bai dire ta ko Inaba sai toilet wanke ta yayi tsaf..  sannan yafita Dan ya Bata damar yin wanka.

Bayan tafito taga harya gama gyara gurun kamar ma ba"ayi komai agurin ba, murmushi tayi tana Kara godewa ALLAH daya azurta ta da samun miji Mai tsananin kaunar ta .

Koma wa ɗaki tayi ta kwanta ba jimawa barci ya dauke ta,cikin barcin taji muryan shi Yana Kiran sunan ta da ardu'a abakin ta tashi tana rike Kai da ke barazanar tarwatsiwa.

Tallafo kanta yayi sannan yace tayi kokari ta tashi likita Yana faloYana jiran ta .
Kallon ma'maki ta bishi dashi...  sannan tace, " Kai Wannan dan karami zazzabin shine za'akira likita?
Ganin ya hada girar sama Dana kasa yasa tayi saurin mikewa Yana gaba tana biye dashi harsuka karasa falon.

Gaisheshi tayi cikin girmamawa sannan ya fara da duba tafukan hannayan ta sannan ya mata wasu Yan tambayoyi bayan ta amsa ya umarce ta da tayi fitsari ta kawo Masa.

Harza tayi gardama saidai ganin babu Hali yasa ta miƙe  Dan bin umarnin likita.

Ba'afi mintina biyu zuwa uku ba ta dawo dauke da fitsarin ta zuba cikin karamin kolba ta Mika Masa, kasancewar yazo da kayan aiki gaba daya kasancewar yunusa yariga da ya mishi bayanin Aman datayi.

Cikin kankanin lokaci yagama bin ciken shi sannna ya dube yunusa ya Mika Mishi hannu sukayi musabaha sannan yace"Ina ta yaka murna, matar ka tana dauke da juna biyu na tsawan sati shid....
Karar faduwar mutum da sukaji yasa yakasa karisa maganar sa,wazasu gani....

*The only thing i like about stones that come in my way is, once i pass them, they automatically become my MILESTONE.*😍😘



1⃣5⃣➖1⃣6⃣


*Bismillahi rahamanir Rahim*




______________Wa zasu gani suwaiba ce kwance ko Motsi batayi cikin sauri likitan ya karasa gunta ta... taɓa jikin ta yayi saidai jin zafin da jikin ta yayi shiyasa ya umarci yunusa da su kaita asibiti bazai iya duba  ta anan ba,kasan cewar baizo da kayan aiki ba.

Haka kuwa akayi Kiran mijin ta sukayi suka shaida Mishi abinda yake faruw.. a sannan suka sanar dashi asibitin da zaizo ya same su.

Itah kuwa Khadija Banda kuka babu abinda takeyi duk yanda yunusa yaso ta kwantar da hankalin ta Amma Abu ya ci tura haka dai ya hakura ya barta wata Kila idan tayi Mai isarta taji sassauci game da abinda take ji.

Suna isa asibitin akashigar da ita dakin taimakon gaggawa.lokacin mijin ta Wanda suke Kira da isah ya iso gurin,kana ganin shi basai anfaɗa maka ba kasan Yana cikin tashin hakalin.

  rike shi yunusa yayi lokacin da ya karaso gurin Yana tambayar su maka sudin rashin lafiyar matar shi,Dan shidai aiya sanin sa lafyar ta kalau yabar ta.

Yunusa zaiyi magana kenan sukaji anbuɗe ɗakin da aka shigar da ita,cikin sauri yunusa ya saki hannun isah ya nufi likitan Yana tambayar sa jikin nata,maimakon ya amsa masa sai ya Mika Mishi hannu da murmushi akan fuskar sa yace "congratulations.

Duk kansu suka sake kallon juna cike da mamaki isah yace,"kaga likita kamana bayani nine mijin ta,sake duban isah yace,"Ina ta yaka murna matar ka tana dauke da juna biyu na tsawan sati takwas.

Farin ciki agurin wannan ahali biyu  ba'acewa komai.

Koda suka dawo gida Saida suka haɗa yar karamar walima Dan nunawa ALLAH farin cikin su da wanna kyautar da ya musu.

Dangin yunusa kuwa kamar su mayar da Khadija ciki Dan tsananin murna har yarinya aka kawo Mata wacce zata rinka taya ta aikace aikace .

Idan suwaiba tazo gidan haka zata rinka kallon su abin gunin sha'wa,wani lokaci tayi kuka wani lokaci Kuma idan sunayi ta yita dariya.

Sam Khadija Bata so yanda dangin yunusa suke hanata aiki ba, daidai da buta idan zata dauka to sai ansamu Wanda zai karba ya kai mata toilet.

Haka dai suka cigaba da rai non cikin su,cikin so da kaunar juna duk abinda daya ta samu to batada wani buri daya wuce taga dayar ma ta samu .

Wata Rana Khadija tana zaune cikin gida ita kadai ta tsinci kanta da son zuwa gurin suwaiba kasancewar yau kusan kwanan su biyu Basu haɗu ba saidai kowace tana aikawa yar'uwa ta da sakon gaisuwa.

Mikewa tayi ta zari gelan ta, ta nufi gidan suwaiba,da sallama ta shigo cikin gidan saidai shiru babu amsa Jin shirun yayi yawa yasa ta shiga gidan da tunani Killa matar gidan tana makewaya.

Saidai me ganin suwaiba azaune shiya mattukar Bata ma'maki,cikin. Sauri ta nufin gurin ta tana Isa meza ta gani suwaiba ce zaune  hawaye wani na  korar wani,da alama tayi nisa cikin tunani Dan da alama Bata masan da zuwan ta ba.

Ciki sauri ta cire Mata ta gumin da tayi,sai asannan ne ta firit ta dawo ciki haiyacin ta sannan tafara kiciniyar share hawayan dake kan fuskar ta Wai Dan Kar Khadija ta fahimci halin da take ciki.

Murmushi Khadija tayi Wanda baiwuce iya leben bakin taba tace,"suwaiba dama kinbar hawayan ki,kindai na boye su.

Juyawa tayi zata fita daga gidan cikin sauri suwaiba ta riko hannun ta cikin kuka tace,"karki yi  fushi Dani Khadija,kisani babu abinda fushin ki zai haifar sai Karin damuwa agare ni.

Cikin fushi Khadija ta fisgi hannun ta tace,"da Ina da mahimmmanci agurin ki suwaiba da baki boyemin damuwar ki ba,Ashe dama yanda na dauke ki ba haka kika dauke niba ?

Banda girgiza Kai babu abinda suwaiba take yi hawaye kuwa kamar anbuɗe  fanfo tace,"kiyi hakuri Khadija zanfaɗa  miki duk abinda kike son sani dangane da damuwa ta,Jan hannuwan ta tayi suka shiga cikin dakin.

Duk kansu abakin gado suka zauna ta fara da cewa,"sunana suwaiba kamar yarda kika sani sunan mahaifina iliya mahaifiya ta Kuma hajara mu Yan asalin jahar Niger ne wato minna.

karamar  hukumar suleja, wata unguwa da ake Kira dawaki mu uku ne agun mahaifina nice bab'ba sai sani Wanda shine yake biye dani sai kuma karamar kanwar mu sadiya.

Mun taso cikin so da kaunar juna. mahaifina  sana'r dinki yake shiyasa baki taba rasa dinkin yayi ajiki na nakan rasa wanda yafi Sona atsakanin mahaifana, domin kuwa duk kansu  Basu da wani burin daya wuce su gasun kyautata min.

Haka Muka kasance cikin farin ciki arayuwar mu, saidai kamar yarda kowani bawa yasani babu wani abunda yake ɗorewa aduniyar Nan walau farin ciki ko akasin haka.

Ranar labara 31_7_2014 Rana CE wacce bazan taba mancewa da ita ba arayuwa ta, ranace da ta tafi da duk kannin farin ciki na ta musan yamin da akasin shi.

Da safiyan Ranar mahaifiya ta ta tashi da matsanan cin ciwan kai  Wanda yakai ta ga kwanciya muna ganin Haka hankalin kuwa ya tashi... domin takasan ce mace Mai juriya idan kaga ciwo ya kwantar daita to babu shakka ciwon yayi tsanani.

Cikin tashin hankali mahaifina yaje yasayo Mata magani saidai ,me cikin kankanin lokaci kaman nin ta yafara sauyawa ganin Haka yasa yafita da sauri ba'afi mintina biyar ba saigasu sundawo da likita,cikin sauri likitan yafara duba ta.

Saidai yakusa mintina talatin sannan yaju yo   ya kalli mahaifina Wanda tuni hawaye yagama wanke masa fuska yace,"malam iliya garin Yaya kuka Bari har wannan ciwon yayi Mata mumunar Kamu Haka ?

Cike da ma'maki yake kallon shi yace,"bangane ba likita wani ciwo kake magana akai.

Shi ɗinma kallo yake binshi dashi sannan yace maganar gaskiya shine tana da hawan jini Kuma ya  Mata mumunar kam..

Bai kai da karasa maganar Saba mu kaji tafara tari  da gudu naje na rungume ta Ina kuka na Bata ruwa kadan Tasha sannan takoma ta kwanta.

Kamar ance ta kwanta ko mintina biyar batayi da kwanciya tari ya sarke ta, wannan lokacin baba na yariga Ni kaiwa gunta.

Da karfi ta damke hannun shi kamar za'akwace Mata shi tace "mal..malam ga amanar yara na ka kulamin da su sannan...  Ina rokun ka alfarma idan kasamu Mata kayi aure kodan yaran Nan, sannan duk abinda kamin na yafe maka kaima Kuma Ina niman yafiyar k..

Bata kai ga karasa maganar ba tari ya sarke ta kankace me Rai yayi halin sa.

Fadan irin tashin hankalin da muka shiga Bata lokaci ne,Haka dai akayi Mata sutura  aka sa data da gidan ta na gaskiya.

Bayan anyi sadakar arba'in izuwa lokacin kowa ya koma gidan sa gida yarage daga mu saimu.

Makocin mune yashigo gidan ana kiran shi ba'ba audu da sallama yashigo gidan suka gaisa da baba sannan yace,"Nazo muyi wata Yar shawara ne ALLAH yasa banyi maka kasalan danba.

Murmushi yayi sannan yace,"haba malam audu aibabu irin wannan atsakanin mu.

Shidin ma murmushi yayi sannan yace,"dama maganar aure nazo inyi maka kaga gaskiya Zama Haka babu dadi.

Girgiza Kai yayi sannan yace,"idan nayi aure yanzu gaskiya kamar naci amanar hajara ne,shiyasa nake ganin abar maganar auran Nan kawai.

Rarrashin shi yashiga Yi harsai da yaciwo kain shi murmushin karfin hali yayi sannan yace,"to saidai wani hanzari ba gudu ba,gaskiya banida kudi..

Tunkan ya karasa maganar yace,"karka damu iliya nariga Dana samo maka Mata harna bada kudin sadaki Kuma ansaka Rana Nanda sati uku IN SHA ALLAH.

Banda kallon ma'maki babu abinda yake Binshi dashi sannan yace ,"nagode sosai ALLAH yabar zumunci.

Girgiza masa Kai yayi sannan yace,"babu Haka atsakanin mu yarinyar gidan malam iro ne zuwaira.
Yana faɗan  haka baitsaya Jin me ba'ba zaice ba yasa Kai yafita.


💖ya'ya nane ko miji na 💖

Bismillahi rahamanin Rahim

Cikin kankanin lokaci akayi bikin ba'ba na da inna zuwaira kamar yanda ba'ba na yace murinka Kiran ta.

Da farko ta rike mu hannun bibbiyu Bata so ta gammu cikin damuwa komai kankantar sa,saidai me bayan kwana biyu ta sauya gabaki ɗaya muguntar yanzu daban  na anjima daban.

Muguntar ta baya tashi sai Abba na yafita, duka zagi rashin abinci duk saita Mana,ga aiki kamar jakai muna gama wanna zata fito da wancan.

Dazarar abban mu yadawo kuwa zata rinka ririta mu, zaka rantsi da ALLAH Bata musguna mana.

Hakan bakaramar dadi yake yiwa abban mu ba saidai Abu daya ke daure Mishi kai,ramar da mukeyi tayi yawa ,da yayi  zatan rashin mahaifiyar mu ke damun mu saidai ganin abin take ci taki cinyawa yasa ya daukar wa kanshi alkawarin Nemo bakin zaran.

Wata ranar alhamis abban mu yafita kasuwa Yana fita Inna zuwaira ta fito Mana da kayan wanki tace sai nayi  kafin tabani Karin safe,Haka na kwashe wankin nafara ba'a kama hannun yaro cikin kankanin lokaci na gama Ina gamawa ta dauko wanke wanke waishima sainayi kafin tabani Karin,cikin kuka na Fara magiya ina cewa.

,"DAN ALLAH Inna ki taimakeni wallahi yunwa nake j..
Kokan na rufe baki ta rufeni da matsanan cin duka to karina ta daga ƙafa zatayi, rintsa ido nayi Ina jira naji tokari, jin shiru ne yasa na ɗago ido danganin dalilin tsayawar, abban mu nagani tsaye Yana zubar da hawaye.

Cikin sauri na tashi da gudu na boye abayan shi,ya janyo Ni ya rungume Ni Yana wani irin kuka Saida yayi Mai isar sa sannan ya juya ya kalli Inna zuwaira yace,"kije na barki da ALLAH .

Yana kaiwa Nan ya janyo Ni muka shiga ɗaki,daga ɗakin muka rinka jiyu muryar inna zuwaira tana kurma ihu tana cewa," kayi min Rai ka yafe min ka marya dani daki na wallahi bazan Kara ba.

Janyo wani farar takarda Abba na yayi ya fara rubutu cikin mintina kalilan ya gama, mikewa tsaye yayi,Ina ganin Haka cikin sauri na mike cikin kuka nace,"Abba karka tafi ka barmu wallahi kashemu zatayi.

Girgiza Kai yayi hawaye na zuba akan fuskar sa yace,"babu inda zantafi kinji suwaiba ki jira Ni Ina zuwa,

sauke ajiyar zuciya nayi na koma  na zauna,shikuwa yanufi wajan dakin,Yana zuwa ya same ta tana ta birgima,Mika Mata takar dar yayi sannan yace,"na sakeki saki daya idan kin samu miji kiyi aure .

Kurma ihu tayi shikuwa dakin da nake ya dawo mukayi zama mu.

Tunbayan tafiyar Inna zuwaira farin cikin gidan mu ya dawo kamar da saidai rashin uwa ba abinda mutum zai mance lokaci ɗaya bane.

Muna cikin Haka wata Rana muna zaune muna Hira da Abba na kasan cewar ranar bantafi islamiya ba shikuma sani sun fita tare da abokin shi Salim  yaron makkucin mu.

Sadiya tana wasa atsakar gida Hira muke tsakanin ya da uba har aka Kira sallar magrib,duk kanmu
tashi mukayi Dan sauke farali.

Bayan na idar ne nashiga tunanin inda sani yashiga bayan bai taba kaiwa wannan lokacin awaje ba,Haka dai najira har akayi sallar isha'i sannan Abba yashigo agaigauce na gabatar da sallar hankali na Yana Kan sani.

Fita nayi na sanar da Abba rashin dawowar sani gida tambaya ta yayi lokacin da suka fita,sanar dashi nayi tun karfi biyu,cike da ma'maki yafita Dan duba gidan malam Mustafa mahaifin salim.

Cikin kankanin lokaci saiga shi ya dawo Saida kana ganin shi kasan babu lafiya,cikin sauri na Isa gare shi nace,"Abba kagan shi Ina yake,duk alokaci daya na jero Mishi tambar,Mai makon amsa,sai ma Zama dayayi hawaye na bin fuskar sa.

Jingina jiki na nayi Nima na Soma kuka Mai sauti sabanin shi da yake zubda hawaye,Saida mukayi Mai isarmu sannan ya kalle Ni da jajayan idanuwan sa yace,"saidai hakuri suwaiba sani ya rigamu gidan gaskiya.

Ai bai karasa maganar ba na sumee,Saida nashafe awanni shidda sannan na farfado,dube dube na farayi, Dan ganin mutane cike da gidan, idanuwan kowa akai na,cikin sauri na mike tsaya Dan tunuwa da abinda ke faruwa da gudu na nufi hanyar kofar gida Ina Kiran sunan sani.

Cikin sauri aka rikoni duk Wanda ke wajan Saida ya tausaya min.

Ashe Wai motace ta bige su,ayayin da su kazo tsalake titi muguwar hatsare suka samu Wanda sai dai aka tsince namar jikin su Dan Haka tattara wa kawai akayi aka sa dasu da gidan su na gaskiya bayan an sallace gawar.

Bayan rasuwar sani da Yan kwanaki Abba ya dawo da Inna zuwaira gidan sakamakon Kiran shi da manyan anguwa sukayi suka bashi hakuri da Kuma nuna Mishi illar rashin mace agidan ne ya jawo fitar sani daga gida batare da ansan inda yaje ba .

Badan Yana so ba yadawo da ita gidan da sharadin duk lokacin da yakama ta da aikata laifi makaman cinyar wannan to abakin auran ta.

Amsawa tayi da ladabi sannan ta dauki alkawarin bazata sake aikata irin Haka ba.

Saidai?me tunda Inna zuwaira ta dafa abinci ta bawa abba na shikenan na kasa Gane kanshi tun Ina daukar abin wasa, har abin ya zarcewa tunani na.

Wani lokacin ma idan na gaishe shi baya amsawa Saidai ya daga min hannu.

Aiki kuwa babu abinda Inna zuwaira keyi agidan komai ni keyi,Dana gaji na sanarwa da Abba na...  Amma saime, rufeni da faɗa yayi kamar zai dake Ni haryana ikiraran idan banyi aiki ba karta bani abinci.

Ranar kuwa bakamar kuka naci ba, washa garin ranar sadiya ta tashi da matsanan cin zazabi, daukar ta nayi na kaita gun Inna zuwaira alokacin Abba bai fita kasuwa ba na shaida Mata rashin lafiyar sadiya,bankai ga karasa maganar ba Abba yafito Yana cewa.

"To dakika zo kika faɗa  Mata itace zata warkar da ita? KO itace ta daura Mata cuta?maza ki bace min Da gani tunkan na babbala ki shashasha kawai.

Juyawa nayi na koma zaure Wanda ayanzu shine ɗakin kwanan mu nida sadiya, kwantar da ita nayi sannan nafito na shiga gidan makkucin mu,da sallama  na shiga gidan ,matar mai gidan ba'ba mairo ce tafito tana ganina ta sake murmushi tace,"a a Lailai za'ayi ruwa da kankara yau ga suwaiba cikin gidan nan.

Murmushin karfin hali nayi sannan nace,"kiyi hakuri iya ta, wallahi aiyuka ne su kamin yawa Amma zan rinka shigowa IN SHA ALLAH .

Girgiza Kai tayi tace,"nasani suwaiba nasan duk abinda ke faruwa Saidai Ina Mai shawartar ki da ki cigaba da hakuri wata Rana sai labari kinji,

Nod dakai kawai nayi.

sannan tace,"Ina sadiya?

Sai alokacin na tuna da abinda yakawo Ni gidan,cikin sauri na shaida Mata abinda ke tafe Dani ,aiban Kai ga karasa maganar ba ta zari gelanta muka nufi gidan mu,muna zuwa daidai Abba zaifita muka hadu,gaisawa sukayi cikin girmama juna harya Sakai zaifice tace,"malam iliya kaban ma'maki mattuka saidai kasani ALLAH Yana kallon duk abinda kuke aikatawa.

Kallon ma'maki yake binta dashi sannan yace,"mairo bangane abinda kike magana akai ba,wani irin kallo ta warga Mishi sannan tace,"yanzu saboda ALLAH da abinda zaka sakawa hajara kenan?ka kasa kula Mata da amanar da ta barmak...

Tsawar daya daka Mata shiya hanata karasa maganar yace,"Ina ruwan ki da shiga sabgar gida na oooo ok nagane dama kune masu zuwa kuna yadani abakin mutane ko?

To wallahi mairo kifita a idon..karar da muka ji  zuwaira tayi shiya Hana shi karasa maganar cikin sauri duk kanmu muka karasa shiga zauran.

Saidai Mee sadiya ce take wani irin Mika kunfa na fita abakin ta ,da gudu na karasa gurin ta 
girgiza ta nashiga yi, Ina kuka Mai tsoma zuciya nace,"Dan ALLAH karki tafi ki barni acikin irin wannan duniyar Mai ciki da kunci da kunar zuciya ,ALLAH kacece Ni kamar yarda kaceci ANNABIN ka yunus daga cikin kifi.

Shiru nayi sakamakon nauyi danaji tayi ko Ina na jikin ta yasake, girgiza ta nashiga yi da karfi ina kirin sunan ta....  sadiya sadiya sadiya nandanan nashiga rera Mata wakar da mahaifiyar mu take Mata lokacin da take raye.

dan ta farka,sadiya sadiya halima Hali dubu akwai na dauka akwai na zubar wa sadiya Yar autar ma'ma kuka na fashe dashi danaji babu ko alamar motsi ajikin ta bare NASA ran zata buɗe  ido.

Ba'ba mairo ce ta matso kusa Dani tace"kiyi hakuri suwaiba ke kuma irin Taki jarabawar kenan,kiyi imani kisani ALLAH Yana kallon ki Kuma shi zai jebanci lamuran ki.

Nandanan naji na samu karfin guiwa na Mika Mata gawar sadiya, sannan na dauko yagaggiyar tabarmar da muke kwanciya akai na  kwantar da ita akai,cikin mintuna kalilan mutane suka cika gida ana ta yani jimami.

Saida na bawa kuwa tausayi ganin yanda nayi juriya, shikuwa Abba kuka yake kamar karamin yaro saidai babu Wanda yabi ta kanshi kasancewar kowa yasan halin da muke ciki.

Tunbayan rasuwar sadiya ba'ba mairo kullum saita Aiko min da abinci hakan kuwa bakaramar bakata ran Inna zuwaira yake ba... saidai babu bakin magana, kasancewar yanzu Abba baya Bari Ina aiki kamar da.

Ana cikin Haka yaron Inna mairo yazo Wanda yake aiki agarin kaduna ana kiran shi Isa, zuwan Isa garin bakaramar daɗi yayi min ba Dan munsaba da juna.

Wata ranar asabar ina zaune cikin ɗaki na Dan tunbayan rasuwar,Abba yace bazan Kuma kwana azaure ba, shiguwa yayi ya samu guri daga nisa ya zauna yace,"suwaiba ki gafarce Ni na cutar dake Kuma nasan ALLAH bazai barni ba,sai yanzu  nagane sihirin da zuwaira ta yimin,mutane da yawa sunsha gayamin Amma banyar da ba sai yanzu Dana jida kunne na.

ALLAH ya jiyar danine sakamakon wata ƙawar ta da tazo ɗazu naji su sunata Hira da shewa,cikin sanda na nufi dakin Dan Dama Ina zargin ta Akan hakan,Ina zuwa kofar dakin kuwa naji kawar tana cewa,"dama na faɗa  miki   aikin malamin kamar yankan wuka yake.

Shewa suka sakeyi sannan tace,"gaskiya Kam,Kinga yanda yake rawar kafa akaina kuwa hmmm kedai Bari abun ba'acewa komai.

Ta bata tayi tace,"nikuwa ya labarin yaran sa?

Mi kewa tsaye tayi sannan ta kwashe labarin duk abinda yafaru damu tafaɗa mata.
Shewa suka sakeyi akaro na barkatai.

Daga nanne nasake tambatawa kaina zargin da ake mata,Kuma Ina fata Zaki yafemin.

Girgiza Kai nayi cikin kuka nace,"bakayi min komai ba Abba idan ma kamin bansani  ba,to na yafe maka.

Ajiyar zuciya yayi sannan yace,"dama kuwa Ina so na tambaye ki.

Dago kaina nayi ina kallon shi saidai bance komai ba.

Cigaba da maganar yayi yace,"yaron Nan Isa soyaya kuke ne?

Sunkuyar da kaina nayi kasa Ina murza yatsan hannu na,Dan tunowa da maganar da yafaɗa min jiya Wanda hakan yasa nayi barci cikin farin ciki.

Yace"Ina sonki suwaiba Kuma wallahi da aure nake son ki,kasa tsayuwa  a gurin nayi saboda kunyar sa da ta lullube ni, nashiga gida da gudu.

Kiran sunana da ya kuma yi yasa na dawo daga tunanin Dana shilla 

Cikin kankanin lokaci aka daura aure na da isa,farin ciki agurin mahaifina ba'a magana,ba'afi kwana uku dayin auran muba muka daga zuwa kaduna inda isah yake aiki,nayi kuka aranar da zambar gari na, mahaifata zuwa duniyar da bansan kuwa ba.

Zaman lafiya muke sosai ba mufi wata ɗaya da zuwa ba sakon rasuwar mahaifina ya riske ni,kaɗuwa iya kaduwa mutuwar ta girgiza Ni, Haka muka tattara muka nufi garin  suleja.

Bayan ardu'a bakkwai muka tattara ina mu Ina mu,muka dawo gida.

Sauke ajiyar zuciya suwaiba tayi lokacin da tagama bawa Khadija labarin ta....

__________Kuka suke gabaki ɗayan su aka rasa wanda zai bawa ɗan uwan sa hakuri..  saida sukayi mai isar su sannan..  Khadija ta ɗago ido wanda tuni suka canza launi tace"da farko ina mai baki hakurin rashin mahaifan ki da yan'uwan ki.



"Sannan ki sani duk wani mumini ALLAH yakan jarabce shi da abubuwa da dama dan gwada imanin sa,dan haka nake ƙara baki hakuri...  sannan ina mai tabbatar miki da yardar ALLAH bakin cikin ki ya kare karshan wahalar  ki  tazo.. 

"Ina so ki ɗauke ni tamkar yar'uwar ki wacce kuka fito ciki ɗaya,ni kuma na miki alkawari zan riƙe ki da amana.

Suwaiba banda hawaye babu abinda takeyi sai yanzu taji kamar an sauke mata wani nauyi da ta daɗe tana fama dashi akirjin ta.. tashi tayi ta rungume ta tana kukan farin ciki.. 

Janye ta tayi sannan tasa bakin gelenta ta share mata hawaye tana cewa ,"kukan ya isa haka karki illata min ɗa.

Ita kamma abin dariya ya bata wato hartasan namiji zata haifa kenan.

Wunin rana Khadija tayi shine atare da suwaiba girki ma agidan sukayi abinsu suka ci.. 

Sai bayan sallar La'asar ta tashi ta nufi gida zuciyar ta cike fal da tausayin suwaiba.

Haka rayuwa taci gaba da gara wa har gashi yau Khadija da suwaiba sunshiga watar haihuwar su,
Zaune take abakin gado tana safa da marwa tare da yarfe hannu saboda tsananin ciwon maran da ta tashi dashi. 

Malam isah ne yashigo rike da wata farar gorar swan yamiƙa mata da alamu rubutu ya amsu mata yana cewa"sannu kinji suwaiba ALLAH ya raba lafiya.

Nod da kai Kawai tayi.

Harzai fita ta dan ciji lebe tace"malam daka taimaka kakira min Khadija.

Dawowa yayi ɗakin yace'suwaiba wai yaushe kika koyi kafiya ne?nafaɗa miki banida kudin da zankai ki asibiti..  kinfi kowa sanin halin da nake ciki,kuma kinsan mutanan nan idan suka zo cewa zasuyi muje asibiti..

akarshe idan nafaɗa musu banida Kudi cewa zasuyi zasu biya,kuma kinsan irin ƙwakarin da suke damu,gaskiya kiyi hakuri nariga da na'aikawa matar makocin
mu tananan zuwa zata taimaka miki har ALLAH ya raba lafiya kinji.

Nod da kai kawai tayi a zuciyar ta tana ardu'a ALLAH yakawo sanadin da Khadija zata zo gidan.

Tasha wahala sosai dan gabaki ɗaya ta gama galabaita gashi matar bata zo ba shikuma tunda yafita haryanzu baidawo ba.

Jin muryar Khadija tayi kamar daga sama,sauke wata kakkarfar ajiyar zuciya tayi tana yiwa ALLAH godiya daya turo mata da Khadija.

Ita kuwa tunda ga waje take kwalla mata kira tana cewa"assalamun alaikum yar'uwa kina ina,jin ba'a amsa sallamar ba yasa tayi saurin shigewa ɗakin dan dama suna saran haihuwar ta cikin satin nan da yardar ALLAH.

Aikuwa tana shiga ta tarar da ita tana nishi ga ruwa yana bin kafar ta da gudu ta karasa gurin ta tana mata sannu.

Ganin jini yafara biyu bayan ruwan yasa ta fita waje da gudu dan samo motar da zata kaita asibiti tana fita suka ci karo da unguwar zomar .

Cikin firgita tace,dan ALLAH ba'ba ki taimaka ki lurar min da ita,bara naje na samo motar da za'akai ta asibiti.

Katse ta tayi tace"kudai yaran zamani wallahi kunyi asara,yanzu saboda rashin hankali irin naki saiki dauki tsirancin ki ki kaiwa wani kato yana kallo alhalin ba maharramin ki bane.

"To karma kisha wahalar niman mota dan mijin ta cewa yayi babu inda zaikai ta saidai ta haihu agida ,yanzu shine yakira ni yace naje na karbi haihuwar.

Tsayawa tayi tana binta da kallon ma'maki.

Ganin bata da niyar magana yasa tace,"kin wani saki baki kina kallo na ni kinga tafiya ta,

Shigewa gidan tayi bata jira jin me Khadija zata ceba.

Khadija tana ganin haka itama tabi ta cikin gidan.

Suna shiga suka samu suwaiba amim miƙe banda nishi babu abinda take kankace mi, saiga kan yaro yana fitowa rintsa  idonta tayi  tana yiwa ALLAH kirari.

Khadija bata buɗe ido ba saida taji ba'bar tana cewa" alhamdullah barkan ka da zuwa kai masha ALLAH.

Bude ido tayi cikin sauri ta isa gurin, karbar yaron tayi ta sakashi cikin towel sai da me kankace yaron yafara amai ruwa na fita ta hanci ta baki.

Cikin kiɗima ta nunawa ungo zomar ita ma abin bakaramar daga mata hankali yayi ba.

Ganin haka yasa Khadija fita da shi da gudu aikuwa suka ci karo da isa ciki kiɗima take nuna mishi yaron.

Baƙaramar  tashin hankali yashi ga ba...  cikin sauri suka karasa fitowa kofar gidan sunyi sa'a kuwa suna fita dan adaidai sahu suka samu,faɗa masa asibitin da zaikai su sukayi.

Har suka kai asibitin yana amai,da ker'aka karbi su danganin yaron sabuwar haihuwa ne kuma da alamu haihuwar gida ne .

Abakin ɗakin da aka shigar dashi muka tsaya kowa da abinda yake sakawa aranshi,muna cikin haka saiga likitan ya fito yana yarfe gumin dake kan fuskar sa,

Cikin sauri suka isa inda yake har haɗa baki suke gurin tambayar sa jikin yaron,kallon su yayi cikin kulawa yace,ina uban yaron?cikin  sauri isa yace shine,

Aikuwa  likitan yafara faɗa ta inda yake shiga bata nan yake fita ba,sai A karshi yafaɗa mana abinda saida na dur kusa akan guiwoyi na saboda wata bakar azaba dana ji tun daga kaina har tafin kafana.

Nanda nan baya na da marata suke rike jin maganar likitan nake tana amsa kuwa akunne na kamar yanzu yake faɗa,to gashi saboda sakaci irin na ku, yaron yarasa ransa sakamakon ruwa da yasha a ciki.

Wata uwar kara ta sake jin marata ta kuma rikewa,cikin sauri suka shigar da ita dakin haihuwa.

Isa kuwa yana jin haka ya dafe kanshi da bangon ɗakin yana kuka mai cin rai,ganin halin da take ciki yasa ya tsayar da kukan ya kira yunusa ba jimawa yunusa ya iso gurin, hankalin shi ba karamar tashi yayi ba dajin rasuwar yaron da kuma musabbabin hakan.

Shiga ɗakin da aka ije gawar sukayi yunusa ne yayi karfin halin buɗe masa fuska,kamannin yaron sak uban sa,shikuwa isa yana ganin fuskar yaron ya fashe da wata irin kuka yana cewa"ki gafarce ni suwaiba na kasa ciki alkawarin dana ɗauka kigafarce ni.

Ganin kukan yaki ci yake cinyewa yasa yunusa lallashin sa, huɗuba sukayi mishi da sunan mahaifin isa wato HASHIM.

Suna cikin ɗakin wata ma'ikaciyar jinya ta shigo take  shaida musu Khadija ta haifi da namiji da uwan da dan duk suna cikin koshin lafiya,amsawa sukayi duk kansu fuskar nan ba yabo ba falllasa suka nufi dakin hutu da aka ijeye ta.

tana ganin su ta tashi da gudu ta rungume yunusa tana kuka mai tsuma zuciyar duk wani mai sauraro.

Bai lallashe taba saida tayi mai isarta...  sannan yaɗago fuskar ta yace"shin Khadija kinyi fushi da hukuncin da uban giji ya zartar ne akan bawan sa?shin kedin ma kin kubutane daga mutuwar?dabarar kice da kika haihu lafiya? Wani tabbaci kike dashi na cewar zaki kara minti daya arayuwar ki?

Banda girgiza kai da ta keyi babu abinda takeyi,yana gamawa ta share hawayan ta da kuma daukar alkawarin bazata sake kuka ba.

Sai asannan ne take tambayar isa inda aka ajiye gawar.

Rike mata hannu yunusa yayi suka shiga ɗayan ɗakin kasan cewar suna fuskantar juna ne,ai tana ganin shi ta sake barke wa da sabon kuka suna hada ido da yunusa ta kama baki tayi shiru,saida hawaye daki ban kuncin ta.

Isa ne ya kalle ta yana kara jaddada kaunar da sukewa junan su yace'"nayi masa huɗuba da hashim yanzu zamu tafi gida ayi mishi sutura.

Aibata san lokacin da ta isa jikin yunusa ba tana sauke ajiyar zuciya tace"ina niman wata alfarma ALLAH yasa zakamin.

Murmushi yayi dan baya raba dayan biyu akan maganar suwaiba ne yace"ina jinki.

Kara shaga Jikin shi tayi sannan tace" yaron can nakeso amayar masa da sunan wannan  .

Cikin fargita dajin maganar ta isa ya dago kai danjin abinda yunusa zaifaɗa saidai me,murmushi ne akan fuskar sa yace"indai abinda zai saka matata  farin ciki  kenan angama.

Rungume shi tayi harda sumbatar kuncin shi saboda tsabar farin ciki shafta mance da isa agurin....

cikin ƙanƙanin lokaci aka sallame su suka nufi gida, izuwa lokacin sakon mutuwar  yaro ya'isa kunnuwar jama'a.



Koda suka isa gida suntarar kofar gidan da mutane ana jiran isowar su,karbar gawar akayi daga hannun yunusa aka shigar da ita zauran gida Dan yi mishi sutura.

Ita kuwa tana shiga ta tarar da suwaiba da sauran makuta suna zaune wanda idan ba'anfaɗa maka ba bazaka taba cewa itace akayiwa mutuwa ba.

Tsayawa tayi daga bakin kofa ta kallon ta,ita dinma  ita take kallo hawayen idota kuwa sam sunki bata hadan kai sai zuba suke tana kokarin mayar dasu.

Kamar sai alokacin suwaiba ta hangi babu ciki ajiki ta sakamakon dangin yunusa da suka amshi yaron tun akofar gida suka nufi gida dashi.

Aikuwa cikin sauri ta taso take tambayar ta ina ciki kamar dama jira take tayi mata magana, rungume ta tayi kamar za'asace mata ita tace,"kiyi hakuri suwaiba kisani bawai ALLAH baya sonki bane yake jarabtar ki da abubuwa kiyi imani kiyi imani kinji.

Kasa daurewa tayi itama ta rushe da kuka,kuka mai tattare da tarin damuwa takeyi,saida tayi mai isar ta,shigowar da akayi da jariri yana risgar kuka yasa tayi saurin tsaida kukan ta,tana kallon Khadija ita dinma ita take kallo ganin batada niyar karbar yaron yasa tasa hannu ta karbe shi tana murmushi ganin kaman ninshi ɗaya da Khadija.

Tace,"Sister yaron badai kyau ba,kinga bama wannan ba bara na kai miki ruwa toilet kiyi wanka kinga yana ta kuka kifito ki shayar dashi.

Mamaki ne falcike da zuciyoyin mutanan dake ɗakin dan suwaiba komawa tayi kamar ita ma basa bon haihuwa tayi ba.

Haka aka yiwa gawar sutura aka sadashi da gidan sa na gaskiya saidai muce (ALLAH)yabata wani.

Banda shan nono dake hada hashim da mahaifiyar sa babu abinda ke haɗasu kasancewar gabaki daya komai nashi yakuma hannun ta tun bayan da yunusa ya bukaci subar gida haya sudawo cikin gidan sa...  amma bangaran su daban.

Da farko isa kin amincewa yayi saidai ganin da gaski mutanan suke sonsu tsakani da ALLAH yasa ya amince suka dawo gidan da zama,haka zaman su yaci gaba da kasancewa cikin aminci da kaunar juna.

abu daya ke damun gabaki daya ahali biyu wanda bai wuce rashin sake samun haihuwa ba  har yau gashi hashim yana da shekara tara aduniya,izuwa lokacin kuwa kasa banbance mahaifiyar sa yake atsakanin Khadija da suwaiba,saidai yafi sabawa da suwaiba dan Khadija akwai kawaici.

Duk lokacin da Khadija tayiwa suwaiba zancan haihuwa saidai tayi murmushi tace,"ki daina damuwa Khadija" ALLAH yana sane damu,ta haka take kawar da maganar, tun Khadija ta nayi har ta gaji ta zubawa  sarautar ALLAH ido,dan shi buwayi ne gagara misali.

Bayan shekara daya lokacin hashim yana da shekaru goma aduniya abubuwa da dama sun faru ciki harda mutuwar ba'ba mairo mahaifiyar isa saidai muce (ALLAH)yajikan ta DA Rahama .

suwaiba ce zaune a falo tana kallon labarai ita DA mutumin Nata Hashim..  khadija ta shigo da sallama saidai sam suwaiba bata amsa ba kasancewar kallon kawai takeyi amma gabaki daya hankalin ta ba'awajan yake ba,cikin sauri khadija ta karasa gun ta.

ciri mata tagumin da tayi ne yadawo da ita daga duniyar da ta shalla tayi murmushi tana mai duban fuskar ta tace"yaushe kika shigo?

Ita dinma kallon ta take tace"au yaushe zaki sani tunda kinyi nisa cikin tunani.

Sunkuyar da kai tayi saboda sam bata so khadija tasan rashin haihuwar ta na damun ta.

Murmushi tayi wanda iya kansa leben baki tace,"dole ai kinsan dan adam ba arabashi da tunani.

, "Haka ne saidai ba'aso tayi yawa,domin takan zama cuta.

Muskutawa suwaiba tayi tace,"kinga abar wannan maganar dama yanzu nake shirin kawo miki mutumin nake dan shinayi,bata jira jin amsar ta ba ta wuce ciki da sauri dan bata so hawayen da take boyewa su zuba agaban khadija,saida tashiga cikin kitchen  sannan ta dan zubda hawayee ko zata samu saukin abinda take ji.

Debo mata fatan waken tayi ta ije mata  ita kuwa kamar dama jira take ta dauka cikin sauri ta deba zata  kai baki saidai mee wani irin abu taje yataso mata ai da gudu ta nufi hanyar toilet amai take kamar zata amayar da hanjin cikin ta,ganin haka yasa suwaiba da gudu ta nufi toilet din tana tambayar ta ko lafiya.

Duban ta tayi tace nikam bansani ba duk abinda naci sai nayi aman shi,cikin son gasgata abinda take zargi tace"tun yaushe rabon da kiga al'adar ki?

Murmushi tayi tana kallon ta dan ita kam tambayar ma dariya ya bata tace"suwaiba me kike tunani ?

Bakomai nidai kiban amsa ta kawai.

Sa hannun ta abaki tayi tana wulkita ido alamar son tunowa,cikin lokaci kalilan ta waro ido waje tana kallon ta ita dinma kallon ta take,sannan tace aiya nazari na yanzu nakai wata uku .

Waro ido suwaiba tayi sannan ta rungume ta tana dariyar farin ciki,ita dai khadija binta kawai take da ido tace"wannan farin cikin kuma na menene haka.

Kallon ta tayi tace"yanzu kina nufin kice bakisan kina dauke da juna biyu ba .

Girgiza kai tayi tace"kin mance wani lokaci al'ada tana min haka?kin mance bayan na haifi hashim da shikara uku nayi missin al'adata nakusan wata shida,duk kanku lokacin kun dauka ciki ne dani saida muka je asibiti tukunna aka shaida muku bana da komai kuma babu wata cuta dake damu na .

Cikin hanzari suwaiba tace"saidai lokacin baki amai kuma bakijin kasala ko?

Shiru tayi na dan lokaci sannan tace, " gaskiyar ki kam amma kai ni bani da komai kuma bazan samu ciki ba saikin samu saikin samar ma hashim kani ko mata.

Jin muryar hashim sukayi abayan su yana cewa,"wai da gaske ummi na zaki haifamin mata.

Sugunnawa tayi daidai sitin shi tace"koda baka samu mata ba hashim mummy zata samar maka kanwa ta nuna khadija.

Bubbuga kafa ya shigayi kankace me hawayee yafara zarya afuskar sa yace"ni bawai bana son kanwa bane..  amma nafison mata kamo hannun ta yayi yace"ummi kimin alkawarin zaki haifarmin mata.

Murmushin karfin hali tayi tace"karka damu hashim zan haifar maka mata..  amma saika daina kuka sannan duk lokacin da kayi sallah karin ka ardu'a ALLAH yakawo mata kaji.

Nod da kai kawai yayi,sannan yace amma ummi baki faɗamin  sunan taba.

Dariya sukayi atare suna mamakin maganar hashim kamar wani babba,sannan tace"aikai zaka zabarwa matar ka suna.

Daga kai sama yayi kamar mai son tuno wani abu sannan yace,"yauwa ummi sunan matata Maryam.

Dariya suka yi atare sannan suwaiba ta juya gun khadija tace,bara muyi sallah saimu je asibiti aduba ko.

To kawai tace dan ita kanta ta fara zargin ciki ne da ita .

```SOYAYYA kamar tsuntsuwa take takan yadda zango aduk zuciyar wanda taso, babu ruwanta da nasa barka, wanene kai?  ita dai idan ta samu mafaka shine cikar burinta, bazaka taɓa sanin mene ne farin ciki ko akasin hakan ba sai kana soyayyah...  SOYAYYA tana da girman gaske,...  tana sanya zuciya da ruhi su kasance cikin yananyi na jindaɗi da akasin haka...  bazaka taɓa gane mai kalmar SO yake nufi ba sai alokacin da ka\kika fara SOYAYYA.```



1⃣9⃣➖2⃣0⃣


________Saida
suka gama aikin dake gabansu sannan suka nufi asibiti,anan ne aka tambatar musu da khadija tana ɗauke da juna biyu.

Murna agun suwaiba ba'a magana sosai take nuna soyayar ta da abinda ke cikin khadija ganin haka yasa kullum Hashim cikin mita yake ummin shi taga sabon ciki Ta mance dashi.

Cikin Khadija yana kaiwa wata tara Ta haifu diyar ta mace kyakwa ranar suna taci sunan mahaifiyar Khadija wato safina.

Bakaramar kulawa Hashim yake bawa safnah ba kullum suna manne da juna dake shin nono ke mayar da safnah hannun khadija.

Ranar DA safnah ta cika shekara daya aduniya...  suka shirya karamar walima,tun misalin karfe goma na safe suka fara girke girke abinda za'aci Dan karfe biyu suke son farawa.

Suwaiba dai aiki kawai ta keyi... amma sam bata jin dadin jikin ta, tambayar duniyar nan Khadija tayi mata amma amsar daya ce,babu komai Kawai dai gajiya ce.

Karfi daya jama'a harsun Fara cika Gida saidai babu inda khadija bata duba Ba...  amma KO maikama da suwaiba bata gani Ba.

Hakan bakaramar tayar mata da hankali yayi ba,nan danan mutanan dake gurin suka fara tambayar ta lafiya ganin gabaki daya tana niman fita daga haiyacin Ta.

Tambayar ta suke amma ko kulasu batayi ba ta nufi shashin suwaiba akaro na barkatai tana shiga kakarin amai dataji ne Ya hanata karasa sallamar dake bakinta tanufi toilet dinda gudu.

Abinda tagani bakaramar tayar Mata DA hankali yayi ba,suwaiba ce kwance tana amai Kamar zata amayar da hanjin cikin ta,amai bana komai Ba illah zallar jini.

Kwalla karar da Khadija tayi shiya jawu hankalin mutanan dake gurin suka shigo ɗakin akidime,suma ganin abinda yake faruwa kanwar yunusa ta umarce suda sufito da ita akaita asibiti.

Suna isa asibiti aka shigar da ita dakin taimakon gaggawa.

Likitoci sun jima akanta kafin daga bisani sukayi nasarar shawo kan al'amarin suka fito.

izuwa lokacin Kuwa asibitin cike yake da mutani daga dangin Khadija harna yunusa kasan cewar kowa yasan tsananin shakuwar dake tsakanin wadanan ahalin.
Ganin likitan yasa Khadija tashi cikin sauri Ta nufe shi tana tambayar sa.

"Likita yajikin Nata ina fata babu abinda yasame Ta,saikuma Ta fashe da kuka.

Kallo ɗaya likitan ya mata ya tabbatar DA idan yafaɗa Mata matsalar a Nan.to bakaramar Marsala za asamu ba.

Murmushi yayi sannan yace",Malam ki daina kuka ba mutuwa tayi Ba hasalima yanzu tasamu sauki yanzu haka barci take zoki gani.

Binshi tayi cikin ɗaki saida taga numfashin suwaiba na sauka daidai sannan tasamu Dan natsuwa yashige ta.
Likitan yace"yanzu dai kin yarda tasamu Sauki ko?

Nod dakai kawai tayi kamar karamar yarinya.

Saida yayi dariya sannan yace"ya kuke da ita.

Kallon shitayi sannan tace"Yaya tace.

OK dama dalilin da yasa na tambaye ki,saboda aiya binciken da mukayi mungano tana dauke da juna biyu natsawan wata UK...

Kabbarar da khadija tayi saida likitan ya razana kallon ta yake yadda take shafe fuskar suwaiba fuskar ta kuwa  kamar gonar audiga.

Jinjina kai yayi dan baisan yanda zatayi ba idan tasan dayar matsalar.

Gyarar murya yayi sanan yace"saikuma Abu na biyu,tsayawa tayi tana sauraron shi haryanzu da murmushi dauke afuskar ta,gaskiya nayi mattukar mamaki yanda naga tsansar kauna da kike mata...  amma kuka kasa Samar Mata da maganin cutar dake addaban ta na tsawan lokaci.... 

Wanda ahalin da aka ciki cutar tayi Mata mummunar kamu yanda koda za'abata magani saidai tasha Na dan wani lokaci.

Kan ya gama magana tuni hawayee yagama wanke mata fuska tace,"Dan ALLAH likita ka gaggauta faɗamin abinda ke damun yar'uwa ta.

Shiru yayi na Dan wani lokaci sannan yace wato inaso ki kwantar da hankalin ki,ki dauka kaddarar ta kenan, wato tana fama da ciwan zuciya na tawan shekaru Dan yanda naga ciwan yaci jikin ta hakan ne ya tabbatar mana da ciwan Ya dade ajikin Ta.

Banda kuka babu abinda Khadija take yi take,"likita yanzu kana nufin ako wani lokaci Zata iya mutuwa kenan?

Murmushi yayi sannan yace"ki kwantar da hankalin ki, kisani koda ciwo KO babu zata iya mutuwa kema haka nima,to kinga idan haka ne banga abin damuwa ba,ki kwantar da hankalin ki,kawai dai ina shawartar Ku da Ku kula da ita duk abinda take so kuyi kukarin Kuga kun Samar Mata.

, "IN SHA ALLAH,ta nayi tana share hawayee,bayan fitar likita ta juyu gun suwaiba, saidai Mee hawayee ne face face akan fuskar ta,cikin sauri ta karaso gunta tace,"meyasa kikamin haka suwaiba meyasa kika boye min wanna ba'bar al'amarin.

Rike hannun ta tayi tace,"ki gafarce ni ni kaina bansan da wannan ciwan Ba sai yanzu,amma ina so ki Kantar da hankalin ki Khadija rayuwa DA mutuwa duk ahannun ALLAH take.

kwanan su uku aka sallamu su suka dawo Gida,izuwa lokacin kowa yasan Khadija tana da ciki sai dai sun boye ciwan dake damun ta.

Haka suka ci gaba da rainon cikin cike da soyayar Hashim kuwa sai anyi da gaske yake tafiya makaran ta dancewa yake babu inda zai tafi yabar Matar sa,duk lokacin da yafaɗi  haka ALLAH kadai yasan halin da suke shiga...  saidai suna kokarin boye damuwar su Dan karya fahimci halin da suke ciki.

Wata rana lokacin cikin suwaiba yana wata takwas malam Isah ya shirya Yace zaitafi suleja ganin yan'uwa bayan yagama shiri yafito yana yiwa suwaiba sallama ta tashe da ker zatayi mishi rakeya ganin tashin nane man bata walaha yace,"kiyi zaman ki suwaiba,dama kuwa Ina so nayi miki nasiha.

suwaiba ki kula da rayuwa,karki yarda abin duniya ya tsole miki ido,ki kasance Mai tsoron ALLAH aduk inda kike ki sani ALLAH yana ganin duk abinda kike yi,magana Ta karshi da zanmiki shine ALLAH ya sauke ki lafiya yayiwa abinda zaki haifa albarka.

Kuka suwaiba take kamar wacce aka aikowa mutuwa tace,"nayi maka alkawarin zanyi duk abinda ka umarce ni,saidai malam maganganun ka suna Kama DA wasiya.

Girgiza Kai yayi yace,"wannan duk na ALLAH ne suwaiba,ki kwantar da hankalin ki saina dawo,tunda yafita bai waiwayo ba har'ya fita daga harabar Gida.

Itah kuwa kuka take kamar ranta zaifita,gawani faɗuwar gaban dake damunta tun fitar Malam.



Tana zaune kan salayar Ta, taji yunusa yana zabga salati fetuwa tayi dan ganin abinda yake faruwa,saidai Mee mutane ne ciki acikin Gidan,cikin sauri Ta nufi gurin Dan ganin abinda Ya tara mutane haka.



Kasancewar saita wuce Ta babbar falon Gidan kafin Ta shiga bangaran Khadija yasa Ta ratsa mutanan zata wuce ganin mutum akwance a falon...  yasa Ta tsaya tana karewa Wanda yake kwancan kallon kasancewar fuskar sa arufe take yasa bata gane waye acikin   makarar ba.

Saidai Ta tabbatar mutuwa akayi tunda ga gawa akwance,ɗakin Khadija ta nufa harzata shiga amma jin kukan khadija yasa Ta tsaya,ALLAH kar'afaɗawa suwaiba wannan mutuwar Malam kasan halin da taka ciki Dan ALLAH.

"a a Khadija bana ganin wannan shawarar taki itace mafita idan ba'afaɗa mata Ba sai yaushe harzuwa wani lokaci zatayi Ta zaman jiran mijinta bayan yayi  tafiyar da babu DA wowa....

Karar da suwaiba Ta kwalla Shiya Hana Alhaji yunusa Mai kanti karasa maganar sa suka yo Kanta cikin sauri ...



Cikin sauri khadija Ta ɗabo ruwa aka zuba Mata sakamakon sumewan datayi.

Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi alamar ta dawo daidai saidai ruwan hawayen dake zarya akan fuskar Ta,Khadija tana share Mata sannan tace,"kiyi hakuri suwaiba haka ALLAH Mai kowa Mai komai Ya hukunta.

"Ki sani duk mai rai mammaci ne ni dake duk bazamu tserewa hukuncin ubangiji ba.

Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi haɗe  da share hawaye tace,shikenan  yanzu Malam yatafi yabarni? 

Kafaɗan ta Khadija ta rike tana rarrashin ta,har tasamu tayi shiru,saida aka gama shirya gawar Malam isah sannna aka bukaci ta shiga suyi sallama.

DA taimakon Khadija ta samu ta shiga ɗakin...  saidai tana ganin gawar Ta fadi sumammiya da dabara aka samo aka saka ta amota suka nufi asibiti sakamakon aman jinin da take yi bayan Ta farfado,sauran mutanan kuma suka nufi makabarTa dan yin jana'izar Malam Isah.

Suna zuwa aka karbi su cikin hanzari kasan cewar Khadija Ta kira likitan ta shaida mishi halin da ake ciki.

Ba'afi mintina biyar Ba likitan yafito ya shaida musu tana , nakuda.

Wannan shi ake Kira DA ga kushi ga kwanan yunwa,Ana cikin halin jimami ga kuma haihuwa saidai fatan Allah ya raba lafiya.

Ba karamar wahala suwaiba tasha Ba kasancewar bata da kwaranran lafiya ga kuma alhinin rashin mijin ta,saida tasha fe awannin bakwai tana abu ɗaya kafin ALLAH yayi nashi ikon.

Ta haifu dayar Ta Mace mai kama da ita sak,saida aka gama shirya ta da diyar sannan aka fita da ita Zuwa ɗakin Hutu,duk wanda yaga yarinya saiya zubda hawayee saboda maraicin daya sameta.

Ana cikin haka suwaiba Ta ɗago Kai tace,"Niku mayasa ba'ataho da Hashim ba?

Murmushi Khadija tayi tace," Hashim KO dan rigima,rigima ya dinga yi musu wai sai'ankawo shi gun Ummi shi,shine Alhaji yace,"aje adauko shi.

Murmushi tayi tace,"ALLAH yasa suna hanya dan Ina da tabbacin lokaci Ya kusa.

Cikin rashin fahimta tace"lokaci kuma lokacin meee.

Murmushi tayi akaro na biyu har zatayi magana aka turo kofar ɗakin,DA gudu Hashim ya rungume suwaiba yana kuka yace,"Ummi na shine kika tafi Kika barni? 

Itah dinma hawayee ta keyi tace,"maza share hawayeen ka sannan kamin alkawarin bazaka sake kukan rashi Na ba.

Duk Kansu kallon Ta suke Dan maganar Ta, Ta tabesu sosai,Khadija kuwa hawayee ya cika kurmin idon ta.sannan gawani sanyi da jikin ta ya mata.

Tace,"suwaiba wannan wace iriyar magana ce.



Murmushi tayi sannan ta rike hannun Hashim ta dan janye zanin da aka rufe jikin jaririyar DA shi tana kallon ta akaro na farko,ta riko hannun ta ta haɗa dana Hashim tace,"Hashim ga alkawarin dana daukar maka ALLAH yabani ikon cikawa.

Na baka amanar Maryam na mallaka maka ita har,Abadan Maryam malakar kace.

Ka yimin alkawarin rike ta amana.

Murmushi Hashim yayi sannan yace,"Ummi mata tace itah?itace Maryama ta?

"Zaki aura min itah?

Nod da kai kawai tayi.

Da gudu ya koma gun Khadija ya rungume ta yace,mami kinga matata.

Murmushin karfin hali tayi tace,"naganta amma ka kuma kacewa ummin ka babu inda zataje ta bark..

Tarin da Ya sarke suwaiba ne Ya hanata karasa maganar cikin sauri ta nufi gunta tana mika mata ruwa kankace Mee jini Ya fara zuba Ta baki Ta hanci.

Cikin sauri har tana bangaje yunusa ta fita da gudu tana kiran likitan,shikuma yunusa yana gefi yana Mata sannu.

Kankace Mee tafara Kalmar shahada bata ida ba rai yayi halin shi ko ina Na jikin ta ya sake,daidai lokacin  Khadija ta shigo saboda sabar kiɗima  har hannun likitan ta rike ..  Daidai lokacin.yunusa Ya dauke zanin ya lullube Ta kara Khadija ta kulla.

Saida ya gigita duk Wanda ke harabar asibitin koma wa da baya tayi ta fadi sumanmeya.

hashim kuwa kuka yake yana cewa ba'ba meya same Ummi na da mami na naga duk Kansu suna barci.

Daukar shi yayi ya yafitar dashi waje,yana share hawayee.

Haka rayuwa taci gaba da garawa haryau gashi shikarun Maryam bakwai.. izuwa lokacin kuwa bata da wani buri da yahuci kasancewa tare da yayan ta Hashim,shikuma lokacin yagama secondary school,Ana niman mishi admission zuwai jami'ar turai kasancewar  acikin shekaru da basu wuci bakwai ba ALLAH ya buɗewa yunusa Mai kanti hanyar samu ta sanadiyar wani attajiri Mai suna Alhaji talba.

Mai kudine sosai wanda duk fadin kaduna babu Wanda baisan shaharar sa ba,kasancewar sa Mai taimakon talakawa,koda yaushe kaje kofar gidan sa to babu shakka zaka tarar da mutane cike da kofar Gidan suna jiran fitowar Sa.

Yana DA Mata daya da yaran su biyu duk mata shafa'atu DA Zainab.

Arayuwar talba matsalar sa daya ce,matar sa hajiya aunty kamar yanda yaran suke kiranta.

Hajiya aunty ta kasance Mace wacce bata kaunar talaka kona miskala zarratin,babu irin nasihar da Alhaji talba baya Mata,amma akullum maganar ta daya ce ,babu ita babu talaka har'Abadan,yayan nata ma ahaka ta tarbiyan tar dasu.

Bayan an Samar wa Hashim admission ajami'ar turai aka saka ranar tafiyar sa,saidai matsalar su daya yanda Hashim da Maryam suka kasance bayan labarin tafiyar nashi ya iso kunnen su.

Kuka Maryam ta rinkayi Ana gobe tafiyar  shi,hakan ba karamar tayar musu da hankali yayi ba,harsuka fara tunanin abar tafiyar kawai yayi karatun shi kusa da gida,dan idan kanaso kaga walwala afuskokin yan gidan to kafaran tawa Maryam.

Yunusa ne yakira Alhaji talba yasanar dashi Halin da ake ciki bakaramar mamaki abin yabashi ba jin Maryam bayar Gidan bace duk tarin gatan daya ga ana mata,saidai ya daukar wa kanshi alkawarin bazai faɗawa kowa maganar ba kamar yanda yunusu mai kanti ya bukata.

Sannan yace" karka damu kuma karka sauke maganar tafiyar zansan yanda Zan shawo kan al'amarin,ahaka sukayi sallama .

Washa garin ranar Alhaji talba ya kwasu iyalan shi gabaki ɗaya suka nufi Gidan  alhaji yunusa.

Tarba mai kyau suka samu agun khadija dan daima tana cikin alhinin rabuwa da tilun danta namiji, sannan ga kukan da Maryam keyi sanadin haka ita ma safnah ta Kama.

Zaune suke suna  dan taba hira sama sama kasancewar Hajiya aunty dauke kanta sama tayi tana hura hanci adole ita ga mai arziki,ita khadija ma abin nata dariya yake bata.

Ba sufi mintina talatin ba Hashim ya fito rike da hannun Maryam dayan hannun shi kuma dauki da karamar jakka.

Sanye yake da sut ruwan toka,hannun sa dauke yake da bakar agogo kafar sa sanye yake da  bakar haf cover ,sumar sa akwance lufluf kasancewar mahaifiyar sa Khadija bafulata nace.

Idon sa fari ne kal,fararan hakoran sa reras ajire.

Karasawa gunsu su Khadija suka yi ya dan suggunna ya gaishi su sannan yanimi guri ya zauna,sannan yayi wa Maryam nuni da kujerar da ke kusa da nashi ta zauna.

Zainab kuwa diyar Alhaji talba kasa dauke idon ta tayi daga kan Hashim tun bai lura ba har'ya gane.

kara tsuke fuska yayi.....❄❄❄❄❄

```Wannan shafin gabaki ɗaya naka nee```..

 *SAFNAZ*



"Yanayin ƙallon da zainab take binsa dashi yasa zaman gurin gabaki ɗaya ya gundure shi... 

Ƙallon sa  Alhaji talba yayi yadanyi murmushi irin nasu na manya yace,"Hashim kardai harka kusa ka tafi naga sai kallon agogo kake? 

Martanin murmushin shima ya mayar sannan yace,"a a Abba kawai dai dama Ina tunanin zuwa na sayawa maryama  ta abin tabawa ne,shine nake tunanin kar lokaci ya kure.

Kallon agogon falon yayi sannan yace"gaskiya kam amma inba daɗewa zakuyi ba aida sauran lokaci.

Tashi yayi kamar an tsikare shi yace,"maryama ta,tashi mu tafi.

Kamar dama jira take cikin sauri ta mike tace,"bro's Hashim nima Zan biku.

Cikin sauri shafa'atu ta riko hannun ta tana mata alama da ta zauna karta bisu kasancewar sarai take Lura da yanayin kallon da take yiwa Hashim ɗin.

Kinzama tayi saima wucewa datayi da sauri ta riko dayan hannun Maryam ta na cewa,"maryamar mu,mu tafi KO.

Girgiza Kai Maryam tayi tace,"nidai ki sake ni ,kuma niba maryamar ki bace KO yaya?

Yanayin da tayi maganar saida kowa yayi dariya harshi kansa Hashim ɗin,sabanin shafa'atu da tabita da wata uwar harara,tace Dan ALLAH tafi can bakima gode ba.

Duk wanda yake gurin saida yaga wani matsiyacin kallon da Hashim yayiwa shafa'atu...  sannan yace"daɗin abun ba gaiyatar ki mukayi ba.

Bai tsaya jin abinda zata faɗa  ba,YA fisgi hannun maryam suka wuce.

Binsu tayi cikin sauri gudu gudu harta cim musu.

Karamar super market suka shiga ,dauki wancan dauki wancan harsai da suka shafe awa daya agurin,cikin sauri suka nufi gida.

Suna zuwa Alhaji yunusa ya fara faɗa yana cewa,"bansan randa zakayi hankali ba Hashim kuntafi kun zauna gashi lokaci na neman kurewa.

Hakuri yabashi duk suka shiga mota suka nufi airport suna zuwa aka fara kiran fasanja ciki sauri Hashim ya fito da zubuna guda biyu azurfa daya tana dauke da kalmar *H*  ɗayar kuma *m* mikowa Maryam Mai kalmar h yayi yace" ta kawo hannun ta ,miko hannun ta tayi ya Zara mata bakaramar kyau azurfar tayi mata ba.

Sannan ya mika mata Mai kalmar m yace ta saka mishi ahannun yanda ya mata  Haka tayi mishi .

Kukan da safnah ta sake ne ya juyo da hankalin kowa kanta,Alhaji talba yace,"lafiya me ya faru,cikin kukan tace,"ba ya Hashim bane ya sayawa maryam zube bai sayamin ba.

Duk ka murmushi akayi sannan Alhaji talba ya zaro Kudi yace," gashi kema gashi ki siya,murmushi tayi tana yiwa Maryam golo.

Suna kallo har jirgin su Hashim ya daga,wani irin kuka Maryam ta sake tana cewa,"ya Hashim ka dawo Karka tafi.

lallashin ta suka shiga yi 
. sannan aka samu ta shiga mota suka nufi gida.

Bangaran Hashim ma abin bai canza zani ba hawayee ya rinka yi lokacin da jirgin su ya tashi,ganin hankalin mutane yafara dawowo kansa yasa yayi saurin daidaita natsuwar sa.

Bangaran Zainab ɗiyar Alhaji talba har suka isa gida banda labarin Hashim babu abinda abban suke bata
..  lokacin da suka isa gida ma falon sa ta nufa tana cewa,"gaskiya daddy ni bangaji DA jin labarin ya Hashim ba.

Cikin sauri mummyn su ta riko hannun ta tace," naga take taken ki Zainab kifita a ido na Karna ji karna gani kindai ji na faɗa  miki.

Kuka ta fashe dashi ta shige ɗakin ta tana ji idan bata mallaki Hashim amatsayin mijinta ba bazata iya rayuwa ba.

Tun daga ranar Zainab bata da gurin yini sai gidan su Hashim wani lokacin har kwana,shaƙuwa ce mai karfi tsakanin Maryam da Zainab,shaƙuwar su ta samo asaline kasancewar Zainab tana samun damar jin muryar Hashim ta gun Maryam.

Mummyn su tayi faɗa harda duka..  amma duk abanza dan Zainab tayi nisa bata jin kira,ɗakin ta kuwa lungu   da sako   na ɗakin duk hotunan Hashim ne birjik.

A bangaran Hashim kuwa bai kawo komai aranshi game da Zainab ba,shidai kawai yana kulata ne albarkacin maryam.
  

Akwana atashi yau shakarar Hashim biyu da tafiya,shakuwar Maryam da Zainab kulum kara gaba yake dan har ita kanta Maryam tana zuwa Gidan su ta kwana,ranar da Maryam ta fara kwana a Gidan ta ga koina na ɗakin hutunan Hashim ne,da murna ta dauki wayar Zainab ta kira Hashim, kasancewar yasan number shiyasa ya dauka da sauri.

KO sallama bata yiba ta fashe da dariya,dakatar da ita yayi yace,"sister ki daina irin wanna dariya kinji,bana so kuwa ya rinka jin muryar ki.

cikin shagwaba tace,"ni kam Nama fasa faɗa maka maganar.

Lallashin ta ya shigayi sannan tace,"Yaya hotonan ka da yawa nagani.

Aina?

"Dakin aunty Zainab ya'ya wallahi harda toilet hotonan  kane.

Dakatar da itah yayi cikin tsawa yace"me ya kaiki Gidan? 

Rashin Sabo da irin wannan tsawar yasa ta fashe da kuka,cikin kuka tace,wallah yaya itace tace saina biyo ta.

"Kuma su mami suka yarda kika bita ko ina take?

Juyawa tayi tana duba inda ta barta Amma bata gantaba tace,"yaya tafita.

Idan ta dawo kice ta Kira ni,baijira jin amsar taba ya kartsi kiran.

Kuka ta fashe dashi dan atsawan rayuwar ta yaune Karo na farko da yayan ta yayi Mata irin wannan fadar,duk da karamcin shakarun ta, ta
sancewa yayanta Mai kaunar tane.

Ba'afi minti biyar ba Zainab tashigo dakin hannun ta dauke da doguwar rigar barci mai matsifar kyau tana cewa,"tashi sister kinga sabuwar rigar barcin dana siyo miki.

Dago Kai dazata yi taga hawayee na zuba akuncin ta.

Cikin sauri ta karaso tace,"sister na meya faru waya ta bamin ke? 

"Yaya yace ki kirashi,iya abinda ta iya faɗa kenan ta kuma ta kwanta.

Zainab kuwa tsabar kauɗi KO tsayawa tambayar dalili batayi ba, ta zabgamai kira.

Ring uku yayi aka dauka,  tace,"hello ya Hashim wai kace nakira ka,wani irin takaici yaji da kara tsanar ta,yace,"tunda Ku agidan Ku a ba'a koya miki gaisuwa bako?to ki saurare ni da kyau kiji abinda Zan faɗa miki.

"Kiyi gaggawar fita daga cikin duniyar mu,bama bukatar ki,mu biyu muka Fara bamu bukatar bako,sannan abu na karshe karki kuma zuwa Gidan mu ki mayar da Maryam gida,hotona kuma da kika saka aɗakin ki kiyi gaggawar cire wa,Banza mara aji.

Bai tsaya jin abinda zata ce ba ya kashe wayar.

Kuka ta fashe dashi wanda saida Maryam ta tsorata dan kuka mai kara take rizga.

Batafi mintina biyar da aje wayar ba saiga shafa ta shigo ɗakin,ganin tana kuka yasa ta karasowa inda take cikin sauri take tambayar ta dalilin kukan.

Shiru tayi domin tasan faɗa Mata daidai yake da rasa rayuwar ta Dan Dama kiris mummyn su ke jira.

Sauya akalar maganar tayi da cewa aunty shafa kidau ko min magani kaina zai fashe.

Tashi tayi tana cewa"saboda Kai nami ki ciwo shine zaki zauna kina kuka,to nidai idan kingama ki fito musu da yarinya anzo daukar ta.

Hakanan tanaji tana gani aka tafi da Maryam.

Bakin ciki kamar zata kasheta ahaka ta kwana KO Ritsawa bata
Yi ba,sanadin haka ta tashi da matsanan cin zazzabi kankace Mee,ciwo yayi tsanani saida aka dangana da Zuwa asibiti.

Lokacin da labarin ya iski ahalin gidan alhaji yunusa Mai kanti bakaramar tashin hankali suka shiga ba,cikin sauri suka shirya dan zuwa asibiti,saidai babu yanda ba'ayi ba Maryam ta tafi ba tace babu inda zataje.

Mamakin abun suka shiga yi dan kowa yasan yanayin shakuwar maryam DA aunty Zainab,cikin hikima abban su yake tambayar ta dalilin  rashin son zuwan na ta ,ita kuwa kamar dama jira take nan danan ta kwashe duk abinda ya faru ta faɗa  musu.

Sanda suka gama jin labarin salati suka sake lokacin daya,daddy baiyi wata wata ba ya fito da waya yafara kiran layin Hashim ring biyu yayi aka dauka,cikin natsuwa ya fara da sallama.

Dakatar dashi alhaji yayi sannan yace"Ashe dama kai mutumin banza ne Hashim bansani ba, Akan wani dalili zaka walakanta yar mutane,to Bari kaji idan har'ya tabbata Zainab sonka take to wallahi kaji na rantse maka babu makawa saika aure ta ,saidai idan ALLAH bai yarda ba,katsi wayar shi yayi baijira jin amsar shiba.

Ganin yanda abban su ya hada rai yasa bata kuma musu ba ta bisu suka nufi asibiti,kowa da abinda yake tunani aran shi,bangaran khadija addu'a take ALLAH yasa ba son Hashim zainab take ba,dan ita asan ranta Hashim Ya aure Maryam ita ɗaya,dan bata son abinda zaikawo damuwa cikin zaman su.

Shikuwa Alhaji yunusa kasa tantance dalilin da yasa yayi rantsuwar Yayi dan gaskiya yana mattukar son Maryam tazama matar Hashim na farko kuma na karshi. 

Ahaka har suka Kai asibitin,yanayin da suka ga jikin Zainab bakaramar tayar musu da hankali yayi ba,gabaki daya bata iya gane wanda ke tsaye akan ta,sannan  babu abinda bakinta ke furtawa sai sunan Hashim.

Mami bata tsaya wani tunani ba ta daga waya ta kira Hashim,yana daga wa tace,"kasan cewa nice mahaifiyar ka KO?
Daga dayan bangaran aka amsa da eh.

To umarni nake baka Ba shawara ba inaso ka amince da auran Zainab.

"Zainab mami Zainab fa kikace 
Yarinyar da Kalmar soyaya bai taba shiga tsakanina da itaba.

Eh ita nake nufi  Kaine Baka taba jin sonta Ba..  amma ita da sonka da kaunar ka take kwana take tashi.

Mami Maryam fa?

"Kana da damar auran mata ɗaɗai har huɗu Maryam tazo daga baya.

"Shikenan mami... amma aidai abari harna dawo ko,kinsan fa sauran min shekara uku,idan zata eh jirana to.

Shikenan duk yanda muka yanke Zan faɗa maka.ALLAH yayi maka albarka ALLAH ya jibanci lamarin ka.

Amsawa kawai yake amma wani irin ciwon kai yake ji hade da jiri,da kyar ya samu ya daidai ta natsuwar sa har sukayi sallama bata fahimci halin da yake ciki ba.

Koda sukayi sallama ta samu Alhaji tayi mishi bayane,bakaramar daɗi yaji Ba,sannan shima ya sanar da Alhaji talba abinda suka yanke,rashin jin dadin haka Alhaji talba yanuna dan sam baiso Zainab tazama silar rushewar burukan suba.

Sabanin mummyn su dacewa tayi  yin hakan bakaramar taimaka musu sukayi ba.

Yau da gobe kayan ALLAH haryau gashi sauran wata daya Hashim ya kammala karatun shi dayayi na tsawan shekaru biyar.

Shirye shirye zuwanshi ake babu Kama hannun Yaro kasancewar bayan ya dawo da sati daya za'adaura auran su da Zainab.

Farin cikin da Zainab take ciki bazai misaltu ba jitake kamar tafi kowa dace da miji.

Maryam kuwa yanzu haka shekarun ta Goma sha biyu safnah sha hudu. Hashim kuwa ashirin da hudu.
Zainab sha tara.

Saidai kasancewar ta Mai gaba gaban Jiki idan ka ganta zaka dauka ta haurawa shekarun ta dan komai na cikir budurci ya fara fita ajikin ta, saidai jikin ta ba'irin wannan mara kyaun ganin bane,daidai wa daidai.

gata Fara tana DA fararan dara daran idano gashin idon ta gazar gazar girar ta akwance. DA Dan bakin ta madaidai ci,gata DA tsayen gashi,Kai intakai ce muku duk inda ake niman Mace to Maryam ta Kai har tana niman wuce wa.

Gyar jiki mami tasa aka mata kankace Mee kyaun ta yakuma fituwa,ranar da Hashim zai dawo.

Wata hadaddiyar leshi mami tasa aka dinka mata aka mata jan lalle,idan kaga maryam aranar zaka rantse amaryace.

Agugguje suka nufi airport suna Zuwa jirgin su na sauka daidai lokacin Zainab da iyayan ta suka iso suma cikin fara'a suka gaisa,sannan suka tsaya jiran sauko warshi.

Shiya fara ganin su kasancewar akwai yawan mutane airport din,Maryam yafara hanguwa daidai lokacin wani matashin saurayi yana mata magana ita kuma tana amsa mishi harda. dariya,dariyar da tayi ne yabawa dempol dinta damar lotsiwa ga fararan hakuran ta reres.

Tsabar fushi baisan lokacin da ya karasa gurin ba.....



Jitaye anyi jifa da ita gefe sauran kaɗan takai kasa,... saurin juyuwa tayi tana kallon shi cike da mamaki,suɗin ma kallon sa suke da mamakin faruwan wannan al'amarin,kyawawan mari guda biyu yayi mishi sannan yace,"bansan ko Kai waye ba kamar yanda bakasan matsayena wancan aguna ba,ya mishi nuni da Maryam,KO wace irin halaka ke tsakanin ko Ina maka gargadin yanke ta,idan ba haka ba zaka gamu da mummunar hukunci.

Cikin sauri Mami ta nufi gunshi tana cewa,"Kai hashim bansan shashanci kasan KO waye?Munir ne fa Dan kanin Alhaji talba.

"Mami KO Dan kanin waye ni bandamu ba,abinda nasani shine bazan Bari kowa ya shiga gona ta ba,bai tsaya jin abinda zata Kuma cewa ya nufi dayar motar.

Babu Gardama ya fisgi motar suka tafi,bakaramar tashin hankali Maryam ta shiga ba babu abinda takeyi sai kuka,lalashin duniyar nan mami tayi Mata... amma duk abanza maganar ta daya ce,mami Nice na batawa ya Hashim rai haryayi irin wannan fushin ko? 

Murmushi mami tayi tace,"Maryam autar mami ki daina kuka kar kanki yayi ciwo Hashim bazai taba yin fushi da keba,jin haka yasa tadan sassauta kukan da takeyi.

bangaran Zainab kuwa banda ruwan bala'in da take saukewa Munir babu abinda takeyi,waishi ya jawo bacin ran mijin ta.

Taba baki shafa'atu tayi tace,"su miji manya,ke harkina saran samun farin ciki agun wannan dan jijji da Kain? "alhalin ubanshi ma da bazarmu yake taka rawa.

Ina wuta Zainab ta saka shafa'atu tace,"koma menene aigoma ni tunda na samu ɗan jijji da Kai Zan aure kefa waya taba zuwa gida DA sunan yazu niman auran ki?

"To baki idan kasan abinda zaka faɗa bakasan wace za'amayar maka ba,bakin ciki kamar ya kashi shafa'atu,kasancewar motar su uku ne kawai shiyasa aka rasa wanda zai tsawatar musu.
💥💥💥💥💥💥💥💥

Ahaka har suka isa Gida,Maryam bata tsaya ko inah ba sai kofar ɗakin Hashim,tana Zuwa taji kofar arufe,bugun kofar tashiga yi tana rera kuka tana bashi hakuri daya bude kofar.

Shiru yayi yana jin kukan nata na ratsa jijiyuyin jikin shi,saiyayi kamar ya tashi ya buɗe,sai kuma Ya tuna da yanda yaga take ta washe hakura tana dariya da wani bashiba.

Jin kukan nata yaki Karewa yasa ya tashi ya buɗe kofar,yana buɗewa ta fada jikin shi tana kuka Mai ratsa jiki,shikuwa gogan shiru yayi sakamakon wani irin shock da yaji daga saman kanshi harzuwa tafin kafar shi.

Ganin tana niman jefashi wata duniyar yayi karfin halin janye ta daga jikin shi yana share mata hawayee.

Dago kanta tayi tana kallon shi da rinannun idanuwan ta dake kara rikitar dashi tace,"mena maka kake fushi dani?

Juyar dakai yayi sannan yace,"Maryam bana son na kara ganin ki kin tsaya da wani ɗana miji idan bani ba.

Nod dakai kawai taye.

Ranar asabar donbin jama'a suka shaida daurin auran Hashim yunusa Mai kanti da Zainab Alhaji talba.

zumuɗi agun amarya ba'a magana..  shikuwa gugan naka saida aka Kai ruwa rana aka samu ya halalci daurin auran,shidinma tare da Maryam yatafi duk inda kajuya zaka gansu Amakale da juna.

💞zainab ce ƙwance kan gado.. Banda hawaye babu abinda take.. Lokaci zuwa lokaci take kallon agogon ɗakin karfe biyu na dari agogon ɗakin yanuna,izuwa lokacin kuwa babu Hashim babu labarin shi.



Ahaka har barci barawo ya sace ta... baita ta farka ba saida taji ana bugun kofar falon,da tsaki ta nufi kofar babu KO dankwali akanta suman kanta abarbaje,tana budi kofar Maryam ta fada jikin ta tana cewa aunty naji kewar...

Tsawar da Zainab ta daka mata shiya hanata karasa maganar tace,"meya kawaki da safennan kinzo zaki cikamin kunne da surutu,ke nifa bara kiji KO kaɗan bantaba jin sonki ba..  asalima babu Wanda na tsana sama dake duk duniya tunda nalura abinda nafiso bashida abinso da kauna sama dake,duk da nasan ke kanwar sace ke amm...

Saukar Mari taji afuskar ta Wanda saida ta daina gani na wucan gadi,sannan ta dawo daidai.

Maryam kuwa kallon sa take cike da mamaki afuskar ta.

nuna ta yayi yace,"sanin kanki ne ni dake babu wata  alakar soyaya atsakanin mu,na aure kine saboda yin biyayya  ga iyaye na..  dan haka nake sake gargadin ki, ki fita daga rayuwar mu bama bukatar ki,Zainab ki tausayawa ruhin data rayu da son mutum daya tak duk cikin  faɗin duniyar nan.

Yana kaiwa nan bai tsaya jin abinda zata ce ba ya rike hannun Maryam suka bar gurin.

Binsu tayi da ido baki abude tana tunani maganar dan gabaki daya ya gin dayar da ita.

***

Sallamar sis Shafa'atu ne ya dawo da ita daga duniyar tunanin data shilla,tana ganin ta da gudu ta faɗa jikin ta tana kuka mai ban tausayi tace,"sis ki taimake ni sis Hashim akwai wace yake so bani yake so ba.

Ture ta daga jikin ta tayi tace,"aidama duk wanda baiji bari ba abinda zai tarar ma saitafi Wannan,saida nafaɗa miki... amma ki kayi burus dani harda min gori ai ga irin ta nan.

ita dai Zainab bata ce komai ba banda kuka babu abinda ta keyi.

Ganin kukan yakika rewa yasa shafa'atu tafara rarrashin ta da alkawarin zata taimaka Mata ta hanyar zuwa gurin bako wanda tuni mahaifiyar su tayi Mata register agurin shi dan duk wani sako ita ce take amsowa.

Daɗi ne ya cika ta jin za'a taimaka Mata.

Tun Daga ranar Zainab bata kuma shiga sabgar Hashim ba bisa sharadin da shafa'atu ta gindaya Mata na karta kuma kulashi ko shiga sabgar shi...  harsai karbo maganin da shida kanshi zaikawo kanshi gare ta,hakan kuwa bakara mar dadi yayi wa hashim  ba.. Ko babu komai ya huta da ganin mummunar fuskar ta .

Ranar da shafa'atu ta kawo Mata sakon boka kuwa murna agunta ba'a magana,saidai lokacin da sha'afa ta sanar da ita hanyar da zatabi tasamu biyar bukata nan da nan rain ta ya baci tace,"yanzu saboda ALLAH sis yanzu babu wata hanya saita hanyar wannan shashashar yarinyar mai kama da maiyar?

"Gaskiya dai itace kadai hanya mafi sauki.

Gaskiya nidai kam daso samu ne acanza wata dabarar dan duk duniya babu wace na tsana sama da itah.

"Dalla can Banza dadina dake ba'a miki abin arziki idan kinga baza kiyi ba kibani na mayar masa dashi,tunda cewa akayi kiso ta da gaske,ai nuna wa zakiyi kamar kin fishi kaunar ta kinga saiki amfani da wannan damar kiyi abinci ki zuba maganin aciki kibata ta Kai mishi kinga daga zarar kin samu cikar burin ki saiki watsi da ita.

Shewa hade da tsalle Zainab tayi ta faɗa jikin shafa tana murnar wannan shawara data bata.

Tunda wannan ranar shakuwa Mai karfi Yashiga tsakanin maryan da aunty Zainab shakuwa ce wacce ta Kai har kayan sawan su ta koma iri daya abinci ma tare suke ci,hakan ba karamar daɗi ya kewa mutanan Gidan ba,sabanin Hashim wanda sam bai yarda da shakkuwar tasu ba.. 

Wata rana Hashim yayi tafiya kwanan sa uku acan ranar dazai dawo  a ranar  Zainab tayi kudirin tabbatar da kudirin ta akansu.

Maryam tana zaune afalo tana kallon arewa 24 Zainab ta shigo ta dafe mata Kafaɗa Tana murmushi tace,"kanwa ta wai yaushi yayan ki zaidawo ne?

Cikin sauri tace,"yau ne aunty lafiya kike tambaya ta bai faɗa miki bane?

Murmushi Zainab tayi wanda iyakar sa lebe tace,"ya faɗa min,kinga Maryam jikina ciwo yakemin bazan samu yi masa abinci ba ki kirashi kifaɗa masa kece zakiyi masa girki yafaɗa miki abinda yake so,idan ya tambaye ki  ni kice masa natafi gida duba mummy na.

Cikin sauri Maryam ta dauko wayar ta bugu daya aka dauka,da sallama abakin ta tace yaya zaka dawo yau kuwa?

Daga can bangarar aka amsa da zandawo IN SHA ALLAH menene kina bukatar wani abunne?

"A a Yaya dama so nake nayi maka abinci kafin ka dawo kaga aunty batanan mamee kuma tana gurin suna.

Murmushi yayi sannan yace duk abinda ki kaga yayi miki kidafa min kinji,amma kiyi ahankali  da hannun ki kinji angel.

"To Yaya sai anjima.

sauke wayar ta keda wuya ta duba bata ga Zainab ba da sauri ta nufi dakin ta,tana Zuwa ta same ta kwanci tayi rufda ciki tana risgar kuka cikin sauri ta Karasa gurin ta tana tambayar ta tace,"aunty kan ne ke miki ciwo?️❄❄❄❄❄
