*Bismillahi rahamanin Rahim*

Murmushi zainab tayi tace,"eh Amma yayi sauki me ya'yan naki yace?

Zama tayi abakin gadon tace,'hmmm aunty cewa yayi duk abinda yamin na dafa masa.

Wani Abu  zainab taji ya taso mata ya tsaya Mata awuya shibai fito ba kuma bai koma ba,tashi tayi da sauri ta dauko ruwa kafawa abaki tayi Bata cire ba Saida ta shanye kaf izuwa lokacin kuwa idanuwan ta sun canza launi.

Da sauri maryam ta Isa gare ta tana tambayar ta,"aunty zainab menene?

Murya adakile ta amsa da,"me Kika gani?

,"Naga idanuwan  ki sun canza kala Kona kira ya'ya na faɗa  mishi baki da lafiya?

Da sauri tace,"yauwa maryama ta Ina sonki Dan kina da wayau sosai.

Murmushin Jin daɗi tayi Dan idan akwai abinda tafi so aduniya sama da iyayan ta da kuma ya'yan ta to sai ganin  aunty ta cikin farin ciki,da sauri taje ta dauko wayar ta, ringing ɗaya ya daga.

Da sallama abakin ta radau.

Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi Dan idan zaiji muryar Maryam so dari arana to babu shakka bazai taba gajiya ba.

Amsa sallamar yayi sannan yace,"autar mama Ina jinki.

Dama ya'ya aunty ce Bata da lafiy...

Tsawa ya daka Mata Yana cewa,",shot up waya aike ki?nace waya aiki ki?ita zainab din Bata da baki ne saike?

Shiru tayi nandanan jikin ta ya dauki rawa kiris take jira ta fashe da kuka jin tayi shiru yasa ya katse wayar Yana Dana sanin tsawar daya mata.

Kwanciya tayi ta fashe da kuka zainab kuwa hakan bakaramar daɗi yayi Mata ba Dan ko babu komai tayi sanadiyar saka ta kuka, rarrashin ta tashiga yi tana Bata hakuri Wanda iyakar sa fatan baki Dan zuciyar ta cike take da farin ciki harta mance da bacin ran da take cike.

Tashi tayi tace,"Maryam tashi muje mu hadawa ya'yan ki abinci,ga ma'makin ta sai taga ta mike kamar wani Abu bai shiga tsakanin suba,tsayawa tayi tana kallon ta tace,"hmm Maryam gaskiya abinda ya'yan ki yayi miki Sam banji dadi ba.

Murmushi Maryam tayi tace,"ni kuwa Kinga komai ya wuce aguna Kinga nikam bama abinda yamin ne ya sakani kuka ba a a kawai dai Ina kuka ne Dan ta dalilina ran ya'ya na ya baci.

Tsaki aunty zainab tayi ta shige kitchen,kallon ma'maki maryam ke binta dashi Dan Hali irin na aunty zainab bakaramar daure Mata Kai yake ba,yanzu ka ganta cikin walwala cikin mintina kalilan saita canza ganin tsayuwar bazai amfanar da ita komai ba yasa tabi bayanta.

Shikuwa Hashim Haka ya kama hanya zuciyar sa tana Masa radaɗi  Dan kwata kwata baiso saka maryamar sa kuka ba Dan Haka ma yasa yake ta sharara gudu kamar zai tashi sama.

Abinci  Maryam tayi Mai dankaran daɗi abinci kusan kala hudu tayi na tarban Hashim Saida ta KAMMALA sannan kowaccen su ta nufi toilet Dan tsaftace jikin ta.

Ƙwalliya Mai dankaran kyau  zainab tayi tayi kyau daidai misali. lace ta saka ruwan sararin samaniya  yana dauke da duwatsu farara ,ita kuwa Maryam abaya baka ta saka sannan ta dauko farar veil ta yane kanta dashi duk da  bawata kwalliya tayi ba Amma tayi mattukar kyau.

Cikin sanda aunty zainab ta nufi kitchen buɗe  kular abincin tayi akowanne Saida ta barbada garin maganin aciki Dan ma kar'asamu akasi.

Cikin sauri ta bar kitchen din ta nufi dakin ta.

Suna zaune afalo sukaji hon da sauri zainab ta leka window aikuwa shine wani ajiyar zuciya zainab ta sake tana aiyanawa aranta yau dai burin ta zai cika Dan yanda akace da zarar yaci zasuga aiki Nan take .

Juyawan Nan da zatayi taga wayam babu maryam babu labarin ta sake kallon window tayi aikuwa tayi mumunar gamu maryam ta gani Hashim ya daga ta sama ita Kuwa sai watsil watsil take kamar wata yar tsana.

Bakin ciki kamar ya kashe zainab Amma saita daure Dan samun biyan bukatar ta, ahaka har suka shigo gani zainab ahakimce  yasa Hashim daure fuska kamar bai taba dariya ba .

Da murmushi afuskar ta tayi mishi sannu da zuwa hannu kawai ya daga Mata sannan yace da Maryam ta jira shi bara ya watsa ruwa.

Tunda Hashim yashiga ciki zainab Bata koma ko kallon inda maryam ke tsaye ba ahaka Hashim ya fito ya same su.

Hannun Maryam ya rike Basu tsaya ko ina ba sai dinning table Maryam kuwa tafara Shan jinin jikin ta ganin  ya'yan nata  ko magana bai yiwa aunty zainab ba.

Bude baki tayi da niyar magana Amma Ina Hashim Dan yatsarsa ya daura akan lebenta yace,"ba'a magana yayin cin abinci.

Shiru tayi da kansa ya debo abinci a plet ya mika Mata sannan ya zuba nashi.

Ita dai zainab Alla'alla take taga yakai abincin bakin sa.

Da bismillahi yafara cin abincin cokali uku yayi yaji kansa yayi nauyi ga wani irin Abu da yaji Yana ratsa cikin jikin sa,dafe Kai yayi Wanda zuwa yanzu jinsa yake kamar zata Fadi kasa.

Ganin Haka yasa zainab tayi murmushi mugunta sannan ta taso zuwa inda yake zaune,dafe kansa tayi aikuwa Nan danan yaji kamar ancire Masa ciwon  kan,gani ya fara warewa yasa ta cire hannun ta tana cirewa yaji kamar ciwon matafi nada,aida sauri ya janyo hannuwan ta ya daura saman kansa.

Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi sannan ya kalle ta da jajayan idanuwan sa yace,"Dan ALLAH karki kuma cire hannun ki.

Shagwaba fuska tayi tace,"to Ni daki nakeson zuwa Kuma kace karna cire hannu na.

Cikin sauri yace,"muje na rakaki dakin,tashi yayi har'ya Kai bakin kofar dakin ya juyo ya kalli Maryam sannan yace,"maryam zo muje.

Cikin sauri aunty zainab ta cire hannun ta, fisgar hannun yayi ya daura akansa kamar zaiyi kuka yace," Dan ALLAH karki cire kinji.

To nidai idan bakaso na cire saika Kori Maryam kace ta tafi.

Batare da bata lokaci ba yace,"maryam ki tafi gurin Mami zan nemeki anjima kinji .

Nod da Kai kawai tayi  tafita zuciyar ta cike da  tunanin abinda ya sami ya'yan nata.

Koda ta shiga bangaran Mami kwanciya  tayi kamar Mai barci saidai kwata kwata ba barcin take ba hasalima ko alamar sa Bata ji.

Mami taga yanayin autar tata Amma tayi shiru kamar Bata lura ba acewar ta Yana da kyau karka fiya shiga lamarin yara.

Cikin kwana biyu kowa dake gidan yaga sauyi tattare da Hashim dan 
Yanzu duk inda yake zainab na biye dashi bayaso tayi nisa dashi Kona minti daya,Maryam kuwa Abu kadan zatayi Nan danan zai hauta da faɗa.

Abin Yana daurewa Mami Kai saidai wani bangare na zuciyar ta tana aiyana hakan shiyafi alkairi tunda ko babu komai zainab matar sace.

Wasa wasa karamar magana ta Zama babba Dan yakai Hashim kwata kwata baya kaunar ganin Maryam, safnah cema take dan samun sakin fuska agunsa tunda idan ta gaishe shi Yana amsawa.

Babu irin tambayar da Mami batayi masa ba Amma Sam yace shifa babu komai.

Maryam kuwa dama gashi ita ba jikin kirki ke gare taba hakan yasa ta Kara kanjame wa, tayi baki ga aiki da take yiwa  zainab  kullum babu fashi girki wanke wanke.

Haka aka shafe tsawan shekara ana Abu daya ganin abin bamai karewa bane yasa Mami ta Tara su afalo Bayan angama cin abinci.

Da sallama ta fara sannan tace,"na tara ku anan ne badan komai ba sai Dan na Kara jawo hankalin ku kan Zama da junanku da Amana ku kasance masu yawan yafiya ga Yan uwan KU Koda wata matsalar ta gifta atsakani.

Sannan Kai Hashim musamman wannan zaman..  domin kai ne muka zauna zaman yinsa,kamar yanda na saba tambayar ka kullum yauma zan nanata,shin dakwai wani boyayyiyar laifi da maryama tayi maka ne Wanda muka kasa fada Mana?

Dukar da Kai yayi Yana Jin kirjinsa na bugawa da karfi gawani irin tsoro daya shiga jikin sa lokaci daya, kasa hada ido da Mami yayi sanda yaji ta Kara nanata masa tambayar sannan ya dago Kai cikin dakewa yafara magana,"Mami wallahi wallahi kamar yanda na fada miki babu wan laifi da yarinyar Nan tame.

Duba daya yayi mata yakasa dauke ido daga kanta Dan yanda tayi baki ta fige ta rame abin bakaramar tsoro ya bashi ba.

Budi baki yayi da niyyar magana amma  yaji kamar ana rufi Masa baki......




Mami kallon sa kawai take... Amma tabbas tasan da wata akasa ganin ya kasa magana Banda hawaye babu abinda ke zarya akuncin sa yasa tace,"tashi ka tafi ALLAH yayi Mana Mai kyau.

"Ameen yace sannan ya tashi ya fice.

Tashi zainab tayi zata bi Bayan sa Mami tace,"zainab ko kinsan abinda ke damun yaron nan?

Murguɗa baki tayi tace,"ta ina zansani kema kenan baki sani ba bare ni,Bata tsaya jiran jin abinda zata faɗa  ba ta fice tana gunguni.

Kuka Mai tsuma  zuciya Maryam kiyi Saida Mami ta rarrashi ta sannan tayi shiru sai sauki ajiyar zuciya da take lokaci zuwa lokaci, kallon Mami tayi tace,"Dan ALLAH Mami karki Kara yiwa ya'ya magana akaina Kinga saboda kin masa magana ne yake kuka Kuma Ni bana son ganin kukan sa,na gwammace na dauwama ahaka.

Tashi tayi da gudu ta nufi ɗaki kulle kofar ɗakin tayi,babu irin magiyar da safnah batayi ba kanta buɗe  Amma ina Haka ta hakura ta koma gun mami.

Hashim kuwa ɗakin sa ya shiga ya rufe kofar faɗawa  gado yayi ya fashe da wani irin kuka Mai tsuma zuciya..  lokaci Mai tsawo ya dauka cikin wannan halin Dan shi kansa bai san. Meke damunsa ba.

Zainab koda ta dawo taga ya rufe kofar ɗakin sa Bata tsaya bata lokacin ta ba ta nufi &akin ta, wayar ta ta dauko number shafa'atu ta kira daga dayan bangaran aka dauka da cewa,"Hy kanwar ykk?

,"Hmm lafiya lau nake saidai yau...abinda ya faru kafta faɗa mata harda kari.

,"Hmmm gaskiya da matsala Kinga yanzu Abu ɗaya zakiyi,kisan duk yanda zakiyi kisashi ya nemi transfer kubar garin ma gabaki daya,inba Haka ba wallahi wannan matar zata Bata Mana aiki.

"Shiyasa nake sonki sis Wallahi kuwa itace dabara mafi sauki , shikenan zankira ki anjima.

Tana gama wayar ta nufi ɗakin da yake kwance bugawa take da karfi tana kuka Wanda acikin kankanin lokaci ta kirkire ta.

Jin kukan ta yasa ya tashi da sauri ya buɗe  kofar Yana buɗewa  ta faɗa  ajikin sa tana  kuka hani'an, rungume ta yayi yana tambayar ta?

Abinda kunnuwa na sukajiyo take faɗa   Masa Ni kaina *SAFNAH ALIYU JAWABI* Saida na buɗe baki tsabar ma'maki 😲😲😲

Cikin kuka take cewa,"Mami ce wai duk Abinda ke faruwa da sani na nice na rabaka da yar'uwar ka,Kuma IN SHA ALLAH saitayi Sanadiyar da zanbar gidan nan.

Rarrashin ta yashiga yi duk  da Yana kwakwanton maganar nata.

"Shikenan kiyi hakuri kinji.

"Gaskiya nidai bazan zauna agidan Nan ba saidai na tafi gidan mu.

Afirgice yake kallon ta gabaki ɗaya yaji ya rasa natsuwar sa yace,"saboda mee?bazaki bar gidan nan ba faɗamin  abinda kike so.

Murmushi tayi cikin zuciyar ta tace,"gaskiya nidai kanemi transfer mubar garin ma gabaki daya.

Shiru yayi Yana nazarin maganar can dai yace shikenan Ina kike so mu koma?

Dago Kai tayi da sauri tace,"da gaske kakeyi?

Murmushi yayi yace,"anything for you my love.

Shiga jikin sa tayi tana Mai godiya ga bokan da yayi Mata aiki,NI KUWA SAFNAH NACE AJURI ZUWA RAFI.

Murmushi tayi tace,"love mu koma kaduna kawai .

"Nan yayi miki?

"Eh.

"To shikenan kifara shiri.

Abba na dawowa Bayan yaci abinci ya huta Mami ta zauna kusa dashi.

Ganin yanda tayi shiru yasa yasan da magana abakin ta,murmushi yayi yace," maman maryam kamar akwai magana abakin ki ko?

Aikam dama jira take ya tambaye ta auku ta fashe da kuka.abba shikam abinma tsoro ya bashi Dan yasanta jajirtatciya mace ce ba karamar Abu ke sakata kuka ba, Dan rabon sa da ganin kukan ta tun rasuwar suwaiba mahaifiyar maryam.

Barin ta yayi Saida ta gama kuka Dan hakan ne kawai zaisa ta danji saukin abinda ke damunta .

Ganin tayi shiru Saidai ajiyar zuciyar da takeyi  lokaci zuwa lokaci, yasa ya tashi ya dauko Mata ruwa Mai sanyi tasha sannan yace,"nasan cewa duk Abinda ke damun ki,dan duk duniyan baki da Wanda Zaki faɗa masa sama dani,nayi ma'maki Jin yanda kike kuka nasan damuwar bata yau bace ta dade tana damun ki, tunda yanzun kin yake hukuncin sanar da ni  Ina sauraron ki.

Da muryar kuka ta zaiyana Masa duk Abinda ke faruwa wanda tsawan shekara tayi tana boye masa da Kuma yanda sukayi dashi ɗazu.

Banda gumi dake karyo masa babu abinda ya iya furtawa idanuwan sa kuwa sun kada sunyi ja.

Tsawan mintina sai yanzu ya ɗago Kai yace,"Amma kinsan Maryam marainiya ce ko? Kuma Amana agummu  ko?

"To wallahi bazan lamunci azabtar da maraini agidan Nan ba,zan iya batawa kowa Rai agidan Nan takan maryam.

Tashe yayi ya shige ɗakin sa Rai abace.

Mami kuwa kuka ta shiga rairawa Saida tayi Mai isar ta sannan ta hakura ta shige ɗaki.

Cikin kwana biyu Hashim ya gama shirye shiryen tafiya su kaduna sai, ranar da zasu tafi ya shiga cikin shashin Mami Dan ya sanar da ita Dan duk yanda yaso ya sanar da Mami zainab ta Hana Wai idan yafaɗa mata bazata Bari su tafi ba.

Da sallama yashiga lokacin Mami tana kitchen  Jin sallamar sa yasa ta fito kallon ta yayi ya dauke Kai Dan tun ranar bai Kuma shigowa shashin taba.

Rusunawa yayi yace," Mami Ina kwana?

"Lafiya lau ya iyalan.

Shiru yayi Dan koba'a faɗa ba yasan zainab Bata zuwa gaisar da mahaifiyar sa.

Kasa magana yayi Saida Mami tace,"aiki nake bara naje na karasa.

"Mami dama ba komai bane nazo na sanar dake ammin transfer  zan koma kaduna.

Da murmushi afuskar ta tace,"alhamdulillah yaushe zaka tafi?

"Yau nake saran tafiya mami.

Da ma'maki take kallon sa Amma sai tace ,"to abban naka ya sani ne?

"A a Mami idan ya dawo saiki sanar dashi.

Kukan da Mami takasa rikewa ne ya kubce mata.

Da sauri Hashim ya Isa gare ta da zummar rarrashin ta Dan iya tsayin  rayuwar sa bai taba ganin kukan taba aikuwa tunkan ya karaso ta tsayar dashi tana cewa,"karka yanda ka karaso Kai ko taku daya idan ka Kara ban yafe maka ba.

Jin kalmar yayi kamar saukar aradu tsayawa yayi kaman andasa shi.

"Jeka bama bukatar ka mun sallamawa matar ka duniya da lahira tunda ba'abakin komai muke agunka ba.

Bata tsaya jin abinda zaice ba ta nufi ɗakin ta, shikuwa Hashim ji yake  kamar ana zare masa Abu ajiki Nan danan ya fara hada gumi kan kace me hajijiya ta fara daukar sa ganin Yana niman faɗuwa dama Kuma tana tsaye bakin kofa tana jin duk Abinda ke faruwa da gudu ta fito daidai lokacin yayi kasa cikin sa'a  ta tallabo shi ta baya fadi kasa  sukayi gabaki dayan su.

Raza nanniyar karar da ta kwalla shiya fitar da Mami da gudu ganin Hashim kwance kamar gawa yasa ta karasu gurin da sauri ta fara jijjiga shi tana Kiran sunan sa Wanda tunda yazo duniya Bata taba kiransa da sunan saba.

Da gudu safnah ta fito Jin kukan Mami da tayi tana ganin halin da ake ciki da gudu ta nufi gurin driver,dawowa sukayi tare ,kinkimar sa sukayi
  sai asibiti, Maryam kuwa kasa tashi tayi Dan babu shakka ta samu kariyar kafa Dan wani irin zugi da kafar ke mata, ganin baxata iya tashi ba gashi Kuma Mami da safnah sun nufi asibiti yasa ta kwanta agurin tana kuka ga zugi ga tunanin halin da hashim yake ciki.

2⃣5⃣⏏2⃣6⃣



*Oo to mee it a very sad news.. Mumma is gone and gone for rever. Take heart dear in Sha Allah I know he's gone for good*




_________Emergency room aka nufa dashi Mami kuwa baƙaramar tashin hankali ta shiga ba ganin tilon ɗanta cikin mawuyacin  hali, safnah cema tayi dabarar Kiran Abba awaya shima cikin kankanin lokaci ya iso asibitin Yana tambayar su abinda ya faru.

Mami da Banda hawaye babu abinda take ganin kusan awa babu Wanda yace dasu kala ga likitocin sai fita suke suna komawa.

Abba ne yake Dan kwantar Mata da hankali duk da shiɗin ma hankalin sa atashe yake.

Jin anbuɗe  kofar yasa Mami ɗagowa da sauri likitan dake duba shine yafito Yana yarfe zufa,cikin sauri Abba ya nufi gunsa Yana tambayar sa jikin nashi.

Alama yamasa daya biyu shi office dinsa.

Zama abba yayi Yana fuskantar likitan yayin da  shikuma yake Dan rubuce rubuce a wata karamar takarda saida ya KAMMALA sannan ya ɗago ya dube Abba yace,"yallabai mecece alakar ka  da mara lafiyar?

"Mahaifin sane ni.

"Yauwa abinda nake so na sanar daku shine alhamdulillah mun shawo kan lamarin.. Amma shawarar da nake so na baku itace agaskiya yanda na lura ciwon ɗanku bana asibiti bane,akwai bukatar ahada harda na gida wato addu'a,Dan yanda Naga  yake yi gaskiya akwai bukatar haka.

Da ma'maki afuskar abba yace,"to mungode sosai likita IN SHA ALLAH zamuyi amfani da shawarar ka.

Mika Masa takardar yayi  magunguna da ake da bukatar su saya Wanda basa dashi a ahalin yanzu.

Godiya sosai Abba yayiwa likitan sannan yanufi ɗakin da yake,Mami kuwa kaman dama jira take likita ya fita da sauri ta nufi ɗakin,ganin halin da gudan jininta yake ciki ba karamar girgiza ta yayi ba,da sauri ta rungume Shi tana kuka tana cewa,"meya sameka hashim?

" Daga hannuwa ta samatayi tana cewa"ALLAH ga ɗana kabashi lafiya ALLAH Kai shaidane maganar Dana faɗa masa ɗazu cikin fushine Amma bai kai cikin zuciya ta ba.

Ɗagota Abba yayi daga jikinsa Yana rarrashin ta, shikuwa Hashim Banda hawaye dake zuba ta gefen fuskar sa babu abinda yake Dan ko motsa dan yatsarsa bayajin zai iya....  Amma Yana jin duk Abinda suke fada.

Haka suka ɗauke shi suka nufi gida dashi Wanda Banda numfashin da dake kaiwa da komowa babu abinda zaisa kace Yana da Rai.

*******

Ita kuwa zainab tana zaune zaman jiran dawowar Hashim yazo su tafi, Amma shiru kusan awa tashi tayi ta shiga sashin Mami Dan ganin abinda ya tsayar dashi.

Shiru taji babu alamar da mutane agidan Dan tuni Maryam  tayi barci agurin ga kafarta kuwa ta tashi tayi sundum.

Da ma'maki take Kiran sunan Hashim Amma shiru duba gefenta tayi aikuwa ta hango maryam kwance babu ko dankwale akanta.

Da sauri ta karasa gurun kafarta tasa ta tokari kafar dake  ciwan  tana cewa,"ke Ina mijina?

Kara Maryam ta kwala Dan wani irin azababbiyar zafi dataji ya ziyarci kwalkwalur ta.

Ganin zainab atsaye tana jijjiga kafa yasa Bata Bata lokacin ta gurin tambayar Wanda yayi Mata wannan rashin imanin ba.

Gani maryam Bata da niyar amsawa yasa tace,"ke bada ke nake magana ba?

Nanma shiru Maryam tayi aikuwa babu Bata lokaci zainab tafara Kai Mata duka ta ko ina, ihun niman taimako Maryam take Amma Ina babu kowa agidan.

Zainab ganin kamar Bata wa kanta lokaci take yasa ta dauko plet da safnah taci abinci ta mance dashi agurin, ɗagawa tayi da zummar kwada mata.

Tsawa taji daga Bayan ta Wanda ba muryar kowa bane illa Mami wacce ta rigasu shigowa Dan ta gyara ɗakin da za'a ije Hashim dan bazata taba Bari akaishi dakin matar saba.

Sauke hannuwanta tayi cike da kunya  Dan batayi tsamanin zuwan ta cikin  wannan lokacin ba.

Ko kallon inda take batayi ba kuma  bata ce da ita komai ba, ganin halin da Maryam ke cike ba karamar ɗaga hankalin ta yayi ba, Amma sai ta daure dan yanzu ba lokacin kuka bane.

Komawa waje tayi tace dasu su shigo dashi, shigowa da Hashim akayi kamar matacce aka shimfiɗa shi  a sofa ,ganin Maryam akwance tayi rufda ciki yasa Abba kallon Mami da sauri yace,"meya same maryama Naga tayi irin wannan kwanciyar?

Hawaye ne kawai ke zarya akuncin Mami Bata ce uffan ba Banda nuna masa kafar Maryam da tayi sannan ga shatin hannu abayan ta radau kasancewar ta fara.

Zainab kuwa tana ganin anshigo da Hashim kamar gawa ta sandare agun ko motsi takasa.

Banda salati babu abinda Abba keyi daukar maryam yayi bai tsaya ko Ina ba sai asibiti cikin lokaci kalilan suka gyara Mata kafar sannan aka daure,dakin hutu aka nufa da ita.

Kallo ɗaya zakayi wa Abba ka gane Yana cikin tashin hankali Mami ya kira ya shaida mata halin da ake ciki .

Zama yayi kusa da ita Yana Jin yanda take sauke ajiyar zuciya tausayin tane ya cika Masa zuciya,Yana zaune ya jiyo sallamar  aunty ilham gaisawa sukayi sannan tayi mishi jajan abinda ya faru.

Saida Abba yaga farkawar ta sannan ya nufi gida Dan yariga ya Kira malaman da zasuyi saukar alqu'ani agidan.

Yana zuwa kuwa ya tarar harsun fara karatun,ɗaya daga  cikin su kuma yana ciki Yana yiwa Hashim ruke'a cikin kankanin lokaci da fara karatun Hashim ya fara Mika tare da hamma Mai karfi daga baya Kuma fisge fisge  ya biyu baya Saida aka kira Mai gadi da drive suka taimaka aka rirrike Hashim  malamin kuwa cigaba da karatun yayi cikin sa'a aka samu Hashim yayi barci.

Haka malamin yaci gaba da zuwa harna tsawan kwana bakwai uzuwa lokacin kuwa ALLAH ya karbi ijaba Hashim ya warke saidai har yanzu bai koma kamar daba, gashi ya tashi da wani irin tsanar zainab Dan ko ganin ta baya kaunar yi,Banda sunan Maryam babu abinda yake kira.

Ranar da Hashim ya dawo daidai har'yana kuka Dan ance yabari basai ya tafi asibitin ba Dan yau za'a sallamu su,tsabar farin ciki Saida Mami ta zubar da kwalla.

Zainab kuwa idan ka ganta zaka rantse tayi jinyar shakara da shekaru ne Dan gabaki daya abin duniya ya taru yayi Mata Kaka gida,ga hantarar da take fuskanta daga gun Hashim da Mami gwara safnah da Abba suna amsa gaisuwar ta Dan shi Abba cewa yayi ya mikawa ALLAH duk kannin lamuran sa.


***

Shafa'atu dake taimaka Mata Kuma tayi tafiya babu irin magiyar da batayi Mata ba kanta dawo domin sutafi gun bokan data amso Mata wancan asirin agunsa,Amma Ina kullum cikin cewa gata zuwa take.

Ranar da aka sallamu Maryam daga asibiti murna agun  Hashim ba'a magana Dan ko motsi tayi saiya tambaye ta koda wani Abu da take da bukata.

Ita kuwa Maryam kwata kwata ta kasa sakin jikin ta dashi, Dan abin nashi ma tsoro yake Bata ganin abin nashi bana wasa bane yasa ta fara kuka tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH Mami kicewa ya Hashim yafita wallahi bana kaunar ganin sa Mami Dan ALLAH kice yafita ya koma gun aunty zainab kafin ta shigo ta kashe ni.

Kuka maryam keyi kamar an Aiko Mata da sakon mutuwa ganin hakan yasa Hashim mikewa da zummar barin ɗakin, ganin Haka yasa Mami ta dago Kai ta kalle shi tace"ka zauna Ina Kuma zaka tafi?

Cikin muryar kuka yace,"Mami tace Bata kaunar ganina Kinga gwara nafita ko zata samu natsuwa.

Kallon sa tayi zuciyar ta cike da tausayin ɗan nata tace,"karka damu zan fahimtar da ita.

Jin hakan Yasa ya zauna ita kuwa banda ajiyar zuciya babu abinda take.

Kallon su Mami tayi tace,"inaso duk kanku kubani Aron hankalin ku,kuji abinda zanfaɗa muku.

,"Inaso kasani duk Abinda Maryam tayi maka Bata da laifi Dan ALLAH kadai yasan  irin halin data shiga alokacin, Dan Haka nake so kayi hakuri da duk abinda zaka fuskanta daga gare ta.

,"Ke Kuma Maryam inaso ki sani anaso kowani bawa ya kasance Mai yafiya yayin da ɗan uwan sa yayi Masa ba daidai ba.

Sunkuyar dakai Maryam tayi hawaye na zuba Dan wani irin zafi takeji akasar zuciyar ta.

Godiya sukayi wa Mami gabaki dayan su.

Zainab kuwa ganin babu sarki sai ALLAH yasa taje ta same Mami ta Bata hakuri kan duk Abinda tayi Mata harda Dan kukan ta, kasancewar mami mace Mai hakuri yasa tace"da ita babu komai.

Mami kuwa samun Hashim tayi ta Masa nasiha akan yanda taga sukeyi da matar sa.

Bayan wasu wattanni komai ya dawo daidai agidan Maryam da zainab sun koma kamar da lokacin da zasu koma kaduna Maryam tace batasan zancan ba saidai su tafi da ita,Haka kuwa akayi .

*back to story*

Sauke ajiyar zuciya Abba yayi yace,"to kaji sanadiyar zaman su kaduna.

Yusuf Banda hawaye babu abinda yake jiya ke inama ace alokacin mala'ikar mutuwa ya dauke shi ma ya huta.





Yusuf kuka yake kamar karamin yaro,
Abba da Aryan sukayi ta rarrashin sa Saida ya ɗauki lokaci Mai tsaye cikin wannan halin sannan sukayi wa abba sallama suka nufi gida.

*****

Koda suka tafi gida zazzaɓi mai zafi ne ya rufe Yusuf, ganin jikin nasa yaki daɗi yasa Aryan yake shawarar daukar sa zuwa gida Dan ya lura bakaramar so yake yiwa yarinyar ba.

Haka kuwa akayi Aryan bai Isa garin kaduna ba sai gabda ana kiran sallar magrib, shiga gidan yayi amma babu kowa,Mai gadi ne yake fada Masa cewa,"suna gurin da ake shagalin kamu.

Dafi kansa yayi Dan ganin yanda Yusuf ke fitar da numfashi,Kiran number mama yayi ya shaida Mata halin da ake ciki ai babu shiri ta haɗa komatsanta ta nufi gida.

Ganin halin da ɗan nata yake ciki bakaramar ta yar Mata da hankali yayi ba batare da Mata lokaci ba suka nufi asibiti.

Taimakon gaggawa malaman asibitin suka shiga bashi cikin kankanin lokaci suka shawo kan al'amarin cikin ikon ALLAH.

Shawarar wari likitan ya bawa mama Dan gane da ciwon zuciyan nashi, dan yanzun halin da ake ciki ya shiga matakin karshe.

Banda kuka babu abinda Mama keyi ai babu shiri ta nufi gida ta sanar da daddy abinda ake ciki.

Tara kowa da kowa daddy yayi harda iyayan yumna ya shaida musu duk Abinda ke faruwa ,da Kuma bukatar da yake da ita na a daga auran har zuwa lokacin da Yusuf zai warke.

Ba Haka suka soba Amma yaza suyi ciwo ya kauda komai.



Maryan kuwa abubuwa sunyi Mata yawa Dan cikin kwanakin Nan jinta take kamar Mara lafiya.



Sallamar aunty ilham ne ya katse Mata tunanin ta da sauri ta dago Kai tana murmushi tace,"aunty na.

"Na'am yar kanwana.

Kallon ta sosai aunty ilham tayi tace,Maryam idan son da nake miki gaskiya ne to kina cikin damuwa, kuma bake so ki faɗa  min.

Kamar dama jira take aikuwa ta fashe da kuka tana cewa,'wallahi ni kaina aunty bansan abinda ke damuna ba jinanake kamar sabuwar mutum .

Rarrashin ta tashiga yi cikin ranta kuwa tausayawa yarinyar tayi Dan tasan dole Bayan an daura aure tasan asalinta.

Murmushi tayi tace,"maza tashi ki shirya Kinga lokaci na kurewa ,karfi hudu zamu tafi kuma kinga yanzu karfi uku harda minti biyu.

Cikin sauri Maryam ta shiga toilet tayi wanka, fitowa tayi rike da towel tana tsane jikin ta,abinda ta gani ne yasa ta wurgar da towel din ta nufi kan katifar ta.

Lace ce purple am Mata ɗinkin Dogowar riga Mai dogon hannu ,tana cikin dubawa aunty ilham ta shigo ganin ko Mai Maryam Bata shafa wa jikin taba yasa ta hada rai tana cewa,"Maryam wallahi zamu samu matsala dake Wai mekike yi har yanzu baki shirya ba?

Mararrace fuskar ta tayi kamar zatayi kuka tace,"haba aunty kinbi kin damu, Wai kodai nice amaryar?

Bude ido tayi tana kallon ta Dan Bata tsamaci Jin tambayar ba,Amma saita waske tace,"ok Haka ma zakice ko? dan kinga na damu da kene shikenan.

Da gudu ta taso ta rungume ta tace,"a a kiyi hakuri aunty ilham wallahi wasa nake miki kiyi hakuri.

Shikenan bara nakira mai makeup tazo ta cancaɗawa ƙanwata kwalliya.

Murmushi Maryam tayi tana Jinjina irin kaunar da aunty su ke mata.

Bayan mintina talatin har angama kwalliyar atsaye na ganta gaban mirror bude baki nayi lokacin da na hada ido da ita ni kai na SAFNAH ALIYU Saida na raina kaina hahahaha lol.

Please rate my Maryam,

How is Maryam looking?

Bude baki aunty ilham tayi lokacin da tayi ido hudu da Maryam ,wani irin tausayin ta da take ji,Dan tasan Nanda mintina wannan farin cikin dake kwance saman fuskar ta zai bace.

Janyo hannun ta tayi basu tsaya ko ina ba sai ɗakin mummy, kallo ɗaya mummy ta yiwa maryam ta kawar da kai,Dan babu inda Maryam ta bar mahaifiyar ta suwaiba ,kamannin su daya Saida fari da Maryam zata nuna Mata, Dan ita farin mahaifin ta ta ɗauko.

Bakin veil dinta tasa ta goge karamar hawayan dake kwakarin zuba,da murmushi ta juyo ta kalli maryam tana cewa,"alhamdulillah babu abinda zance dakai ALLAH sai godiya daka bani kyakyawar yarinyar kamar wannan.

Tura baki safnah tayi tace,"Wai mummy wallahi nidai Bata fini kyau ba.

Atare duk suka fashe da dariya sannan Maryam tace,"karki damu yar'uwa wasa kawai mummy take, aikin fini kyau Ni kawai fari zan Dan nuna miki,Kuma kinsan baki kalace Mai tsada.

Bayan duk sun gama kimtsawa suka nufi inda aka ta nada domin kamu.

Kamar yanda aka tsara Haka akayi da wayau safnah taja Maryam suka shiga filin tare,Bayan an KAMMALA komai kowa ya watse amare Kuma suka Koma ciki.

Aranar ne ita ma aunty zainab ta iso da gudu Maryam ta fito ta dauki ikram tana jifata sama tana cafkewa, cike da farin cikin ganin su tace,"aunty sai yau?nida NASA idon. Ganin ku tun jiya.

"Hmmm ke dai bari Maryam ya'yan ki Wai aure zai kara.

Gwalo ido waje tayi tace,"haba dai aunty kodai wasa yake Miki .

"Wani irin wasa mutumin da har tada gini yayi acikin gidan, nidai Kinga mu shiga ciki.

Bayan aunty zainab sun gama gaigaisawa da kowa da kowa ta kalli mummy tace,"mummy inaso muyi magana.

,"To Ina jinki zainab.

"A a mummy mudai shiga ɗaki.

Aunty ilham ce tayi dariya tace,"yau Kuma abin yar wariya ce?

Tashi sukayi batare da mummy ta tanka Mata ba,suna shiga aunty zainab ta mararrace fuskar ta kamar zatayi kuka tace,"mummy Dan ALLAH abinda Hashim yake ya kyauta kenan?

Da ma'maki mummy ke kallon ta tace,"wani abin Hashim din yayi ?

"Mummy waifa aure zai kara Dan ALLAH mummy ki Hana shi.

"Murmushi mummy tayi tace,"a Kinga zainab Ni bazan hana yaron nan Kara aure ba,dan sunnar ma'aki ce,saidai zanyi Masa nasiha da yaji tsoron ALLAH yayi adalci atsakanin k...

Mi kewar da aunty zainab tayi shiya Hana mummy karasa maganar ta daga Kai tayi tana kallon ta.

"Shikenan mummy wallahi tunda hakane saidai Hashim ya zaba ko Ni ko amaryar da yake Shirin auro wa a duk lokacin daya shirya,Bata tsaya jin abinda mummy zata ceba ta fita daga dakin tana share hawaye.

Mummy kuwa murmushi tayi tace,"hmm zainab manyan kasa, kenan ma bakisan cewa gobe ne daurin auran ba.

Tashi mummy tayi ta koma cikin mutane suka cigaba da gudanar da harkokin dake gaban su.

🍃🍃🍃🍃🍃🍃

Hashim kuwa Yana tare da abokan sa sai zolayar sa suke Wai yarasa wacce zai aura sai ƙanwar sa.

Banda murmushi babu abinda yake ganin maganganun nasu yafara isar sa yasa ya dauke su yace suje yanuna Musu amaryar sa.

Babu musu suka nufi gidan, dake kawai Jin labarin ta suke Basu taɓa  ganin taba,yasa kowa acikin su yake ɗokin ganin ta, Dan Hashim kullum cikin zana taswirar haliltar ta yake.

Suna zuwa gidan Hashim ya gabatar dasu ga mummy gaisawa sukayi cikin girmamawa, sannan Hashim yace,"mummy Wai Ina maryam?

Daidai lokacin ta fito daga ɗaki da gudu tana kiran safnah, hannun ta rike da waya da alama Kiran safnah ake awayan, dogowar rigar Pakistan ke jikin ta ruwar piek kayan sunyi Mata kyau sosai ,kanta babu dankwale Banda sheki babu abinda sumar kanta keyi.

￼

Hashim ji yayi kamar zuciyar sa zata Faso kirjin sa ta fito saboda wani irin matsanan cin kishi dake taso masa ganin yanayin shigar da tayi.

Abokan sa kuwa kasa dauke idonsu daga kanta sukayi,kowa da abinda yake rayawa dan Basu masan itace amaryar abokin nasu ba.

Cikin girmamawa ta gaishe su sannan ta dubi ya'yan nata tace,"ya'ya inata niman ka banganka ba Wai Ina kashiga ne?

Murya adakile yace,"inanan babu inda naje.

"To ya'ya na kawa maka abinci? 

Ganin Bata da niyar barin wurin yasa ya daka mata tsawa yace,"baza'aci ba, kuma karna karaji kin furtawa ko kalma daya tunda ke sakarya ce, maza bace minda gani.

Jiki na rawa ta nufi ɗakin ta ciki da Ma'maki Dan asanin ta babu abinda ya haɗa su,kwanciya tayi saman gado tana nazarin yanda rayuwa zata kasance Nan gaba gashi Bata San halin da Yusuf yake ciki ba.


Mummy kuwa faɗa  ta shiga yiwa Hashim tace,"kanme zaka Mata irin wannan tsawar?

"Mummy bakiga yanda ta fito ba,mummy babu fa ko dankwali akanta.

Murmushi mummy tayi dan ta gano tsantsar kishi a kwayar idonsa.

"Shikenan ALLAH ya kyauta.

Fitar mummy keda wuya, abokansa suka shiga zolayar sa kan yanda ya nuna kishin sa afili babu ko kunyar mummy.

Dariya yayi yace,"to ya kuka ga kanwar tawa?

Yaba Maryam suka shigayi harsuka bar gidan.

Misalin 4am Maryam ta tashi afirgice, fuskarta duk zufa da alama mafarki tayi,Saida tayi addu'a ta shafa a ilahirin jikin ta,sannan ta sauke nan nauyar ajiyar zuciya,tunawa da mafarkin da tayi Wanda ta kasa gane ma'anar sa.

Ahaka take zaune ta kasa komawa barci har'aka soma kiraye kirayen sallah,tashi tayi ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da sallah, Bayan ta idar,
mikewa tayi ta nufi ɗakin mummy.

Kamar yarda tayi tsammani asaman sallaya ta same ta, zaune da alama azkar take.

Ganin maryam yasa mummy ta sallame cike da murmushi tace,'yar autar mummy ya akayi?

Saida ta gaisheta sannan tace,"mummy mafarki nayi.

"Wacce irin mafarki kuma?

"Wallahi mummy mafarki nayi Mai ciki da abin tsoro da ma'maki.

"Maryam Kindai San mafarki ba gaskiya bace ko?

"Eh mummy na sani saidai akwai mafarkin da yake zamowa gaskiya.

"To Ina jinki.

"Mummy wata Mata nagani sanyi da fara'ar tufafi goshin ta na kyali hakuran Tama na fitar da farar haske,magana tamin akanki keda ya'ya.

Jin Haka yasa mummy tace,"uhm ina sauraron ki me tace?

"Mummy cewa tayi na kasance Mai biyayya ga duk Abinda kuka sakani, sannan karna yarda na saɓawa umarnin ku,idan nayi Haka to babu shakka zanyi nisa da albarkar ta duniya da lahira,Kuma mummy ina yanayi da matar.

Zufar dake kan fuskar ta tasa hijabin ta tagoge dan ita kanta mafarkin bakaramar girgiza ta yayi ba,kuma babu shakka suwaiba ce matar da Maryam tayi mafarki da ita,to kodai bataso hukuncin da suka yankewa Maryam ɗin bane?

Jin mummy tayi shiru yasa Maryam ta dan tabata tana cewa,"mummy kinyi shiru.

Murmushi tayi Wanda iyakar sa fatar baki tace,"karki damu autata Ina nazarin mafarkin da kika yine,jeki ki kwanta ki huta babu komai mafarki ne kawai.

Babu musu ta tashi ta nufi ɗakin su,tana zuwa ta tarar da aunty ilham da SAFNAH suna drama, kan Wai sai safnah Tasha wani Abu da aka dama cikin cup ita Kuma tace attapap bazata shaba.

Ganin Maryam yasa Aunty ilham ta nufo gunta tana cewa,"yauwa autar mummy amshi Kisha abinki tunda ita tace bata sha .

Karba Maryam tayi batare da gardama ba ta kurbe, aunty ilham kuwa hada ido sukayi da safnah suna dariya kasa kasa.

Kwanciya Maryam zatayi Amma aunty ilham tace Sam saidai tayi wanka su shirya Dan karfi 8am za'a daura aure.

Haka tana ji tana gani barci ya gagare ta ,wata dakakkiyar lace aunty ilham ta fito musu dashi komai iri ɗaya,da ma'maki Maryam tace,"Wai kaya iri daya zamu saka ne?

"Nifa baxan saka wannan ba anko zansaka.

Mararrace fuska safnah tayi kamar zatayi kuka tace,"yanzu saboda Zaki saka Kaya iri ɗaya Dani shine kike wani yatsina fuska?to shikenan kiji ki saka wacce ranki ke so.

Yanda safnah tayi magana yasa Maryam ta dauki Kayan ta saka sannan ta nufi inda take tace," kiyi hakuri sister wallahi banfaɗi hakan da wata manufa ba.

￼

Juyowa tayi tace,"shikenan dama niba fushe nayi dake ba.

Karfi 7:30am harsun gama Shirin su tsaf dasu sai tashin kanshi suke, Mai kwalliya kuwa ta baji kulenta afuskar su,Dan kyau iya kyau sunyi ta harsun gaji.

Abu daya ke daurewa Maryam Kai, shine duk inda ta gifta sai taji ana cewa amarya kinsha mai,wani gurin Kuma Taji ana cewa amarya kinyi kyau harkin gaji.

Ita dai murmushi kawai take cikin ranta kuwa ma'maki take yanda basa iya banbanta tsakanin ta da yayar ta safnah.

Yaune 31-8-2019 alkawarin ALLAH ya cika an daura auran Maryam isah da angon ta Hashim yunusa Mai kanti,da ka kanwar sa safnah alhaji yunusa Mai kanti da angon ta safyan alhaji abdullahi.

Maryam tana kwance sai faɗuwar gaba da take yawan yi,ga wani irin zazzabi daya rufeta lokaci ɗaya.

Lura da yanayin da take ciki yasa safnah ta kira aunty ilham ta faɗa  mata,babu Bata lokaci aunty ilham ta iso ɗakin,jikinta ta taba taji da zafi, aikuwa da sauri ta fita dan faɗawa  mummy halin da ake ciki.

Ko kafin mummy tayi wani kwakwarar motsi Yan barka da ALLAH ya Kara arziki suka shigo, Yan matan makwabta kuwa suka nufi dakin su,wata yar makkociyar su wacce ake kira salima itace taje da gudu ta faɗa  jikin Maryam, batare da la'akari da yanda ta ganta alullube ba.

Da sauri maryam ta tureta tana cewa,"haba salima wannan wacce irin wasa ce baki ganin bana da lafiya ne?

Murmushi salima tayi tace,"ba dole kiyi rashin lafiya ba kin shiga sabowar rayuwa.

Da ma'maki afuskar ta tace,"kamar ya bangane ba?

"Eh mana dole kice baki gane ba tunda kin boye Mana zancan auran ki, Dan kar muzo muta yaki murna.

Murmushi tayi tace,"ke kuwa salima wani irin aure Kuma ?idan zanyi aure saina boye muku menene hakan zai kare nida shi?

Ganin da gaske take yasa salima tace,"wallahi Maryam auran ki aka daura da ya'yan ki hashim.

Dariya Maryam take harda rike ciki tace,"gaskiya salima kin haukace kin taba ganin inda ya'ya ya aure kanwar sa?

"To nima Haka nace shine umma na take faɗamin cewa,"mummy ba ita bace ta haifi ki.

Wani wawan mari Maryam tayiwa salima wanda Saida ta dafa gado.

Da jajayan idanuwan ta Wanda suka sauya lokaci ɗaya tace,"karki Kara bana irin wannan wasan da kowa, Kuma bazan taba ba,ki dinga sanin abinda zai rinka fitowa daga bakinki Kindai ji na faɗa  miki.

,"Dan na faɗa  miki gaskiya Maryam shine Zaki mare ni?

Ganin haryanzu salima tana kan bakarta yasa tace,'salima wasa kike ko gaski?

"Wallahi da gaske nake umma na ta faɗamin cewa,"ba mummy bace maman ki Haka Abba ma bashine mahaifin kiba.

"To suwaye suka haifeni?su wAye iyaye na?meyasa ban taba ganin suba?

Duk ajire tayi Mata tambayar,ganin bazata samu amsa gam sasshiya daga bakin taba yasa ta nufi kofar dakin da niyyar zuwa gun mummy,daidai lokacin Kuma mummy ta sako Kai zata shiga dakin hannuwan ta rike da magunguna.

Tsayawa Maryam tayi tana kallon ta ido cikin ido ga hawaye dake zarya akan fuskar ta tace,"mummy yau dai ki faɗa  min gaskiya Dan ALLAH Kuma kiyi hakuri da tambayar da zan miki.

"Wacce irin tambaya Kuma Maryam Bayan ance baki da lafiya,kowace irin tambaya ce ki bari sai kin samu sauki sai naji ko menene.

"A a mummy na warke.

"To Ina jinki.

"Mummy Dan ALLAH Wai bake bace kike haifi ni?

Shiru mummy tayi Dan tasan Rana irin wannan zatazo Koda kuwa ba Hashim bane ya aure Maryam, Dan Haka bataga amfanin boye Mata ba, dan Haka tace,"tabbas Maryam bani bace mahaifiyar ki,Amma ki yarda Dani muna jinki kamar yar'da muka Haifa da ciki mu.

Wani irin duhu Maryam ke gani kankace me tuni ta yanke jiki ta fadi.....❄❄❄❄❄

*STORY & WRITING*

*BY*

*SAFNAH ALIYU JAWABI*

2⃣7⃣⏏2⃣8⃣

```It doesn’t matter who we are. We all have our successes and failures. We all make mistakes but what is really matter is how we move forward and how much we learned from them!```


*For you SAFNAZ* ❤❤❤


_______Agigice mummy ta kwalla kara tana Kiran sunan ilham.

Jin yanayin Kiran yasa Aunty ilham ta kwasa da gudu Bata tsaya ko Ina ba sai dakin,ganin maryam akwance yasa hankalin ta yakara tashi,da sauri ta fita waje tana kwalla Kiran drive.

Yanda take Kiran drive cikin tashin hankali shiyayi sana diyar janyo hankulan mutanan dake gurin.

Atare da drive suka shigo daukar ta mummy da aunty ilham sukayi sai asibiti,safnah taso binsu Amma mummy tace ta zauna yanzu zasu dawo,tana ji tana gani suka tafi.

Da gudu ta karasa cikin gidan tana kuka,daukar wayar ta tayi ta Danna number hashim, ringing Diya ya dauka Yana cewa,"Yaya akayi amarya?

Cikin muryar kuka ta zaiyana Masa abinda ke faruwa,batare da ya bata amsa ba ya katse wayar cikin tashin hankali ,ko tsayawa sallamar abokansa bai tsaya yayi ba,ya figi motar sa bai tsaya ko Inaba sai asibiti.

Yana Isa yatarar da mummy da aunty ilham sai safa da marwa suke tsakanin dakin da bakin  kofar asibitin,gurin mummy ya nufa Yana tambayar ta abinda ya faru.

Bata kaiga budi baki ba likitan dake duba ta yafito ,cikin sauri Hashim ke tambayar sa jikin nata?

"Hmm KU kwantar da hankalin ku bawata babbar matsala bace,jinin tana ya hau, amma da yardar ALLAH mun shawo kan lamarin saidai  adinga kulawa,yanzun dai munyi Mata alurar barci nanda dan wani lokaci zata farka.

"Mum gode sosai likita.

"ALLAH ne abin godiya.

Nan nauyar ajiyar zuciya Hashim ya sauke Yana Mai kara godiya ga ALLAH daya cika Masa burin sa.

🎄🎄🎄🎄🎄🎄

Aunty zainab kuwa tana Nan agidan su Bata masan wainar da ake toyawa ba, Dan tunda sukayi wannan maganar da mummy tabar gidan,koda aka daura aure tana gidan su,babu yanda mamin ta batayi ba kanta koma can gidan bikin, Amma kememe tayi tace,"ita babu inda zata,adole an Bata Mata rai.

Tana zaune wayar ta dake cikin jakar ta yafara kuka Yana niman agaji,da tsaki ta bude jakar ta Ciro wayar,sunan data gani shiyasa tayi murmushi ta dauko tana cewa,"a a yau sister an tuna Dani kenan?

"Dalla malama  kina ina?

"Ina gida sister meya faru?

"Awani gidan kike?

"Gida gurin mami.

"Ai sai  kiyita Zama anacan ana shagalin biki Hashim ya Kara aure.

Cikin daga murya tace,"wani hashim?

"Banza wani hashim Kika sani?

"Sister kinsan wannan wasan naki Yana iya sanadiyar bari na gidan duniya Dan ALLAH ki daina.

"To shikenan sannan wani Karin abin takaicin, yarinyar da kika Raina da hannuwan ki ya aura.

Tsawar da aunty zainab tayi shiyayi sanadiyar fitowar abban su da Mami cikin sauri ganin ta rike da waya yasa suka tsaya suna sauraron ta.

"Sister kikace maryam ya aura,dama Maryam ba kanwar sa bace?

"Eh  nima Haka naji.

Katse wayar tayi tana wani irin numfashi kamar zata hadiyi zuciya ta mutu,ganin Haka yasa Mami ta debo Mata ruwa Mai sanyi a cup ta mika mata.

Karba tayi,sannan tayi wurgi dashi jikake tas karar tarwa tsewar cup,da hanzari Mami ta nufi gurin ta tana rarrashin ta,ita kuwa cewa take,'wallahi sai nayi ajalin su gabaki dayan su, harni za'ayiwa cin amana.

Tsawa abban su ya daka Mata, Yana cewa,"ke sakaryar inace?Haka mace keyi idan anyi Mata kishiya aka fada miki?to jiki ki Kashi ta koke kashe su kamar yanda kike ikrari maxa jeki.

"To bara kiji  kafin Hashim ya yarda ya aure ki,Saida mahaifinsa ya fadamin wannan kullailiyar alkawarin,Kuma da yarda ta akan maganar auran sai mee?

Tsalle ta daka ta Fadi akasa tana birgima kamar karamar yarinya,janyi Mami yayi suka nufi daki suka barta agurin tana sharbar kuka iya son ranta.

Ganin zaman bazai amfanar da ita komai ba, yasa ta tashi ta dauki jakar ta sannan ta Goya ikram abayan ta,Bata tsaya ko Ina ba sai gidan biki.

Da zage zage ta shigo gidan, mutane sai binta da kallo ake kowa na fadan albarkacin bakin sa.

Tana isa dakin safnah kawai ta tarar akwance tana sharbar kuka,ganin aunty zainab yasa safnah mikewa tsaye tana jiran jin dame tazo.

Aikuwa ashar ta shiga zundumawa, tun safnah tana kawaici harta fara amsa Mata, kankace me mutane sun ciki dakin,da kyar aka samu aka shiga tsakanin su.

Jin Maryam tana asibiti yasa zainab daukar motar ta tanufi asibiti Dan ta kudiri aniyar saita hallakata a yau basai gobe ba.

Nikuwa safnah aliyu jawabi nace anya Zaki iya kuwa?

💮💮💮💮💮💮

Yusuf kuwa Yana farfadowa da sunan Maryam ya farfado, dube dube yafara yi, lokacin daya tabbatar wa kansa a asibiti yake,kuka ya fashe dashi Yana cewa,"dama ALLAH ya dauki ransa daya fiye masa alkairi.

rufe masa baki  mummyn sa tayi tana cewa,"yusuf karkayi sabo ka zama mutum Mai tawakali aduk halin daka tsinci kanka,karka manta ALLAH subhanahu wata'ala Yana cewa"innallaha ma'assabirin,dan Haka nake horar ka, daka zamo Mai hakuri ka rungumi kyautar da ALLAH ya baka,wata Kila akwai abinda yake nufi da Hakan.

Kuka Yusuf keyi iya karfin sa,danshi kadai yasan irin kunar da zuciyar sa keyi masa.

Barinsa tayi Saida yayi Mai isarsa, sannan ya bukaci data taimaka masa da ruwa zaiyi alwala Danya Rama sallolin da ake binsa.

Yana idarwa daidai lokacin yumma da mamin ta suka shigo,ganin sa saman sallaya ba karamar dadi yayi wa yumna ba,da murmushi ta gaida shi hade dayi masa ya jiki.

Da Kai ya amsa fuskar sa ba yabo ba fallasa,Saida suka Dan taba hira kadan, sannan sukayi wa mahaifiyar Yusuf sallama suka tafi,duk da ba haka yumna taso ba amma babu yanda ta iya.

Saida Yusuf yayi kwana goma cir a asibiti sannan aka sallamu su,suka dawo gida cikin da murnar samun lafiyar sa.

💞💞💞💞💞💞

Ita kuwa Maryam Saida tayi kusan awa biyu Kannan ta farfado,da kalmar shahada abakin ta,da Hashim suka fara hada ido kasancewar shine yafi kusa da ita,rike hannuwan sa tayi cikin muryar kuka tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya kace min ba gaskiya bane abinda kunnuwa na suka jiyo daga bakin mummy.

Yanayin data tsare shi da idanuwan ta masu Kashi Masa jiki yasa Sam yakasa Bata amsa sai binta da kallo da yake.

Ganin bashi da niyar Bata amsa yasa ta mike ganin Abba tayi atsaye,cikin sauri tace," yauwa Abba ALLAH yasa kaima kazo, Abba da gaske baku kuka haife ni  ba?

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke Yana Jinjina girmar maganar sannan yace," inaso ki sani Maryam Kuma kiyi hakuri da kaddarar da ALLAH ya hukunta akanki, tunkina da karamcin shekaru,kuma kisani da gaske NE bamu muka haife kiba.

Wani irin kuka Maryam ta fashe dashi. daidai lokacin suka jian banko kofa dakin,da sauri kowa ya maida idanuwan sa kanta, Aunty zainab ce atsaye tana girgiza kafa tana kuci kamar kububuwa.

Ganin Bata da niyar karasowa ga kuma wani irin tsayuwar rashin da'a da datayi yasa Hashim ya karasa gunta Yana cewa,"yakika tsaya anan? ki karasa mana"

Wani irin mugun kallo data aika masa dashi shiyasa ya gano bada arziki tazo ba,Nan danan ya koma mata asalin Hashim data sani yace,"ke bana son raini meya kawo ki?

Ta inda aunty zainab ke shiga Bata Nan take fitowa ba, cin mutumcin kala kala babu Wanda batayi wa iyayan saba,ganin abin yana niman  wuce gonna da iri yasa ya wanke ta da Mari har sau biyu bai jira ta gama dawowa cikin haiyacin taba ya kara Mata da saki daya.

Wani irin ihu take tana durawa Maryam duk wata ashar da tazo bakin ta.

Mummy kuwa sakin ta da Hashim yayi bakaramar daka Mata jiki yayi ba, Amma yata iya aikin gama ya gama Amma tana jin tsoron yanda jama'ar gari zasu dauki al'amarin.

Abba kuwa gabaki daya kasa magana yayi, Saida aunty zainab ta tafi ya shiga yiwa Hashim fada kan hukuncin daya dauka cikin fushi.

Maryam Kuma kuka take da iya karfin ta domin damuwa yayi Mata yawa takasa tantance awani duniya take.

Rarrashin ta yayi bayan hankalin ta ya kwanta ya zaiyana Mata labarin iyayan ta babu abinda ya boye mata.

Bayan data gama Jin labarin ta jitayi kamar annikka matsayin da suke dashi da agunta,Dan tasan Koda iyayan ta suna Raye iyakacin soyayar da zasu nuna Mata kenan.

Komawa tayi ta kwanta tana tunanin makkomar ta da yanda zatayi rayuwa da hashim amatsayin mijin ta, ba ya'yan taba.

Mummy zuwa tayi har bakin gadon tace,"maryam yanzu da kika ji gaskiya da Kuma asalinki, shin Zaki dauke ni amatsayin suruka ne ko uwa?

Da sauri Maryam ta mike daga kwancan da take hawaye na zuba tace,"a a mummy har'abadan ke uwa ce agare Ni  bazan taba can Zaki ba.

Murmushi mummy tayi ta rungume ta tana share Mata hawaye Dan taji dadin maganar da Maryam ta fada.

Abba ne yayi gyarar murya yace,"to mummyn yara kizo mutafi gida lokaci na kurewa ,yakamata afara shirye shiryen Kai wannan yarinyar gidan ta, Dan nasan yanzu Yan daukar amarya suna kan hanya.

Gyara mata kwanciya mummy tayi Dan ita harga ALLAH Tama mance da ana biki,dago Kai tayi tace,"to dawa zamu bar Maryam din tunda kaga ilham ta tafi gida?

"Hmmm ke kuwa baga mijin taba,shi'ai saiya tsaya ya kula da ita kan anjima mu dawo.

Maryam najin haka ta rufe fuskar ta da tafukan hannuwan ta,Dan wani irin kunya da Taji ya lullube ta.

Dariya mummy tayi sannan suka fice.

Hashim kamar dama jira yake sufita, Aida sauri ya karasa gefin katifar hannuwan sa yasa ya tallafo kanta ya daura saman cinyar sa.

Rinsa idonta tayi dan jita ke kamar kasa ya tsage ta shige ga wani irin rawa da jikin ta keyi......



"Shikam abinnata ma dariya yake bashi, ji yanda jikin ta ke rawa kamar yace,"wani abun zai mata.

Bangaran ta kuwa wani irin  fatsari take ji Amma Sam bazata iya hada ido dashi ta fada masa ba,suna cikin haka suka jiyo sallamar aunty ilham,da sauri Hashim ya matsa daga gunta, Dan ko babu komai aunty ilham ta dan girme masa.

Dariya tayi tana cewa,"a a zauna abinka yamai jikin?da sauki alhamdulillah,garama da kika zo Dan ALLAH kidan zauna da ita, bara naje nayi sallama da abokai na,anjima kadan zan dawo.

"To saika dawo"

Yana fita maryam ta miki tana shagwaba fuska kamar zatayi kuka tace,"aunty wallahi fatsari nake ji"

"Shine baki fada masa yataimaka Miki kinyi ba?

Sunkuyar dakai Maryam tayi tana Mai Jin nauyin Bata amsa,ganin Haka yasa Aunty ilham ta cire mata ruwan, sannan ta taimaka Mata har zuwa toilet,Bayan tagama Suka dawo aunty ilham ta mayar Mata da ruwan.

Lumshe idanuwan ta tayi Sam ta kasa hada ido da Aunty ilham,tsaf aunty ilham tana lura da yanayin ta, gyarar murya tayi tace,"ke Maryam Wannan sun sunkuyar da Kai da kike duk ba zasu amfanar dake da komai ba.gwara ma ki daina ki nemarwa kanki mafita.

Murya kasa kasa tace,"to Aunty ilham niban san yanda zanyi ba"murmushi Aunty ilham tayi tace,"to karki damu yanzu inane ke Miki ciwo?

"Bana Jin ciwon komai yanzu"

"To bara na kira Hashim yazo yayi musu magana asallame mu kawai mu koma gida.

Cikin mintina sha biyar Hashim ya iso Yana zuwa da murmushi akan fuskar sa yace,"madalla da Aunty ilham na gode da kulamin da kanwata Kuma matata da kikayi"

"Babu wani dadin bakin da zaka min, nidai kawai amiko min tukuici na"

Hannu ya saka cikin rigar saya kwaso kudin da shi kansan baisan adadin suba ya mika mata,guda Mai dadin sauraro  Aunty ilham tashiga rarrawa daga bisani ta bisu da addu'ar samun zuri'a daiyi ba.

Bakaramar dadi Hashim yaji ba lokacin da aunty ilham ke jiro masa addu'a masu mahimmanci,ita kuwa Maryam azuciyar ta take amsa addu'a tare da maida martani da irin kalar tata addu'ar.

Bayan an sallame su sun koma gida daidai lokacin anzu tafiya da safnah,da kyar aka banbare ta daga jikin mummy, mummy ma dauriya kawai take.

Maryam kuwa shigewa dakinsu tayi ta sakarwa kanta ruwa ko zata Dan samu saukin abinda take ji,ga wani irin tsoron Aunty zainab daya darsu azuciyar ta lokaci daya.

Bayan ta fito ta saka karamar riga nashan iska harzata  zauna koma saita tuna da nanfa ba gidan iyayan ta bane kamar yar'da take dauka ada,yanzu zaman gidan surukai yake agunta.

Cikin sauri ta dauko hijabin ta ta saka,zamanta keda wuya aunty ilham ta shigo da murmushin ta tana cewa,'a a amaryar Hashim kece haka Kinga yanda ki kayi kyau kuwa?

Sunkuyar da Kai kasa tayi tana wasa da yatsun hannun ta.

"Nidai wannan kunyar taki gaskiya ta fara isata,dama Abba ne yace," idan an idar da sallar isha'a kuwa ya hallara afalo"

"To kawai tace ta koma ta kwanta.

Bayan an idar da sallah kuwa ya hallara afalon Abba dake akwai ragowar mutane agidan.

Da addu'a aka bude taron ,sannan Abba yayi gyarar murya yafara magana kamar haka,"da farko zan fara da baki hakuri ke Maryam,kan hukuncin da muka yanke akan ki,da fatan Zaki yafe mana"

Murya na rawa tace,"Abba Ni bakumin komai ba,Kuma nice zanni mi yafiyar ku,Dan ALLAH ku yafimin idan na saba muku ta kwace hanya.

"Babu abinda kika Mana Maryam saidai muce ALLAH ya baku hakurin zama da juna,ya kaiwar da shaidan atsakanin ku,ki Zama mace ta gari agun mijin ki,karki bari mijinki ya kwana da fushin ki,ko kuma yayi fushi dake Kona minti daya.

Idan Kuma kece yayiwa laifi sannan yaba ki hakuri, to ki hanzarta  hakura,hakan nee zaisa duk lokacin da yayi miki laifi yayi gaggawar baki hakuri.

"Sannan Kuma Kai Hashim, inaso kaje ka dawo da matar ka,sannan ka kasance Mai adalci atsakanin matan ka,karka fifita daya Akan daya,yin Hakan na iya janyo maka raini agunsu baki daya.

"IN SHA ALLAH za'a kiyaye"

"Alhamdulillah" Bayan  mummy tadan taba tata nasihar akayi addu'a aka watse da niyyar gobe IN SHA ALLAH za'akaita gidan ta.

🌺🌺🌺🌺🌺🌺

Aunty zainab kuwa da kuka da ihu ta shigo gidan kamar mahaukaciya,da sauri Yan aikin gidan suka rinka fito wa , sanadiyar ihun da suka jiyo yasa suka fito dan Ganewa idonsu abinda ke faruwa.

Mamin ta na fitowa yanda taga zainab Saida hankalin ta yatashi,Dan wallahi idan ba anfada maka ba zaka ratse mahaukaciya ce.

Da sauri ta karasa gunta, rungume ta tayi tana rarrashin ta lokaci Mai tsayi mamin ta dauka tana aikin rarrashin ta,tsagaita kukan ta tayi sannan ta zaiyana Mata karya da gaskiya da kuma sakin da Hashim yayi mata.

Aikuwa Bata tsaya bata lokaci ba ta nufi dakin ta, cikin kan kanin lokaci sai gata ta fito sanye da hijabin ta,kallon ta zainab tayi tace,"Mami Ina Kuma Zaki tafi ki barni?

"Gidan wayan can banzayan mutanan marasa mutunci zantafi nayi musu kaca kaca ,Dan wallahi ba'ataba ni atafi lafiya.

Murmushi jin dadi zainab tayi gani mamin ta ta daure Mata gindi.

Tace,"yauwa Mami shiyasa nake sonki uwa ta abin alfahari na,Amma Kinga yanzu yamma tayi ki Bari sai gobe saiki tafi"

Itama Mami murmushi tayi Dan bakaramar dadi taji ba,ganin zainab ta dan fara sau kowa sannan tace,"to shikenan ALLAH yakai mu.

"Amin Mami yunwa nake ji.

Cikin sauri Mami ta nufi kitchen ta dibo Mata coscos dinda ta dafa,aikuwa zainab ci take  kamar wacce ta shikara Bata ci abinci ba Dan rabon ta da abinci tun safe.

Koda daddyn su yadawo suka fada masa cewa yayi,"duk Abinda taga zai fiye Mata tayi danshi babu ruwan sa.

Sanin Hali irin nasa yasa Mami Bata fada masa zancan zuwan ta gidan iyayan Hashim gobe ba.

Aikuwa daddyn su nafita itama tasa kafa tafita Bata tsaya ko Inaba sai gidan tana zuwa ko sallama babu ta kutsa Kai cikin gidan.

Lokacin kowa ya hallara ana zaune ana Karin safe,ganin ta tsaya saman kansu tana jijjiga kafa yasa mummy ta mike tana cewa,"a a hajiya kece Haka da safiyan nan?zauna Mana aibake tsaya atsaye ba.

Dakatar da ita da hannu tayi tana cewa,"dalla can malama baxama naxo yiba, Dan Banga gidan Zama anan ba,cin mutumcin da kuka yiwa zainab wallahi kusani sai kunyi nadama,dama Haka Dan Adam yake Dan Adam butulu ne, wallahi Kundai ji kunya Dan kun zama macizai masu baku abinci kuke cizo,zata kara bude baki taji saukar mari daya kan daya.

Agigice ta juyo Dan ganin wani Mai karfin haline wannan,aikuwa Wanda ta gani Saida ta kusan sakin fitsari azaune,mijinta tagani atsaye Yana huci Dan duk Wanda ya ganshi awannan lokacin saiya tsorata da yanayin sa.

Tambayar ta yashiga yi cikin sa
Zare ido Yana cewa,"da izinin wa Kika taho nan?

"Shiru tayi kamar ruwa ya cinye ta,ganin yanda jikin ta ke kerma yasa Abba saka baki cikin maganar Yana cewa,"kayi hakuri alhaji kasan halin Mata, Kuma ai da gaske anyi Mata laifi tunda an sako Mata ya.

"Kafin na bude idona maxa kibar gidannan Kuma Wallahi sai kin San kinyi Dani.

Kamar dama jira take aikuwa ta fice da sauri.

guri alhaji talba ya samu suka zauna shida Abba, Danni kaina baxance ga abinda suke tattaunawa ba.

Bayan sun gama abba yakira Hashim,guri Hashim ya samu ya zauna can nisa, alhaji talba yayi gyarar murya yace,"Hashim Dan ALLAH Ina niman alfarma agunka dafatan zan samu.

,"Abba ka wuce niman alfarma aguna,umarnin kawai zaka bani Ni kuma na aiwatar.

,"Indai Haka ne to bana so ka mayar da zainab dakin ta, yanzu Ina Kara niman alfarmar ka barta agida ta gane kuranta, Dan naji duk irin rashin mutumcin da tayi muku.

Cike da ma'maki Hashim ke kallon daddyn zainab dan Yama rasa abin fada.

Mikewa tsaye alhaji talba yayi yana cewa,"shikenan tunda so kake kanuna min iyakata akan hashim.

Rike hannuwan sa Abba yayi yace,"wallahi ba Haka bane ka yarda dani,kawai dai bana so mutanan gari suyiwa abin ba hagon tunani ne shiyasa,Amma tunda hakan ya Bata Rain ka Ina rokon amin afuwa.

"To naga shine daidai da laifin data aikata, ALLAH ya shirya ta kawai zamu ce,bara na tafi Dan akwai mutanan da yakama ta na hadu dasu Nanda mintina kadan IN SHA ALLAH.

Sallama sukayi alhaji talba yatafi shikuma abba yashige ciki Dan bayaso suyi tafiyar dare.


Fitowa da motar da zasu tafi acikin ta Hashim yayi,motace wacce bai taba hawanta ba,dama ya ijetane domin  rana irin wannan,sanye yake da bakar riga  da Bron din wando yayi kyau abinsa kaman ka sace shi ka gudu.

Abin ma'maki bai kare ba Saida Maryam ta shiga gidan,wani ni'imantaccan kanshin data shaka Saida Taji wani irin natsuwa ya saukar mata lokaci daya.

Kara shiga cikin gidan sukayi,zubewa afalon sukayi suna kare Mata kallo.

Hashim kuwa alla alla yake Maryam ta dago Kai, ko babu komai Yana so yaga fararan idanuwan ta masu cike da kwarjini.

Aikuwa suna hada ido ya lumshe idansa kamar Mai Jin barci,ita kuwa Maryam jitayi kamar an tsikara Mata alura cikin sauri ta sauke idanuwan ta kasa.

Hashim batroom

Bayan yagama nuna Mata ko ina suka dawo tsakiyar dakin,ganin basu da niyyar komawa falo yasa ta mararrace fuska tace,"ya'ya Dan ALLAH mu tafi"

Kallon ta yayi sama da kasa yace,"kina so mutafi?

Cikin sauri tace,"eh.

"To shikenan zamu tafi Amma saikin rungume ni.

Zaro ido tayi kamar zatayi kuka tace,"Dan ..

Kafin ta karasa yace,"shikenan tunda bazaki yimin ba,sai kiyita tsayuwa agun, kwanciya yayi saman katifar sa yabarta atsaye tana raba idanuwa kamar Mai karya.

Wata dabara ce ta fado mata cikin sauri ta nufi kofar dakin da nufi fita,Amma ga ma'makin ta saita ji kofar arufe.

A shagwabe ta juyo tana kallon sa tace,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya kabude min na fita kaga akwai mutane afalo karsu jimu shiru.

"To laifin waye?kawai kimin abinda nace saimu tafi.

Ganin babu wata mafita face bin Umarnin sa yasa ta nufi inda yake tsaye idanuwan ta ciki da hawaye,shikuwa bude hannuwan sa yayi Yana jiran karasu warta.

Tana zuwa ta fada jikin sa ta fashe da kuka, shikuwa kankame ta yayi kamar,za'kwace masa ita.

Baya son jin kukan nata ko kadan,Dan jinta yake kamar ana diba namar jikin sa,cire ta yayi daga jikinsa, sannan yasa hannu ya share Mata hawaye yace,"muje tunda nine baki son gani maryam.

Bakaramar tashin hankali ta shiga ba ganin yanda yayi fushi,da gudu ta Isa gare shi batare da bata lokaci ba ta rungume Shi ta baya cikin muryar kuka tace,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya karkayi fushi dani kayi hakuri.

Juyowa yayi ya rungume ta Yana shakar kamshin dake tashi cikin sumar ta,lumshe idonsa yayi, it kuwa ta samu lallausar kirji tayi lamo ji take inama ta dauwama ahaka har'abadan.

Tunowa da aunty
Zainab tayi ai babu shiri ta zare jikin ta daga nashi,tsare ta yayi da ido Yana son gane dalilin da yasa ta zame jikin ta daga nashi,naranara tayi da ido tace,"Ya'ya kasan mutane suna falo suna jiran mu ko?

"Eh nasani sai akayi Yaya?

"Yaya Dan ALLAH kazo mutafi nidai,Jan rigarsa ta shigayi har suka kusa fita daga dakin,ganin idan bai yi wani Abu game dasu Aunty ilham ba,tofa ba zasu tafi cikin dadi ba,shikuma Yana bukatar kwana kusa da matar sa,duk da ya yiwa kansa alkawarin baxai ta bata ba, harsai lokacin data fara daukar sa a matsayin miji ba ya'ya ba.

Janyo hannuwan ta yayi yace,"ke babu inda zaki tafi wuce mu tafi ki kwanta ki huta gajiya.

Ganin bawani rikon kirki yayiwa hannuwan taba,yasa ta kwace hannunta  ta zura aguje.

Dama yasan Haka zata faru shiyasa bai rike hannun ta da karfi ba,bai tsaya bata lokaci ba yabi bayan ta da gudu, aikuwa tana ganin Haka ta Kara wuta.

Daidai lokacin aunty ilham ta fito hannun ta rike da pleat na abinci dan harta dafa musu fatan doya,juyowa Maryam tayi dan taga koya kusa kamata, batare da sanni ba ta bige aunty ilham,jikake tas karar fashewa pleat.

Hakan yayi daidai da karasowar Hashim gurin da sauri ya nufi gun maryam dan duk atunanin sa abincin ya zube Mata ajiki ne, tambayar ta yake bakinsa na rawa yace,"babb babu abinda yasame ki ko?

Hararar sa Aunty ilham tayi tace,"wato ni ba mutum bace ko?

Sosa Kai yayi yace,"kiyi hakuri ba haka nake nufi ba.

Maryam ma kasa daga Kai tayi Dan bakaramar kunya taji ba.

Babu shiri Aunty ilham ta tattara mutanan da suka zo tare suka Kama gaban su,Hashim har rukon karya ya dinga musu kansu tsaya su kwana,Amma kasar zuciyar sa fari kal yake jinta.

Kafin su tafi Saida Aunty ilham ta hadawa Maryam magunguna kala kala ta Bata sannan suka tafi.

Wannan kenan.

✨✨✨✨✨✨

Yusuf na hango zaune a lambu hannun sa rike da cup wacce take cike da juice Yana Sha Yana danne danne cikin wayar sa.

Watace can na hango ta nufo gurin cike da kissa da karairaya Dana kare Mata kallo sai Naga Ashe yumna ce,tana zuwa Bata tsaya ko Inaba sai cikin lambu,da sauri na dauko hijabina nabi bayanta Dan gano muku abinda ke fatuwa.

Da murmushi takarasa inda yake zaune,kamshin turaran da yaji ne yasa ya dago ido Dan ganin inda kamshin ke fitowa,suna hada ido yayi saurin sunkuyar da kansa kamar ma baisan da wanzuwar mutum agun ba.

Ganin Haka yasa annurin fuskar ta bacewa lokaci daya,sugunnawa tayi agurin hawaye tuni yagama wanke Mata fuska tace,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya Mena maka Haka ka tsanani?

"Shinda akwai wani laifi Dana aikata maka ne?meyasa baka Sona? baka tausayi na? ya'ya kai mafa kasan yanda radadin so take Dan kaima ka taba ka..

Wasu zafafan maru ka guda biyu ya sauke Mata saman lallausar fuskar ta,da hannu biyu ta rike kuma tunta tana wani irin kuka Mai cin rai.

Tashi yayi daga gurin ya nufi hanyar main falo,harya kusa Kai step da zai kashi ciki bai daina Jin sautin muryar  taba,ga maganar ta dake Masa yawa cikin kwalkwalur sa,tabbas yasan ciwan so,yasan yanda take da radadi,Amma bayajin zuciyar sa zata kara karbar wata soyaya har'abadan.

Ita kuwa yumna Saida tayi Mai isarta sannan ta tashi ta nufi gida.

Kwance Yusuf yake Amma Sam yakasa barcin,maganganun yumna sai yawo suke cikin zuciyar sa,yarasa dalili,wani irin tausayin ta yaji Yana ratsa jinin jikin sa.

Ganin dare nayi yasa yayi addu'a ya kwanta da kudirin gobe za'ayita takare.

Gari na wayewa Bayan yayi wanka ya shirya cikin shaddar sa fara anmata aiki da jar zari,hula ya dauko itama kalar ja ya daura, bakaramar kyau Yusuf yayi ba,bai tsaya ko Ina ba, sai sashin mamin sa,kallon sa mamin sa keyi cike da soda kaunar Dan nata tace,"a a wannan kyau Haka sai Ina da safannan.

Babu Bata lokaci yace,"Mami gidan uncle bala zani.

"Wani  uncle bala kuma?

"Mami yayanki mana,mahaifin yumna.

Da sauri Mami ta mike tsaya tana kallon sa tace,"Wai da gaske kake ko wasa?

"Wallahi Mami can zantafi.

"To naji mezaka tafiyi da safannan?

"Mami ki zauna gurin yumna zantafi,Dan sona ke musasanta junan mu kafin muyi aure.

Yusuf

Banda hamdala babu abinda Mami keyi cikin zuciyar ta,batare da bata lokaci ba ta dauko wayar ta number yumna ta danna,bugu daya aka dauka,bata jira sun gaisa ba tafara zaiyana mata abinda ke faruwa,yumna kuwa banda tasan ma'ma bazata taba Mata wasa irin wannan ba, da babu shakka saita karyata zancan..




Da rawar jiki yumna ke har hada kayan abincin data shiryawa Yusuf, cikin kankanin lokaci ta shirya komai ama'adanar sa,Bayan ta gama ta shiga toilet,mintina kadan ta gama Shirin ta, kwalliyar ta Mai kyau da tsari tayi.

Tana zaune a palor Taji hon din motar sa, Aida sauri ta koma daki tayiwa kanta barin turare,shigowa gidan yayi suka shiga gaisawa da umma ta.

Tsayawa tayi tana kallon sa cike da ma'maki,ganin Bata da niyar Zama yasa umman ta tashi tashiga ciki danta Basu guri su zanta.

Murmushi Yusuf yayi yace,"to ai saiki zoki zauna ko?

Wani irin kunyar sa Taji ya lullube taji ya lullube ta,ahankali take tafiya harta karaso gurin sa.

Shiru tayi dan tama rasa ta inda zata fara,candai tace,"ya'ya zo muje kaci abinci.

"Bazanci ba'

Nandanan hawaye yacika idonta cikin muryar kuka tace,"saboda me ya'ya?

"Saboda baki iya gaisuwa ba'

Cikin sauri tace,"ya'ya kayi hakuri"Ina kwana'

Murmushi yayi sannan yace,"to aibaki jira na hakura koba ai ko?

Itama murmushi tayi tace,"nasan Kama hakura tunda ya'ya na Mai saukin Kai ne.

Ciki da nishadi suka karasa dinning, bakaramar dadi abincin yayiwa Yusuf ba,Saida yagama sannan ya dube ta yace,"yumna dama magana nazo muyi dafatan Zaki bani Aron hankalin ki?

"IN SHA ALLAH.

"Da farko Ina Mai baki hakuri kan abinda yafaru abaya dafatan zaki gafarce ni.

Sunkuyar da Kai tayi hawaye na zuba Dan tunawa da tayi da halin data shiga ko ince take ciki Dan batama fita ko ba,"babu abinda kamin ya'ya idanma akwai na yafi maka duniya da lahira.

Murmushi yayi yace,"nagode kanwa na.

,"Babu godiya atsakanin mu ya'ya.

Yusuf bai bar gidan ba Saida suka shirya duk Abinda suke bukatar yi abikin auran su.

Wannan kenan.

🍄🍄🍄🍄🍄🍄

Zainab kuwa abin duniya yataru yayi mata yawa,gashi har yanzu Hashim ko kiran sa Bata gani ba,bare ta Saran zuwan sa,tun abin baya damunta har takai yanzu Bata iya barci, kullum cikin tunani take.

Mamin ta na lura da halin da take ciki, Amma ita ta daukar wa kanta alkawarin Koda zainab zata mutu tofa tabar auran Hashim har abadan.

Ganin abin bana karewa bane gashi Mami batada niyyar yiwa daddy magana kan komawar ta gidan mijin ta,hakan yasa ta nufi palorn daddyn nata,daidai lokacin daddy Yana aika ce aikace cikin laptop dinsa,da sallama ta shiga da murmushi kan fuskantar ta,ta gaida shi.

Shima cikin sakin fuska ya amsa haryana tambayar ta ikram, murmushi tayi kawai Dan gaskiya tana Dan Jin kunyar mahaifin ta,'tawannan fannin.

Ganin tayi shiru tana wasa da Dan yatsar hannun ta yasa daddy yace,"da alamu akwai magana abakin ki ko?

Kamar dama jira take aikuwa ta fashe da kuka,bai Hana ta ba Saida tayi Mai isarta sannna yace,"ke Kuma Haka akeyi daga tambayar ki saiki saka kuka eye?

Cikin muryar kuka tafara magana tana cewa,"daddy Dan ALLAH kayi hakuri kayafi min wallahi bazan kara ba.

"Nikam baki min komai ba, idanma akwai toni kam na yafe miki.

"Nagode daddy, sannan Dan ALLAH inaso kakira Hashim ya mayar Dani dak...

Tsawar da daddy yadaka mata Saida Taji kamar ta saki fitsari agun, nunata yayi da Dan yatsarsa yana cewa,"zainab yaushe naza ma abokin wasar ki? Koni sa'anki ne?

,"To bara kiji idan Kika kara zuwan min da magana kwatan kwacin wannan, wallahi kinji na ratse Miki saikin gane kuran ki,maza fice min adaki.

Tashi yayi hartana harde kafa,kan katifar ta tafada tana wani irin kuka Mai ban tausayi.

Shigowa dakin mamin ta tayi tana cewa,"Wallahi kin bani kunya zainab,kan mutumin da baima san kina Raye ba Kika saka kanki cikin damuwa haka, wallahi idan badan acikin hai'yacina nake lokacin da na haifeki ba,da wallahi sai nace can zamin ya'akayi,Dan Ni banga wani da namiji daya Isa ya walakan Tani ba.

Ko kallon ta zainab batayi ba,Dan yanzu ma Banda haushi babu abinda take jina mamin nata,harta gama surutan ta, Bata tamka Mata ba.

Shikuwa alhaji talba bakaramar tausayin ta yaji ba,kuma ya tabbatar da tayi nadama, saidai yayi hakanne danta kara yin hankali.

🥦🥦🥦🥦🥦🥦

Yau kusan satin Maryam biyu kenan agidan,Amma kullum cikin wasan buya suke da Hashim, Bata tashi fita sai taji fitar sa daga gidan, break fast kuwa Yana fita sallar asuba take shiga kitchen cikin sauri take har'hada duk Abinda tasan zai bukata tafita,dake idan yafita sallar asuba baya dawowa sai gari yadan soma haske,hakan ke Bata damar kammalawa kafin yadawo.

Hakan kuwa bakaramin damun Hashim yake ba,Amma Haka yasawa kansa hakuri da juriya Dan bayaso ya takura Mata ko kadan,zancan dawowar zainab kuwa Yana makale da zuciyar sa,gashi Yana Jin kiwar ikram.

Ganin abin nata bamai shirin karewa bane, yasa ya kudiri aniyar yau zai kawo KARSHAN wannan zaman da suke,Yana fita masallaci tafito ahankali kamar wata Mara gaskiya,ahankali take tafiya harta karasa kitchen din.

Cikin sauri ta fara aikin ta,shikuwa ana idar da sallah ya nufi gida,ahankali ya bude kofar main palor cikin sanda yake tafiya harya karasa kitchen din, kallon ta yake daga sama zuwa kasa, gabaki daya jiyayi kamar numfashin sa zai dauke Dan wata arniyar rigace ajikin ta.


￼

Maryam

Bude baki Hashim yayi Yana kallon kyawun haliltar ta,jitayi kamar ana kallo ta,ai babu shiri ta juya, Ware ido tayi tana kallon sa cike da tsoro,ganin Haka yasa yafara taku zuwa cikin kitchen din.

Maryam kuwa kowani addu'a dayazo bakin ta takeyi, Dan atunanin ta dukan ta zaiyi,tunkafin yakai gareta ta shiga bashi hakuri,kankace me hawaye yafara zarya akuncin ta.

Ga ma'maki ta Yana zuwa dago kanta yayi yashiga lasar hawayan dake sauka da harshansa,wani irin kerma jikin ta yashiga yi.

Shikuwa baima damu da kermar da jikin ta keyi ba,abinda ke gabansa kawai yake aiwatar wa,ganin tafara komawa da baya kamar zata Fadi yasa ya tarbo Bayan ta,fadawa jikin sa tayi tana mayar da numfashi sama sama.

Ganin Haka yasa Hashim kashe gas din data kunna,sannan ya dauke ta duk da turje turjen da take baisa ya sauke taba,bai tsaya ko ina ba sai dakin sa.

Ganin inda ya kawo ta yasa ta saki kuka tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH kayi hakuri wallahi bazan sake ba.

Shihhh karna Kuma Jin kin bude wannan wawwarar bakin naki kin dai ji abinda nace ko?

Nod da kai kawai hawaye naci gaba da zuba.

Sauketa yayi saman katafar,babu Bata lokaci yashiga cire mata kayan dake jikin ta, cikin sauri yake gudanar da komai,bai tsaya ba Saida ya rabata da komai na jikin ta,tsayawa yayi Yana karewa haliltar da ALLAH yayiwa wannan baiwar tasa, lallai yakasance daga cikin maza masu sa'a arayuwa,Dan bakowa ALLAH ke bashi irin wannan kyautar ba.

Ita kuwa rintsa idonta tayi, zuciyar tane yatsanan ta bugawa, danda zaka zauna kusa da ita babu abinda zai'hanaka Jin bugun zuciyar ta.

Kwanciya Hashim yayi saman katifar ya rungume ta Yana Mai sauke nan nauyar ajiyar zuciya,sumbatar ta yashi gayi baji ba gani,Saida yayi Mai isar sa sannan ya Kama  nipples dinta yashiga tsosa, Maryam jitayi kamar numfashin zai dauke ai babu shiri ta shiga Kai masa doka tana cewa,"Dan ALLAH ya'ya kabarni wallahi niba yar'iska bace Dan ALLAH.

Hashim bai sarara Mata ba Saida yaji yadan samu natsuwa sannan ya sake nipples dinta,wani irin radadi nipples dinta ke Mata, tsabar tsosa daya sha.

Rungume ta ajikin sa Hashim yayi Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya,Saida suka dauki tsawan lokaci ahaka,Jin yanda numfashin ta ke fita da sauri yasa yagane tayi barci,Kara rungume ta yayi Yana murmushi tunuwa da yanda take ta magiya dazu harda cewa ita ba yar'iska bace, Amma kalli yanda take barci cikin salama.

Har cikin zuciyar sa yake jaddada alkawarin dayayi wa kansa,ahaka barci yayi awan gaba dasu gabaki daya.....

"Inaga zandan dakata daga Nan, Dan na fuskaci wahalar da kaina kawai nake, Dan dayawa daga cikin KU basa godiya,Bayin ALLAH Ina laifin Maison faranta ranka?

______Cikin kwanakin kadan aka fara shirye shiryen auran Yusuf da yumna,kowa kagani cike yake da farin ciki, musamman ma amarya wato yumna, idan ka ganta babu abinda zaka fara hangowa sai afararan hakoran ta.

Bangaran Yusuf ma abinbai canza zani ba,Dan wani irin soda kaunar take bin kowace jijiya dake jikin sa,kowani lokaci suna manne da juna awaya.

Yau laraba yaune za'afara gudanar da shagulgular biki,amarya tayi shigarta Mai kyau da tsare,guri yayi guri Yusuf sai washe Baki yake.

Sai kusan yamma aka gama shagalin kamu da suka gabatar, saidai gobe Kuma wacce acikin tane zasu gabatar da arabiyan night .

Washa gari tun misalin 5:pm suka shirya dan za'afara karfe bakwai da rabi ne.

Yauma  Haka sukayi kwalliyar su Mai kyau ita da kawayan ta,anyi bidiri am cashe naira tayi kuka dan duk ya'yan gata ne.

Yaune alkawarin ALLAH ya cika andaura auran yusuf da amaryar sa yumna saidai muce ALLAH ya Basu zaman lafiya.

Gidane hadaddiya mahaifin Yusuf yasa aka Gina masa,aciki aka sauke amaryar sa,kuwa Saida ya kyasa kyawun gidan.

Farin ciki agun yunma ba'a magana, danjinta take kamar tafi kowa sa'ar zuwa duniya.

Bayan abokai sun shigo da ango, suka Dan taba barkwanci da juna, sannan suka bada kudin siyan baki kowa ya Kama gaban sa.

Bayan Yusuf ya dawo daga rakiyar abokan sa,dakin sa yanufa Saida yayi wanka ya canza kayansa zuwa na barci, sannan ya nufi dakin ta.

Yanda ya barta Haka yadawo yasame ta,yayi veil dinda ke kanta yayi Yana murmushi yace,"amaryar ta Kuma kanwata.

Itama murmushi tayi tace,"na'am mijina Kuma ya'ya na.

Dariya sukayi atare sannan Yusuf ya umarce tada ta tashi tayi alwala su gabatar da sallah.

Tashi tayi ta nufi toilet,Saida tayi wanka sannan ta dauro alwala,zaune yake Yana jiran fitowar ta.

Shimfida sallaya Yusuf yayi suka gabatar da sallah raka'a biyu kamar yanda (annabi) ya umarce mudayi.

Bayan sun idar da sallar yakama tsakiyar kanta, yayi Mata addu'a, sannan yayi Mata Dan tambayoyi da suka shafi addini Masha Allah duk tambayoyin dayayi mata ta amsasu daidai.

Hakan kuwa bakaramar dadi yayiwa Yusuf ba,Danko babu komai yana da kyau ka samarwa ya'yanka uwa na gari.

Tashi yayi ya nufi kitchen,plet ya dauko hannun sa daya rike da gasasshiyar kaza.

Zama yayi yabubbude kazar sanan yace,"Mrs Yusuf bismillah.

Nuke Kai tayi tace,"na koshi.

Janyo kwanun yayi zuwa gabanta sannan yashiga Bata abaki ,har Saida ta nuna alamun ta kwashi, sannan yafara ci shima,Yana gamawa ya kwashe kwanukan yakai kitchen.

Dawowa yayi yazauna Yana jiran fitowar ta daga toilet,dan Da alamun  brush take.

Murmushi yayi ganin haryanzu sanye take da hijabin ta,tana zuwa ta haye saman katifa ta kwanta.

Ganin Haka yasa yanufi clothes danta ya ciro mata nighty Mai kyau,dawowa saman katifar yayi ya mika mata.

Ware ido yumna tayi cikin shagwaba tace,"ya'ya nikam ahaka zan kwanta"

Cike da ma'maki yake kallon ta,"ahaka zaki kwanta fa Kika ce?

Yanda yayi maganar bakaramar bata dariya yayi ba,amma saita danne Dan karyaga ta Raina shi,karban kayan tayi ta shige toilet,Saida ta saka Kaya sannan ta dauro hijabin asama,Koda Yusuf yaganta baice da ita komai ba harta kwanta.

Kwanciya shima yayi kusa da ita,shiru sukayi nadan lokaci kafin Yusuf ya janyo ta jikin sa ,ya rungume ta,gabaki daya yumna jitayi kamar an tsomata cikin ruwan kankara tsabar bari da jikin ta keyi.

Ganin Haka yasa yusuf zagaye ta da hannun sa,  yayi Mata rumfa,wasa da dan yatsarsa ya shigayi saman fuskarta har zuwa lips,Bata Ankara ba taji lallausar lips dinsa saman nata,tun tana basarwa hata fara maida martani.

Ina ganin haka na tattara kwamutsai na nayi waje, lol lol 😋😋😋

Saida nayi wa'a biyu Ina zaman jiran su,sannan na koma,koda na koma yumna na gani kwance kamar gawa duk fuskar ta ta kumbura,ga hawaye da majina tayi kwalliya dasu.

Murmushi nayi nace barka yumna anshiga sahun mata LoL.

Daukar ta saf Yusuf yayi,bai dire ta koina ba sai toilet,Saida ya tabbatar ta gasu sosai,sannan ya barta danta sarkake jikin ta.

Fitowa yayi ya cire bedsheet din ya canzawata, sannan ya dauke wannan yasa a washing machine.

Koda ta fito taga komai agyare tsaf Saida Taji wani sanyin dadi aranta,Saida ta shirya tsaf sannan suka Koma barci,barci Mai ciki da farin cikin yumna tayi sabanin Yusuf da hawaye kawai ke tsiyaya ta gefen kuncinsa Dan jiyake har'abadan bazai taba daina son Maryam ba 

"Wannan Kenan.

Zainab kwance take duk zazzabi ya gama rufeta Banda rawar dari babu abinda take,ga wani irin zafida jikin ta ke fitarwa.

Shafa'atu ce tashi go dakin tana cewa,"sister waike wace iriyar sakarya ce?kena nufin nice zandafa miki abincin da Zaki ci?to wallahi mami tace,"ki gagguta tashi ki daura sanwan abinci,Kindai jina fada miki.

Da muryar tausayi take magana tace,"aiya sister sanin kanki ne bana girki ko'ada bare yanzu,Dan ALLAH kiyi kinji sister na.

Shewa shafa'atu tayi tace,"Kain ub**can wallah karyar ki Tasha karya Dan Wallahi bazan dafa abinci na bawa jibge giyar mace kamar kiba.

Mtsss tsaki tayi tafice daga dakin.

Kuka Mai cin rai zainab ta sake tana tunu gidan mijin ta uban ya'ya ta, Amma Idan ta tuna da cin amanar ta da Hashim yayi sai taji,gwara ma ta zauna agidan duk wahalar da zata Sha zata jure.

Hawayan kan fuskar ta tashare lokacin data tuna da musabbabin samunwar sabaninta da mamin ta.

Flash back

Azaune Zainab take Mamin ta na zaune kusa da ita,Mami tace,"yauwa zainab bawani abu yasa nace kizo ba illa maganar mayar da yarinyar Nan gun ubanta.

Waro ido zainab tayi waje tana kallon mami tace,"na mayar da ita gun hashim faKi kace Mami?

"Eh Abinda nace kenan"

Murmushi zainab tayi dan duk atunanin ta wasa kawai mami keyi,saidai ganin yanda Mami ta hakikice yasa ta murtuke fuskar tace,"gaskiya 
Mami bazan iya mayar da ikram gun hashim ba,kenan Kuna nufin bazan koma gidan hashim ba,ko mekike nufi?

"Tabbas abinda nake nufi kenan.

Bakaramar daga sukayi da Mami ba aranar,Koda daddy ya dawo Zainab ta fada masa baice da ita komai ba,hakan ne yakara tayar Mata da hankali ta shiga Kiran Hashim,shikuwa Yana ganin Kiran ta da Kuma yawan message da take yawan Aiko Masa da shi,amma bai taba dauka ko kuma reply ba.

Back to story

Share hawaye zainab tayi tafito ikram na biye da ita,Saida ta daura abincin sannan ta fara wanke wanke tanayi tana share hawaye.

"Wannan kenan.

Maryam kuwa tayi kyau abinta,danba karamar kulawa take samu gun Hashim ba, shima yayi kyau abinsa duk da kasancewa yanzu yafara dana sanin alkawarin daya daukawar kansa,Dan kullum daker yake samun barci, saboda ciwon Mara da yake fama da ita.

Yau kusan sati daya kenan da fara amsar magani Yana sha,saiya Sha sannan yake samun barci,yauma kamar kullum tana zaune akafar sa tana bashi abinci abaki ,Saida ta tabbatar ya kwashi sannan ta bashi ruwa yasha,sannan ta rakashi hargun motar sa,Saida taga yafice daga gidan sannan ta dawo cikin gida.

Gyara inda sukaci abinci tayi,sannan ta shiga dakin sa danta gyara,Saida ta gama gyarawa tsaf,harta zo zata fita taga wata farar takarda kamar ta magani,cike da ma'maki ta dauka Dan aiya sanin ta lafiya kalau yake,cike da zancan zuci ta warware takardar tana dubawa,Ware ido tayi lokacin datayi arba da sunan maganin.

Jiki asanyaye tabar dakin idonta na zubar da kwalla, Dan tsabar tausayin sa dataji yashege ta lokaci daya.

Kiran kawarta ikram tayi,cikin kankanin lokaci ikram ta karaso gidan Dan rabonta da maryan tun Bayan zuwanta  gidan fada Mata rashin  lafiyar Yusuf.

Yanda taga an ancanza tsarin gidan bakaramar ma'maki abin ya bata ba,Haka dai ta danne tashiga gidan.

Da gudu maryam ta fada jikin ta tana kuka, rarrashin ta ikram tayi sannan suka samu guri suka zauna, tambayar ta aunty zainab ikram tayi,Maryam kuka take kamar an Aiko Mata da mutuwa,Nan dai ta zaiyana Mata duk Abinda ke faruwa harda auran Hashim da tayi.

Guda ikram ta shige rarrawa, Maryam kuwa binta da ido kawai take,saida tayi Mai isarta sannan ta zauna tana cewa,"Maryam nata yaki murna Dan kuwa samun miji irin Hashim awannan zamanin sai anshirya,

Duka Maryam takai mata tana cewa,"ke dallah malama shawara nakira ki kiba ni ba surutu ba.

"Maryam gaskiya zan fada miki,ki cire wata kunya ki ijeta agefe, ki rungumi mijinki ,Mai kaunar ki,hakance kawai mafita agare ki.

Dadin abinma kin kware gun iya girki da kwalliya,saidai duk da Haka, danne tausayin sa zakiyi harsai kin kara gyara kanki, ta hanyar Shan kayan marmari da hade hade Wanda acikin gidan ki zakiyi, sannan Zaki iya bashi yasha gudun zargi In yazo yatarar kina sha.

Sosai ikram tabawa Maryam shawarwari kan yanda zata Rinka janwo hankalin sa kanta,tana janyo sha'awar sa yazama bashi da abin kallo inba ita ba.

Godiya sosai Maryam tayiwa ikram sannan ta rakata da alkawarin gobe zata taho Mata da kayan hadin.....


Ga wata yar karamar shawara mata,ku daina yarda da magungunar da wasu matan ke kawowa suce Kisha da sunan maganin mata,domin Yana da illoli da dama Dan bakisan Dame aka hada shiba, sannan kisani shi Wanda kikeyi domin sa,Idan Kika kamu da wata cutar bafa lale bane yaci gaba da zama dake,idan ma ya daure Yana zaune dake tofa wata rana saidai kiji ana rangada guda anyi sabuwar amarya acikin gidan ki.

Dan Haka nake bamu shawara Mata damu kula,basai kinsha wata magani ba,ga kayan Hadi kala kala Wanda kina zaune cikin gidan ki Zaki hada kisha kamar su kankana ayaba apply dadai sauran su,Dan ALLAH akiyaye.



Cikin kwanakin biyu Maryam duk ta canza takun ta,kullum cikin saka kana nan kaya take masu daukar hankali, sannan Bata tashi sakawa sai daidai lokacin da Hashim zai dawo.

Yauma kamar kullum wata arniyar rigace ajikin ta,kana iya hango na shanun ta aciki,kwance take saman tree sitter tayi nasha nasha ga shi ta barbaza Suman kanta kamar da gaske barcin take.

Hashim yans isowa yaga kofar abude ,da masifa ya shigo cikin falon Amma me yanda ya ganta Saida yaji nunfashin sa nashirin daukewa na wucan gadi.

Da rawar jiki ya karasa gunta Yana tasbihi ga ALLAH,tsugun nawa yayi daidai saitin ta yafara shafarta ahankali kamar Mai Jin tsoro,ita kuwa maryam dama babarce take ba ,duk Wannan cikin shawarar da ikram ta bata ne,jitayi kamar ta saki fitsari sabar yanda jikin ta ya dauki rawa ai babu shiri ta bude ido.

Hashim  na ganin ta bude ido yayi saurin cire hannun sa, kallon sa take cikin marairace fuska sannan ta tashe ta rungume Shi daga zaune boobs dinta na shafar kirjin sa tace,"ya'ya ka dawo?ya aiki?

Da rawar murya ya amsa yana cewa,"Al alhamdulillah.

Dariya taso kubce Mata Amma saita matse, shikuwa janye jikin sa yayi Yana cewa,"kizo ki hada min ruwan wanka.

Ita kuwa tana ganin ya shige ciki ta miki tana tsalle ganin yanayin daya shiga tasan ba lalle bane ya iya jurewa, sannan Kuma gashi ta kwashe magungunar ta zubar.
