maryam

Aikuwa tana shiga ta tarar dashi Yana cire Kaya,da sauri ta shige toilet Dan hada mishi ruwan wanka,tana shiga ta hada masa lafiyanan ruwa Mai cike da kanshi,fitowa tayi ta tarar dashi harya cire kayan daga shi sai gajiriyar wondo,da sauri ta rufe fuskar ta da tafukan hannuwan ta.

Kallon ta Hashim yayi baice da ita komai ba saima murmushi da yayi,harya gifta zai huce,da sauri ta tonu da dayan tsarin da suka shirya, cikin sauri tasa kafa ta bige royar bed dake kusa da ita,ihu ta kulla sannan tayi baya kamar zata fadi,Hashim harya Kai kofar toilet zai shiga yaji ihunta  da gudu ya karaso gurin, Maryam batayi wata wata ba,ta fada jikin sa.

Shikuwa jin lallausar fatan ta saman jikin sa yasa yakara rikicewa rungume ta yayi kamar za'a kwace masa ita.

Maryam kuwa wasa na niman Zama gaske Dan kuwa da gaske tana Jin yanda kafar ke Mata zugi, ganin yanda take matse fuska hakan yasa ya gane akwai inda ke mata ciwo.

Jingina ta yayi da jikin sa yanda zai iya ganin fuskar ta yace,"menene?

Cikin shagwaba tace,"ya'ya kafata zafi Bata karasa maganar ba ta fashe da kuka.

Arikice Hashim yakai duban sa kan kafarta,aikuwa kafar harta fara tashi, rarrashin ta yayi sannan ya kwantar da ita kan katifar,jalabiyar sa ya dauko ya saka sannan itama ya shiga dakin ta ya dauko Mata hijabi.

Dawowa dakin yayi yasami ta akwance kana iya hango Rabin cinyar ta,Hashim dafe kansa yayi yana aiyanawa azuciyar sa Yana cewa,"idan nayi wasa Wannan yarinyar saita kashe ni da raina.

Saka mata hajabin yayi sannan ya umarce ta data tashi su tafi asibiti aduba kafar,ya mutsa fuska tayi tace,"a a ya'ya basai mun tafi asibiti ba.

Daure fuska yayi yace,"Ina wasa dake ne?

Girgiza kai tayi cikin shagwaba tace,"kayi hakuri.

Baiko tamkata ba ganin zata Bata masa lokaci hakan yasa ya dauke ta bai tsaya ko Inaba sai cikin mota.

Tafiya suke babu Mai cewa wani kala har suka Isa asibitin,jan kafar akayi sannan aka daure akayi Mata allurori sannan suka Kama hanyar gida.

Shiru kake ji cikin motar ganin shiru yayi yawa yasa Hashim kunna redio motar,wakar karki manta Dani na umar m Sharif ne yafara tashi ahankali ahankali,Maryam kuwa tuni tayi barci kasancewar anyi Mata allurar barci,tsare tada ido Hashim yayi ganin yanda take barci cikin kwanciyar hankali,ga wani irin kyau da tayi duk da babu kwalliya afuskar ta amma tayi kyau Sosa.

Murmushi yayi tunuwa da yayi da irin rigimar da tayi masa lokacin daya fara tursasa ta kan ,"itace zata Rinka bashi abinci abaki kullum,sannan ta rakashi har gun mota.

Har suka isa cikin farin ciki yake Dan akullum idan yatuna da yanda take rigimar ta cikin shagwaba,abin kwanin birgewa.

Suna Isa ya bude marfin motar daukarta yayi harzuwa dakin ta, sannan ya kwantar da ita,lullube ta yayi  sannan yayi kissing din kuma tunta yace,"Love you so much my damsel.

Murmushi ne ya kubce Mata dan abinda Hashim bai sani ba shine tuni ta dadi da farkawa kawai dai tayi lamu ne.

Ganin ta danyi murmushi yasa ya fice da sauri daga dakin.

Ita kuwa maryam murna agunta bazai misaltu ba Dan Haka waya kawai ta daga ta kira ikram, tana daga wa tashiga yi mata godiya, sannan ta sanar da ita ciwan da Taji akafar ta.

Ita Kuma zainab tuni ta koma tamkar baiwa ita da gidan uban ta, akullum cikin aiki take babu dare babu rana,ana cikin haka wata rana zainab ta shiga toilet tabar ikran tana wasa tsakar daki,Mami na ganin Haka tayi sauri ta dauke ta Bata tsaya ko ina ba sai gidan iyayan hashim,tana zuwa daidai lokacin safnah tazo gida gaida mummy.

Da sauri ta karasa gurin su ta gaishe da Mami sannan ta karbe ikram tanai Mata wasa,kallon mamin tayi tace,"Mami yasu aunty zainab?

,"Lafiyar su kalau,dama zuwa nayi Danna kawo muku ikram,mun gaji da rikonta.

Da ma'maki safnah ke kallon Mami Tama rasa bakin magana har'mami taja motar ta tayi gaba.

Ganin tsayiwar bazai amfanar da ita komai ba yasa ta shiga cikin gida tana Kiran sunan mummy.

Jin yanda safnah ke kwalla Mata Kira yasa tafito cikin sauri,ganin safnah rike da ikram ahannu tana hawaye yasa mummy ta gane dawowa da ita sukayi.

Tana Isa inda take tsaye tasa hannun ta karbi ikram sannan ta sa dayan hannun nata tashare Mata hawaye tace," menene abin kuka kuma?

Rungume mummy safnah tayi tana cewa,"mummy Dan ALLAH kiyi wa ya'ya magana ya dawo da aunty zainab wallahi tausayi take bani ,ada ban dauka Haka kishi yake ba,Saida nashiga dakin mijina nake Jin wallahi idan akayi min kishiya haukace wa kawai zanyi.

Murmushi mummy tayi tace,"Mara kunya wato ke gamai miji ko?

Sai asannan safnah tasan ta tabka babbar Kato bara,da gudu tayi waje tana cewa,"mummy kiyi hakuri Amma wallahi gaskiya nake fada miki.

Murmushi mummy tayi tace,"hmm yaran zamani saidai abar su ALLAH yashirya.

Zainab kuwa da sunan ikram ta fito daga toilet tana cewa,"ikram maman baban ta kena ina?

Wayam tagani babu ikram babu labarin ta,cikin sauri ta nufi falon abin ma'maki nanma Bata nan,Abu kamar wasa Kamar gaske babu inda bata duba ba amma babu ikram,kuka zainab keyi kamar ranta zai fita.

Ganin Bata da wata dabarar data wuce Kiran Hashim ta shaida masa.

Daidai lokacin Hashim na kwance kasancewar yau ya dawo aiki da wuri,yaji karar wayar sa batare da yaduba ba dauki wayar, maganar da yaji ne yasa ya mike arazane Yana zazzaro ido yace,"wallahi wallahi duk inda zakiyi kiyi ki tabbatar kin nimar min yata duk inda Kika sakata, idan Kuma ba Haka ba, wallahi na lahira saiya fiki jin.

Bai tsaya jin abinda zata fada mishi ba yadauki car keys yanufi dakin maryam batare da sallama ba yashiga dakin,daidai lokacin Maryam ta fito daga wanka kasancewar tasan babu Mai shigo Mata daki hakan yasa ta fito zundur hannun ta rike da yar karamar towel tana tsane kanta,kasa rintsa idonsa hashim yayi, yayin da yayi arba da manyan boob's dinta.

Ita kuwa ko karar bude kofar bataji ba,kawai juyowa  tayi taga mutum atsaye kamar gunki,ai babu shiri ta kwalla kara da gudu ta nufi hanyar komawa toilet.

Ganin Haka yasa Hashim ya yanyo hannun ta, fada saman jikin sa tayi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya Danba karamar tsorata tayi ba,ganin yanda ta makalkale shi yasa yayi gyarar murya danda alamun ta mance babu kaya ajikin ta.

Aikuwa tana duba jikin ta taganta haihuwar uwarta ai babu shiri ta yayi bedsheet dinta ta lullube jikin ta dashi.

Murmushi kawai Hashim yayi  cikin zuciyar sa yana cewa,"dama kin bar'abinki Dan aikin gama ya gama,sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi dan ko babu komai ya  danji sanyi aransa.

Gyara murya yayi yace,"kiyi sauri ki shirya zamu tafi Kano yanzu ikram ta bace.

Da sauri ta dago ido tana kallon sa, nandanan hawaye yafara wanke mata fuska,Hashim gani kamar bazata iya shirya kanta cikin sauri ba ,yasa ya dauko mata wata sari wacce ya sayo Mata jiya,mai ya dauko yafara shafa Mata Saida ya shafeta fas, sannan ya dauko Mata bra.

Ganin da gaske yake yasa ta karba ta saka,cikin mintina kalilan ta kammala shirin ta, bakaramar kyau sari din yayi Mata ba, Amma kasancewar ba ayanayin mai dadi suke ba, hakan yasa ko kallon shigar batayi ba tafice.

￼

Awaje ta same shi,yana zaman jiranta, bakaramar kyau yayi Mata ba ta yanda harta karasa gunsa Bata sani ba.

￼

Hashim

Shikuwa baima san tafito ba Dan rintsa idonsa yayi Yana tuno yanda ya ganta adazu.❄❄❄❄❄

3⃣3⃣⏏3⃣4⃣


_______Yanda taga ya rintsa ido abin ba karamar ma'maki yabata ba,to," meyake tunani Haka?hakan Nan taji tana son sanin abinda yake wa wannan murmushi harda ritsa ido,amma babu dama.

Karamar tsake taja har'zata wuce taji an fisgo ta, tafada jikin mutum,da sauri ta dago Kai tana kallon sa,yatsun hannun sa biyu yasa yadan daki bakin ta,da sauri ta rike bakin  danba karamar zafi Taji ba,sakin ta yayi ya nufi mota Yana cewa,"hukuncin mace Mai yiwa mijinta tsaki kenan.

Bin bayansa tayi hannun ta rike da baki tana mararrace fuska,gidan baya ta bude zata shiga taji yana cewa,"yaushe na zama driven gidan ku?

Batare data tamka ba ta bude gidan gaba ta zauna har yanzu dai Bata cire hannun abaki ba,Dan yanda take ji da duk kan alamu bakin yadan tashi,haka suka Kama hanya babu Wanda yace da wani cikan ka.

Ahankali ya waigo Dan yanda yake Jin sautin fitar numfashin ta yasan ta dadi dayin barci,ahankali ya sauke idanuwan sa saman lips dinta da sauri yawaro ido Yana kallon yanda bakin yadan tashi.

Girgiza kai sa yayi yace,"Kai mema yasa nayi mata haka?

Yana cikin zancan zuci yaga lips dinta na mutsi da alama mafarki take,hakanan yaji Yana son Jin abinda take fada,samun guri yayi ya faka,sannan ya rage sautin dake tashi a motar,daidai saitin bakin ta ya ije kunnuwan sa, ahankali take furta "Ina sonka ya hashim.

Ware ido yayi zuciyar sa tana karyata masa abinda kunnuwan sa suka jiyo masa,saki daida tai kunnuwan sa yayi, haryanzu dai magana take,"karka rabu Dani ya Hashim kaine komai nawa.

Karar wayar sane yasa yayi firit ya daidaita zaman sa,Dan yasa babu shakka zata farka, aikuwa Haka akayi tangaran ta bude idon ta, shikuwa ganin number mummy yasa yayi saurin dagawa,saidai yanda yaji muryar ta sarar babu alamun damuwa acikin muryar ta ya aina Killa Bata sani bane.

Gaisheta yayi sannan yace,"mummy muma muna hanya IN SHA ALLAH nanbada dadewa ba zamu karasu"

,"To ALLAH yakawo ku lafiya dama nakira Kane nayi maka maganar yarinyar Nan zainab,dansun Aiko da ikram dazu"

Cike da ma'maki yace,"mummy ita zainab dinda kanta ta kawo ta?

,"A a mahaifiyar tace"

"Shikenan kawai yace wa mummy ya aje wayar da tunani kala kala aransa.

Maryam kuwa batace komai ba,har suka karasa garin Kano ta dabo tumbin giwa Koda mekazo anfika.

Suna zuwa da gudu Maryam ta shiga cikin gidan tana Kiran sunan mummy,amma me ganin ikram tana wasa yasa tayi turus atsaye tana kallon ta cike da ma'maki,Jin muryar Maryam yasa mummy ta wanke hannun ta Dan dama aiki take a kitchen,itama da saurinta ta nufo falon saidai ganin yanda maryam ta tsaya tana kallon ikram hakan yasa tasan da akwai damuwa.

Ganin mummy yasa Maryam ta fada jikin ta tana cewa,"nayi kiwar ki sosai mummy na,itama mummy farin cikin ne sosai aranta ganin yanda Maryam tayi kyau abinta.

Tsaye Hashim  yayi yana kallon yanda mummy ke nuna farin cikin ta, gyara murya yayi yace,"wato mummy kin mance Dani 'diyar ki kawai Kika sani kenan?

Dariya sukayi atare,"sannan mummy tace,"Wama yasan dakai,Ni 'diya ta kawai na sani.

Ikram najin muryar daddyn ta da gudu tazo ta fada jikin sa tana murnan ganin sa,Bayan sundan huta sunci abinci Hashim ke sanar da mummy yanda sukayi da zainab dazu.

Da ma'maki afuskar ta take kallon shi daga bisani tace,"yaron nan inaso kasani shi kishi haliltace ajini take,kowani Dan Adam Yana da kalar tasa,to inaso ka dauka ita Zainab tata kalar kenan,Dan Haka nake umartar ka daka jeka dawo da matar ka.

Da sauri Maryam ta rike saitin zuciyar ta Dan yanda taji ta dauki zafi lokaci daya,Jin yanda numfashin ta ke fita da sauri yasa ta tashi da gudu ta nufi daki,duk kansu binta da ido sukayi suna ma'makin abinda tayi yanzu,cikin sauri hashim yatashi da niyar binta,'daga mishi hannu mummy tayi alamun karya bita.

Hakanan badan Yana soba ya Koma ya zauna, murmushi mummy tayi tace,"naji dadin ganin yanda Maryam ta nuna kishin ta akanka,Kuma a gabana duk da tsananin kunyar da ke tsakani na da ita,hakan na nuni da yanda take tsananin sonka kenan,na ta yaka murna da samun mace salaha,kamila,wacce zuciyar ta ke ciki da tsantsar tsoron ALLAH.

"Saidai duk da Haka Ina horan ka da kayi adalci atsakanin su,karka shiga hakin daya saboda ka faranta ran daya,tashi katafi gunta.

Kamar dama jira yake ta bashi umarni,cikin sauri ya nufi dakin kwance ya same ta tana kuka Mai tsuma zuciya,karasa gurin ta yayi baice da ita komai ba,itama da alama Bata San da zuwan saba,ganin Bata da niyar tsagaita kukan nata hakan yasa ya Kira sunan ta cikin murya Mai sanyaya zuciya yace,"Maryam yaushe Kika fara sona da harkike kishi akaina?

Tasowa tayi ta rungume Shi haryanzu dai kuka take rungume ajikin sa,Saida tayi Mai isarta sannna ta dago fuskar sa yanda zanta dinga kallon cikin  idon sa tace,"meyasa bazan soka ba Ya'ya?Dame ka rageni? Duk Abinda bake so kana min,ka kaunace Ni tun ban San kaina ba"

"akanka nafara sanin so,nasha zama nayi ta tambayar kaina dalilin da yasa muka fito ciki daya,ganin bani da amsar tambayar Kuma ina shiga hurumin ALLAH hakan yasa nayi istigifari,daga Nan nafara addu'a ALLAH yaban miji Mai kauna ta kwatankwacin yanda kake kaunata,sai gashi ALLAH ya azurtani da kyautar da tafi komai tsada.

Hada bakin su yayi guri daya yafara Bata lafiyayan kiss,itama batayi kasa a gwaiba tafara mayar da martani,sun dauki lokaci Mai tsawo ahaka batare da sun sani ba,tunuwa da inda suke yasa Maryam tayi saurin janye bakin ta daga nashi, shikuwa kallon ta yake da rinannun idanuwan sa Yama kasa furta komai, murmushi tayi tace,"ya'ya agidan mummy fa muke"

Kwaikwoyar maganar ta yayi yace,"to saime? ai mummy tasan ke matata ce"

Dariya tayi hartana rike ciki,ganin abin nata ya koma zolaya,Haka yasa ya shiga kamo ta tana zullewa,wasa sukayi Mai ni shadantar da zuciya,Jin anfara kiraye kirayan sallah magrib yasa duk suka nufi toilet Dan yin alwala.

Wannan kenan

Yusuf kuwa soyaya suke sosai Dan idan ka gansu sai sun baka sha'awa Abu 'daya ke yawan damun yumna,shine yawan Kama Yusuf da take Yana zubda hawaye, tambayar duniyar Nan zatayi Masa amma kullum maganar 'daya ce,"babu komai.

Abin duniya yataru yayiwa yumna yawa Dan abin haryana so yashiga cikin al'amarin hakkin aure,Yumna ta kasance mace Mai yawan sha'awa,dan akullum bataki Yusuf ya raya sunna da ita ba.

Ga Yusuf kuwa ba anan gizo ke sakar saba,Dan akuda yaushe idan Yana tare da yumna ko Yana sauke hakkin ta akansa, to babu abinda yake gani sama da fuskar Maryam,abin bakaramar damun sa yake ba,yakan tsince kansa ayanayin da shi kansa bazai iya misaltawa ba aduk lokacin da yatuna cewa maryamar sa tana Nan tare da wani kamar yanda yake tare da yumna akullum.

Suna zaune afalo suna kallon film,amma shi Sam yusuf hankalin sa ba'akan television din yake ba.

Yumna ta gane hakan ne kasancewar film dinda yake so ta saka, Amma sam bataji yayi magana  akai ba kamar yanda ya saba, ahankali ta matsa inda yake,hannu tasa ta shafe fuskar sa,abindai data Saba gani kullum shine kwance afuskar sa wato hawaye.

Jin hannuwan mutum saman fuskar sa yasa yayi fir ya dawo cikin tunanin sa,ganin yumna zaune agaban sa yasa ya dafe kansa Yana tunanin halin da yake saka kansa da iyalin sa.

Bude baki yayi da niyyar magana amma sai yaga yumna ta mike ta shiga ciki,kamar ya bita yabata hakuri amma sai yaga bashida wata karya da zai Mata ahalin yanzu,bayan mintina biyar saiga yumna tafito hannun ta rike da wata karamar akwati,sanyi take da hijabin ta ga hawaye dake zarya kan kuncin ta.

Hashim na zaune yanda ta barshi kawai sai ganin ta yayi dauke da akwati,cikin sauri ya tashi yarike akwatin Yana tambayar ta inda zata tafi cikin daran nan.

Bata kai ga bashi amsa ba ta yanki jiki zata fadi,cikin azama Yusuf ya taro ta,gani babu alamun numfashi atare da ita da gudu  yusuf  ya nufi  waje da ita Yana Kiran drive,cikin hanzari drive yafito da mota,a mota Banda masifa babu abinda Yusuf yake yiwa drive dan gani yake kamar mutuwa yumnar sa zatayi.

Suna Isa asibiti aka shigar da ita emergency room, mintina talatin sai ga likitan ya fito da fara'a afuskar sa, mikawa Yusuf hannun yayi  yace,"congrats inataya ka murna matarka tana dauke da juna biyu.

Kasa magana Yusuf yayi, dan  gabaki 'daya jiyake  kamar mafarki yake ba gaskiya bane,amma saida mee,ganin har yanzu likitan Yana tsaye agaban sa cike da murmushi afuskar sa,hakan yasa ya gasganta  zancan.

Godiya yayi ga ALLAH sannan ya mika wa likitan hannun suka saki yin musabaha, murmushi likitan yayi akaro na barkate yace,"Zaki iya tafiya gida Saidai dan ALLAH adinga kulawa.

IN SHA ALLAH Yusuf yace sannan ya nufi dakin zuciyar sa cike da farin ciki.

Koda suka Koma gida kasa zauma yayi Dan yanda yumna takiyi masa magana,abin na damunsa,ganin bazai iya juriwa ba hakan yasa ya hada hannuwan su guri 'daya yace,"ki gafarce ni yumna nasan na zalumce ki ba sau daya ba,amma ayau ina Mai niman yafiyar ki,da alkawarin hakan bazai kara faruwa ba.

Rungume Shi yunma tayi tace,"kayi alkawarin?

"Nayi alkawari.

Kissing dinsa ta farayi Dan dama tayi missing dinsa sosai kawai dai juriwa take.

Ina ganin haka na fita da gudu dankar na makance lol 😋 😋.

Wannan kenan

Zainab kuka take kamar ranta zai fita gashi babu kowa agidan bare tayi tunanin ko wani neya dauke ta,Dan hatta Yan aikin gidan gabaki daya Mami koran su tayi danma karsu taya zainab aikin gidan,tana zaune taji shigowar mami,Aida sauri ta Isa gare ta cikin kuka tana cewa,"Mami ansace min ikram banganta ba.

Ga ma'makin zainab sai taga Mami na murmushi abinda harta mance yanda murmushin mamin ta yake ,amma sai gashi yau Jin ansace aikram mamin ta harda murmushi lallai yaune ta Kara jaddada kiyayyar dake tsakanin Hashim da mamin ta.

Mami kuwa da murmushi afuskar ta tace,",karki damu yar'autata ikram ba bacewa tayi ba,nice na dauke ta na mayar da ita gidan ubanta,Danna gaji da ganin halin da Kika saka kanki adalilin ta.

Gabaki daya zainab kasa magana tayi baki bude take kallon mami, wannan wacce iriyar uwa ce?

Tambayar da zainab take yiwa kanta kenan......






Da gudu zainab ta nufi dakin ta hannunwan ta rike da saitin zuciyar ta, dan jinta take kamar zata fashi saboda tsananin zafi da take ji.

Kuka take sosai,yanda taga Rana Haka taga dare idanuwan ta duk sun kumkumbura tsabar kuka da tasha,Kiran sallar asubahi ne yasa ta tashi ta shiga toilet,alwala tayi tazo ta gabatar da sallah.

Addu'ar sosai tayi da istigifari kan laifukan da tayi abaya,Dan sosai tayi nadamar duk Abinda ta aikata,tana zaune kan sallayar har Saida gari ya waye sosai, sannan ta tashi ta shiga toilet,wanka tayi tashirya cikin atamfar ta Mai kyau.

Tana zaune taji sallamar daddyn ta,cikin sauri ta nufi gunsa,tana zuwa ta tsuguna har kasa ta gaishe shi,baiyi ma'maki ba Dan Yana lura da yanda ta sauya halayan ta cikin kankanin lokaci.

Da murmushi afuskar sa ya amsa harzata wuce yace,"Kira min mamin KU, sannan ku taho tare.

Babu gardama ta nufi dakin mami,zaune ta same ta hannun ta rike da waya,Saida ta KAMMALA wayan sannan ta sanar da ita sakon daddy.

Atare suka iso falon,batare da kallon fuskar taba yace,"jeki ki sanyo hijabin ki,da ma'maki Mami tace,"Ina zamu tafi da safannan?

"Tambaya kike ko neman sani?

Jin yanda ya mayar Mata da tambayar yasa ta gane babu wasa acikin lamarin,cikin sauri duk kansu suka dauko hijabin su,suka fito atare.

Fita yayi batare da yace dasu komai ba,ganin Haka yasa Suma suka biyo Bayan sa,acikin motar suka iske shi,shiga duk kansu sukayi, sannan yaja motar yayi gaba.

Bai tsaya ko'Ina ba sai gidan alhaji Mai kanti,da ma'maki Mami ke kallon sa tace,"alhaji me muka zoyi gidan nan,ficewa yayi Yana cewa,"idan muka shiga Zaki gani,Kuma Wallahi kikayi wani Abu na rashin Kai kinji naratse"wallahi to a bakin auran ki.

Bade baki tayi tana kallon sa cike da ma'maki har suka shige ciki suka barta tsaye,ganin babu sarki sai ALLAH yasa tabi Bayan su.

Da sallama suka shiga gidan daidai lokacin Maryam tana bawa Hashim abinci abaki,da sauri take tura Masa abincin Dan bataso mummy ta zo ta same su ahaka,shikuwa Hashim  gwalo kawai yake mata,Jin sallamar yasa Maryam tayi saurin mikewa daga kancinyar sa,saidai Ina tuni zainab ta hango su.

Jitayi kamar kafarta bazai iya daukar gangan jikin taba, da sauri ta nime daya daga cikin kujerun dake falon ta zauna, idonwanta tuni ya cika fam da hawaye tsabar kishi.

Alhaji talba sarar yaga irin yanayin data shiga amma saiya basar,cikin girmamawa Hashim da Maryam suka gaishe da alhaji talba, sannan Maryam ta nufi gun zainab.

Rusunawa tayi agabanta tace,"Aunty ina kwana, murya ciki ciki ta amsa, sannan itama ta gaishe da hashim,da fara'ar sa ya amsa har Yana tambayar ta mami,Bata kaiga bashi amsa ba Mami ta shigo bakinta dauke da sallama.

Bayan an gaggaisa Hashim ya umarce Maryam data Kira Abba da mummy,tashi tayi tana tafiyar ta cikin natsuwa,ba'afi mintina biyu ba sai gasu sun sakko atare, gaggaisa sukayi, sannan alhaji talba yafara bude taro da addu'a.

Bayan angama ya dube zainab yace,"ta kanki zan fara Zainab ki sani,nine mahaifin ki kowa ya shaida haka,amma ki sani Ina horar kida kibi mijin ki kiyi masa biyayya kwarkwadon iyawar ki,  Kika sake Kika sabawa Hashim to babu Ni babu ki,kisani daga lokacin na cire ki daga cikin 'yayana.

Bude baki abba yayi zaiyi magana alhaji talba ya dakatar dashi da hannu, sannan yaci gaba"Kai Kuma Hashim inaso kasani idan zainab tayi maka abinda Bai dace ba,kaki hukunta ta  kasani bazan taba yafe maka ba.

Yana kaiwa nan yamike  tare da yi musu sallama ya fice,Mami ma tashi tayi tabi bayansa tana hararar zainab.

Shiru gabaki 'dayan gurin yayi kafin daga bisani abba yayi gyarar murya yace,"alhamdulillah ranar da muka dade muna jira yaudai ALLAH yakawo ta,Hashim inaso kasani Amana ce ALLAH ya damka maka ahannun ka,kasani idanfa kaci amanar daya daga cikin su ALLAH bazai barka ba,Dan Haka nake Horan ka daga ji tsoron ALLAH kayi adalci atsakanin su.

Sannan ke Kuma Maryam kisani koda babu auran Hashim akan zainab tofa ta girme mike,dan hakane dole ki Bata girman ta,babu rai ni atsakanin ku,KU dauka ku Yan uwan junane ba kishiyoyi ba.

Ke Kuma zainab ki Kama girman ki,Dan saita hakane Maryam zata girmamawa ki.

ALLAH yayi Mana jagora,duk ka suka amsa da ameen.

Washa gari da safe suka Kama hanyar kaduna,babu jimawa suka isa da farin ciki zainab ta nufi shashin ta Danko amafarki Bata dauka zata kara sako kafarta cikin gidan ba,Maryam ma shashin ta shige,tana shiga ta fada toilet,shirinta tayi tsaf cikin riga da wondo sannan ta dauko hular sanyi ta saka bakaramar kyau tayi ba,Bata tsaya bata lokaci ba ta nufi kitchen,lafiyaiyar cuscus ta dafa musu Mai cike da kayan hadi kala kala.

￼

Maryam

Saida ta kammala ta jera saman
dinning sannan ta nufi shashin zainab da nufin ta fito suci abinci,tana shiga ta iske ta rike da waya da alamu waya take,tsayuwa Maryam tayi Saida ta gama sannan cikin girmamawa tace,"aunty zainab kizo muci abinci.

Murmushi tayi ta kamo hannuwan ta tace,"Maryam nasan nayi miki ba daidai ba Amma Dan ALLAH ki gafarta min"

Cikin sauri maryam tasa hannun ta rufe Mata baki tace,"a a aunty nice yakamata na baki hakuri Dan ALLAH ki gafarce ni idan nayi miki ba daidai ba.

Murmushi zainab tayi tace,"idan Banda abinki wakika taba gani yayi fushi da kanwar sa?

Murmushi itama tayi sannan suka fito atare.

Saida aunty zainab ta zauna sannan Maryam ta nufi shashin hashim,tana zuwa ta iske shi Yana saka kaya shidin ma shiri yayi kusan irin nata yayi kyau abinsa,tana zuwa ta rungume Shi, lafiyaiyar kiss ta bashi Wanda Saida ya lumshi idonsa.

Budisu yayi lokacin da ta zame bakin ta, murmushi tayi tace,"ya'ya abinci,kallon ta yake cike da ma'maki," yaushe  

￼

muka dawo da harkika samu lokacin girki.

"Ya'ya mutafi idan muka je kagane wa idonka,bin bayanta yayi babu gardama,tun kafin su karasa gun ya hango zainab a hakimce saman dinning,hada girar sama dana kasa yayi Dan yama mance da wanzuwar ta cikin gidan.

Bayan sun kammala cin abuncin yatara su gabaki daya a falon sa, sannan yafara jawabi kamar haka,"Ina fata duk kanku kunji abinda Abba ya fada muku?to zandaura ne saman nashi,inaso kusani gidana ba gidan fada bane bare harta kaiga dambe, sannan banason tashin hankali duk wacce na lura itace ke kawo min matsala to wallahi KU sani babu Bata lokaci zan sallame ta"

Dan Haka nake garkadin KU daku zauna lafiya da junan ku, idan damai magana Ina sauraron ta.

Gyara zama zainab tayi tace,"IN SHA ALLAH bazaka taba samun mu da aikata makamanciyar Haka ba.

"To alhamdulillah Maryam kefa ina sauraron ki"

Murmushi tayi tace,"babu abinda zance ya'ya.

"To shikenan ALLAH yabamu hakurin zama da juna.

Da ameen duk suka amsa, sannan kowa ta shige dakin ta,tunda Hashim ya fita bai dawo ba sai Bayan isha'i,yana dawowa dakin zainab ya nufa hannun sa rike da leda biyu duk iri daya,Bayan tayi masa sannu da dawowa ya mika mata lidar hannun sa sannan yayi Mata Saida safe.

Dama tasan hakan zata kasance shiyasa Bata nuna damuwar taba.

Yana shiga sashin Maryam ya ganta zaune tana wasa da wayar hannun ta da alama game take,da sanda yakara sa gunta,fisgar wayar yayi da karfi,Maryam kuwa ihu ta kwalla Dan ba karamar tsoro taji ba..
ganin yanda jikin ta ke rawa yasa ya rungume ta tsam ajikin sa Yana shafar sa
Sumar kanta,Saida yaga ta dawo natsuwar ta sannan ya dube ta yana dariya yace,"matsoraciya kawai.

Shagwabe fuska tayi tace,"bakai bane ka bani tsoro"

To naji yi hakuri yanzu dai tashi kiban abinci yunwa nake ji,waro ido tayi tace,"na shiga uku habibi na Yana Jin yunwa,da sauri ta dauko hular kanta Wanda garin tsoro ta hurgar,harzata wuce taji ya damko hannun ta yace,"Ina Kuma Zaki tafi?

Da ma'maki take kallon sa tace,"ya'ya Kaine fa kace kana jin yunwa"

"Eh haka nace"

"To aishi ne zandibo maka.

Kamuta yayi ya kwantar da ita saman kijinsa yace,"to gaki wani abinci Kuma zanbokata.

Da sauri tasa hannuwan ta takare fuskar ta tana dariya ciki ciki.

Tashi yayi yace,"kiyi alwala ina zuwa.

.,"ya'ya nafa riga nayi sallah.

"Eh nasani bana son musu.

"To kawai tace ta nufi toilet.

Bayan mintina biyar sai gashi yadawo hannun sa rike da holandia daya Kuma rike da faranti madaidaiciya.

Azaune yasame ta tana zaman jiran sa kirjinta sai bugawa take tsabar tsoro.

Sarar yagane atsorace take,amma saiya basar, ahaka suka idar da sallah Bayan yayi duk wani tambaya daya wajaba ango yayiwa amaryar sa, sannan ya dauko farantin ya juye kazar aciki.

Abaki yashiga Bata harsaida tayi masa Nuni data kushi sannan shima yafaraci

Bayan sun gama ta kwashe komai ta Kai kitchen Saida ta wanke su tsaf sannan ta fito ahankali take tafiya harta karasa gunsa,tashi yayi yataro ta, rungume ta yayi ajikin sa sannan yace,"meke damunki?

"Babu komai ya'ya.

"Kin tabbata?

"Eh.

"To shikenan ki rage kayan jikin ki kizo ki same Ni adaki.

"Nod da Kai kawai tayi.

Shikuwa Hashim Yana fita ya saki murmushi Dan yaga yanda jikin ta ke rawa.

Tana gamawa ta nufi dakin sa zuciyar ta cike da tsoro,akwance ta same shi,Yana jin alamun shigowar ta yamike cikin sauri Dan wani irin kamshi da yaji ta daki hancin sa.

Kasa dauke idon sa daga kanta yayi Dan rigar data saka kana iya hango komai na jikin ta aciki.

Kamuta yayi ya kwantar da ita lumshe idonta tayi tanajin yanda tafiyar tsunso da yake mata ke shiga duk wani sako na jikin ta.

Ahankali harya karaso gunta kissing dinta yashiga yi cikin salon sa Mai tafiya da duk wani tunani nata,cikin Haka ta tunu da maganar da ikram ta fada Mata kamar haka,"karki yarda ki kwanta kamar ruwa kina jiran sa yayi kidinsa Kuma yayi rawar sa,a a kema taimaka masa zakiyi,kina mayar masa da martani,ki ije kunya agefe ki faranta ran mijinki,kiyi masa wasanni ,kibi kowani lungu da sako kina lasa ahankali ,idan Kika kasance haka tofa babu shakka kice kan gaba agun mijin ki.

Babu Bata lokaci Maryam ta shiga mayar da martani,gani Haka yasa ya Kara azama, nipples dinta ya Kama wannan Karan ahankali yake tsosa yanda bazata ji zafi ba,Saida ya tsose ta saf ,sannan yakuma kasa,'daga kafafuwan ta yayi yashiga shanta ahankali yake wasa da harshansa aciki,tuni Maryam ta mance duniyar da take,Banda fitar da numfashi babu abinda take.

Ganin Haka yasa Hashim ya zaro joystick dinsa yafara kwakarin zurawa,tofa nanni labari ya canza Dan Maryam kwakarin hanashi take,Amma ina Hashim bayaji baya gani, lokacin daya samu nasara joystick dinsa ta shiga ciki atare duk suka saki kara,shi Hashim karan dadi ne daya ji,ita Kuma na azaba da taji ya ziyarci ta.

Kuka take da iya karfin ta,tana rokon sa daya barta haka,amma hashim baima san duniyar da yake ba.

Gani Hashim bashida ni'iya barinta yasa nace,"bara bayi shiru kar rahnur da serloni 11 &rrahinaabdoulkader suce nayi  rashin kunya ,Dan Haka nidai nayi Nan idan kuka gama saina dawo LoL 😋😋😋

Ban tashi komawa ba Saida naji saukar ruwa a toilet,da sauri na koma Dan dauko muku rahoto,ina zuwa na tarar babu koma adakin,le

kawa toilet nayi ,nanna hagosu Hashim Yana tsomata cikin ruwan zafi dayaji detol,ita Kuma sai shagwaba take masa.

Saida ya gasata sosai sannan yabarta danta tsaftace jikin ta.

_____Batare da ya furta ko kalma ɗaya ba,yanufi cikin gidan,Yana zuwa ya tarar da Maryam harta fara shan'miyar tata,da murmushi afuskar sa ya dube ta yace,"sahiba kece yau kuma ruwa Kika koya kuma?,"a a Habibi na idan ma zanyi sai nayiwa murandin zuciya ta?

"To shine nima na gani ai"baice da ita komai ba har saida ta gama sannan yace,"nikuwa ba mummy ta Baki hoton Mamin mu ba?

"Eh ta bani Yana guna.

"To maza dauko ki same Ni adakin baki,da ma'maki take kallon sa tace,"dakin baki kuma da hoton Mami na?

"Eh.

Haka nan taji gabanta na dukan uku uku, saidai tayi shiru ta nufi dakin ta,shikuwa hakuri ya Basu yace,"tana zuwa.

Tsabar kaguwa tashi alhaji yayi Yana zaga gurin,lokacin da yayi ido hudu da Maryam hawaye ne ya shiga zarya a idon sa Dan babu ta inda tabar kamanin suwaiba kanwar sa.

Ita kuwa ganin babban mutum kaman wannan Yana zubda hawaye yasa itama idanuwan ta suka cicciko da kwalla,taru ta Hashim yayi yabata kujira ta zauna sannan yakai duban sa gun Yusuf,shikuwa tuni ya shilla duniyar masoya yanda yaga Maryam ta canza fatar ta tayi kyau da sheki.

Da sauri yakai duban sa gun alhaji yace,"gata kamar yanda kuka bukata ,Dan ALLAH muyi abinda yatara mu anan kowa ya Kama gaban sa matata Bata da lafiya.

Tuni alhaji ya hango jirgin Hashim Dan Yana lura da yanayin kallon da Yake mata shiyasa ko kadan zancan Hashim bai bata masa rai ba.

Gyarar murya yayi yace,"'yata shinko mahaifiyar ki ta taba Baki labarin Dan uwan ta Wanda ya rasu sanadiyar hadarin mota?

Shiru tayi daga bisani tace,"eh Amma ba daga bakin ta naji ba,hasalima bansanta ba.

Cikin firgici alhaji ya Miki tsaya Yana cewa,"mekike nufi da baki santaba?
Waya baki labari?

"Sharar hawaye tayi tace,"ranar da aka haifini ranar tabar gidan duni...

Kasa karisa maganar tayi sakamakon Fadi da alhaji yayi asume,cikin sauri Hashim ya shiga yayyafa masa ruwa,Saida yakai mintina talatin sannan yadawo cikin haiyacin sa,saidai yanda fuskar sa ta fada lokaci daya zaka rantse yayi shekara Yana ciwo.

Maryam ce ta dago kai cike da damuwa afuskar ta tace,"ko zansan dalili daya sa kake tambaya akan mahaifiya ta?

Kwalla na zuba afuskar sa yace,"mahaifiyar ki takasance kanwa aguna,nine wannan 'Dan uwan nata Wanda suke tunanin na dade da mutuwa a hatsarin mota.

Kuka Mai sauti ta fashe dashi,kuka Mai ciki da farin cikin samun wani Wanda zata Kira nata, rarrashin ta Hashim ya shigayi Saida ta dauki lokaci Mai tsayi tanayi daga bisani ta fito da hoton ta mikawa alhaji tana cewa,"itace wannan ko?

Nod da Kai kawai yayi.

Murmushi Maryam tayi wannan Karan sannan tace,"kawo kozan iya sanin abinda yafaru dakai wancan lokacin?

Gyara Zama yayi yace,"wato kinsa duk Abinda ALLAH ya kaddara tofa babu shakka sai ya afku,to hakanne ta kasance dani,na kasance inada wani aboki Mai suna auwal,mahaifinsa Yana sayar da man fitur ne a Depot dake garin maje karamar hukumar suleja Dan akarkashin masarautar suleja take.

Kullum tare muke yawo,babu abinda nake boye masa yasan komai nawa nima nasan tashi,wata Rana Bayan nabar gidan cike da yunwa da kishin ruwa na nufi gidan su,ina zuwa aka sanar dani cewa baya Nan,Dana tambaya sai akace,"aiya fara bin mahaifin sa Depot,da ma'maki afuska ta lokacin da naji Haka Dan aiya sanina munyi dashi duk lokacin da zaifara tare zamu tafi.

Ina fita na hango shi Yana zuwa,cikin sauri na karasa gare shi nake tambayar sa dalilin daya sa yayi min haka, murmushi yayi yace,"shin baka yarda da amintakar dake tsakanina dakai bane?

Gyada Kai nayi nace,"na yarda Mana.

"To ka yarda babu inda naje yanzu ma Haka daga gidan ku nake akace kafita.

Rungume Shi nayi nace,"shiyasa nake Kara kaunar ka aboki na.

Muna cikin Haka saiga abokin mahaifin sa yazo  Kiran sa Wai mahaifin sa Yana Kiran sa,Yana bakin titin kaduna road,da sauri  muka nufi motar mutumin yaja muka tafi duk atunanin mu amso sako kawai zamuyi.

Muna zuwa yace,"mu shiga mota daga Nan asaba yayi daukar mai,da kamar nace bazani ba Amma saina tuna da halin da 'yar uwata take ciki Dani kaina,babu Bata lokaci na shiga cikin motar muka tafi,.muna kaiwa zuba mahaifin auwulu ya tsaya siyan ruwa,tsayuwar mu keda wuya,na hango abu Wanda shima ya kasance aboki agare ni,babu Bata lokaci na kwalla mishi kira,da gudu ya karaso gurin Yana tambaya ta inda zantafi.

Sakon tafiyar mu nabashi nace,"ya fadawa suwaiba. Sannan muka tafi.

Koda muka isa garin asaba Saida mukayi kusan wata daya agarin saboda mahaifin auwalu yakasance ma naiman mata ga Kuma shaye shaye,hakan da auwalu yagani bakaramar tunzura shiyayi ba,gani ga babbar sa agidan kullum cikin babu suke,Ashe shi Yana Nan Yana sharho liyar sane da kudi.

Kuka sosai auwalu yayi sannan yace,"wallahi babu abinda zaisa ya biyu mahaifinsa zuwa gida,to Yana iya abokina nane,Dan Haka nima na yanke shawarar binsa.

Haka kuwa akayi muka shiga cikin garin asaba batare da munsan kowa ba,intakaita Miki labarin Saida mukayi shaikara hudu cikin garin asaba muna Fadi tashi niman na halalin mu.

Kullum cin tunanin halinda mahaifiyar ki take ciki nake,Amma Idan na tuna da ba'ba mairo sai naji hankali na ya kwanta Dan nasan zata kulamin da ita.

Muna cikin Haka wata Rana muka ji ana cigiyar masu aiki,wato wani sabon guri ne aka budi a kaduna to Mai ma'aikatan Dan kaduna ne shiyasa yake niman wa yanda zasu Dan rinka Masa aikace aikaca agunrin,kamar masu yin dakon shigar da buhunan folawar da share sharan gurin, lokacin da najewa auwalu da sharawar, da farko kin amincewa yayi acewar sa,baya fatan zuwa gida batare da yatara abin duniya ba.

Bakaramar kwakari nayi ba, wajan ganin na shawo kansa harna samu ya yarda,cikin ikon ALLAH muka dawo kaduna da aiki,rikon amana da ciki alkawari yasa mutumin ya aminta damu ya bamu babbar matsayi cikin ma'akatan,cikin kankanin lokaci muka fara samun sauyin rayuwa.

Dan alokacin inada naira dubu ashirin nawa,ana cikin haka muka hadu da mata suwaiba sunan da take dashi shiya fara jawo shakuwa atsakanin mu, kasancewar ta yarinyar Mai gidan mu,hakan kuwa bakaramar batawa mutanin dake gurin Rai yayi ba, Dan acewar su zan kara samun wani matsayin fiye dasu.

Lokacin da babanta yaji labarin soyayar mu,kirana yayi ya tambaye Ni ra'ayina akan diyar sa,ban boye masa komai game da niba, Bayan na gama sai yace,"babu komai,Amma yanzu idan akace kabada sadakinta  kana dashi?

A a gaskiya abinda nake dashi Bai wuce dubu ashirin ba.

"To babu damuwa idan ka shirya saika turo abokan ka sukawo kudin amatsayin kudin sadakin yata,na baka ita halak malak,kuma bana bukatar komai agunka, akwai gida na dake badarawa itama na baka ita halak malak.

Godiya sosai nayi masa,cikin kankanin lokaci aka daura aure na da suwaiba ,Bayan wasu wattanin muka nufi garina suleja da nufin daukar 'yar uwata mu dawo kaduna,ko babu komai zamu huce zaman azaban da muka dade acikin ta.

Ina zuwa kuwa gabaki 'daya garin sai bina da kallo ake duk inda na wuce da zarar nayi musu sallama sai naga suna bina da kallon ma'maki atunani  na saboda canzawar da nayi ne.

Da sallama na shigo cikin gidan ,Amma saime koina ya Zama kamar kango babu kowa acikin gidan,Saida na samu wani Mai shago dake kofar gidan yake sanar dani duk Abinda ya faru Bayan tafiya ta,da yarda kishiyar mama mu ta hula kanta daga bisani Kuma ta rasu mutuwa irinta Walakance.

Adaburce na dawo kaduna tun daga Nan nashiga niman inda zanganku amma Ina sai yau da ALLAH yayi zanga wani tsarso na suwaiba.

Kuka ya fashe da Yana tuna yanda suwaiba ta Rinka wahala dashi kamar itace yarsa,gashi yanzu yana da duk wani abinda zai iya taimaka mata dashi saidai Ina Bata nan.

Abba na hango Yana rarrashin sa 😲Ware ido nayi nace,"Kai yaushe mutan kano suka iso ban sani ba?
Murmushi na hango hashim yake yace,"yaushe zaki sani Bayan kinanan kina sauraron gulma harda sake baki,murguda Masa baki nayi nace,"Dani kake wato Dan kaga na baka Maryam ko ?to wallahi saina kwace ta na baiwa Yusuf sannanne zaka shiga hankalin ka.

Cikin sauri ya sauko kan gwaiwarsa Yana bani hakuri, murmushi nayi nace"ALLAH yaso ka, LoL 😋😋😋

Bashi labarin zaman ta kewar su da suwaiba da isah sukayi gabaki daya basu rage komai ba.

Godiya yayi musu sosai sannan yace,"Yana niman alfarma suje suga gidan da yake, murmushi abba yayi yace,"ai wanna ba alfarma bace,dole ce.

Da Haka suka tashi suka nufi gidan sa harda Zainab da ikram....

Gidane Mai Kyau da tsari Wanda zuwana na nafarko ban tsaya na kare Masa kallo ba sai yanzu, kallo na tsaye yi har Saida na hango rahrur tana daga min hannun aikuwa da gudu nabi Bayan su,babban falo ne Mai ciki da abubuwan mure rayuwa,alhaji kasa Zama yayi dauko wancan dauko wancan gabaki daya yama rasa abinda zai kawo musu.

Ganin Haka yasa abba yayi murmushi yace,"alhaji karka damu kanka wannan ma aiya isa,"murmushi yayi kawai Dan yama rasa bakin magana,Maryam kuwa kasa dauke ido tayi daga kansa,har'saida yagane kamar tana son Zama kusa da shine,Kiran ta yayi da hannu yace,"zo Mana maryam zoki zauna kusa dani.

Kamar dama jira take babu Bata lokaci ta nufi gunsa tana zuwa ta fada jikin sa nandanan ta fashi da kuka tana kankame dashi kamar za'a kwace mata shi,har zai rarrashe ta Amma Abba ya hanashi cewa yayi,"Alhaji barta tayi hakan nee kadai zaisa taji saukin abinda take ji,rashin uwa ba abin wasa bane.

Haka kuwa akayi Maryam kukan ta tasha sosai harta gode ALLAH,Jin fitar numfashin ta  da sauri hakan yasa ya gane tayi barci,harzai daga ta da sauri Hashim ya karaso gurin dagata yayi saf,ya kwanta da ita saman sofa dake kusa da shi hade da gyara Mata kwanciyar dan yasan alhaji na iya cewa,"yabarta shiyasa Bai yarda ankaita daki ba.

Sun dade sosai suna hira harzuwa lokacin Maryam tana barci bata farka ba,har sun tashi zasu tafi Hashim na zaune,aikuwa da amai Maryam ta farka,akidime Hashim ya kankame ta ajikin sa,duk inda taso ta kwace kanta dankarta mishi ajiki,ina hashim yaki sakinta,ganin Bata da wani  zabi yasa tafara kwara amai da iya karfin ta.

Ganin yanda take kakarin Aman yasa,"Abba yace ,"maza Hashim wanke Mata jiki mu tafi asibiti.

Babu musu alhaji ya nuna musu toilet suka shiga wanke ta yayi tsaf, sannan suka fito,suna zuwa  Babu Bata lokaci likita ya shiga gwaje gwajan sa,Saida ya gama sannan ya nufi waje dankai albishir ga hashim, likitan na fitowa ya mikawa Hashim hannun yace,"congratulations your wife is pregnant.

Alhamdulillah alhamdulillah kawai Hashim ke furtawa kowa dake gurin ma Haka daga bisani alhaji yasa hannun cikin aljihu ya zaro ma kudan kudi ya mikawa likitan fuskar sa kamar gonar audiga tsabar farin ciki 

Hashim ma Haka intakaita muku likintan bakaramar samu yayi ba aranar,Bayan ta huta suka nufi gida,alfarma Maryam da Hashim suka nima gun iyayan ikram Danta zota yi Mata kwana biyu.

Yau cikin Maryam ya shiga wata takwas idan ka ganta saika tausaya Mata duk ta kumbura Bata iya ta buka komai da kanta,Saida aka kawo Mata 'yar aikin dan ta rinka ta yata aikin gida, zainab itama tana  iya bakin kwakarin ta.

Duk kansu suna zaune Maryam da karamin bowel ahannun Wanda cike yake da fruit salad Mai dandanu kwanin dadi.

Jitayi kamar an mintsele ta cikin sauri ta rike bayan ta,ganin Haka yasa da sauri Hashim ya karaso inda take zaune yace,"madam lafiya?

Yatsina fuska tayi Dan yanzu ciwan da take jiya fara tsananta, ahankali ta dago tace,"ya'ya ciki na ciwo.

Arude ya nufi dakin ta ya har hado Kayan jariran da suka siya,suna zuwa asibitin kuwa aka shigar da ita labor room,wahala sosai Maryam Tasha dan har'hashim yayi sign  cewa za'ayi Mata c u, ALLAH cikin hikimar sa ya sauke ta lafiya,kyawawan Yan Mata gudu biyu na hango Kamanin su daya sak maryam.

Duk Wanda ka kalla agurin kasan Yana cike da farin ciki.

Ana jibi suna Ina zaune saiga  team  maryam  sun iso kamar su rahnur da dai sauran su,suna rokon asaka sunan su, murmushi kawai Hashim yayi yace,"to safnah wani suna ya dace asawa 'yan biyu na?

gyada Kai nayi nace to bara muga wacce take da kwakarin vote a cikin su,sauke ajiyar zuciya nayi nace,"gaskiya Hashim mutane hudu su sukafi kowa kwagari,kaga Kuma Kai 'Yan biyu ke gare ka ba hudu ba,Dan Haka a shawarce asaka sunan mamin Maryam da  sister kawai.

"Wace sister kenan?

"Sister safnah mana"

"Kina ganin hakan yayi?

Murmushi nayi nace,"kasan sister tana da ciki itama kaga kenan idan itama ta haihu ta Sami namiji saita saka sunan ka,ko baka so?

Murmushi yayi yace,"gaskiya agaishe ki hajiya safnah.

Washa gari kuwa akayi shagalin bikin suna,yara sunci sunan suwaiba da safnah, Maryam ta  samu kyautar kaya da kudi masu yawa wasu mutanan ma Bata taba ganin suba,alhaji kuwa Wanda ayanzu duk suke kiran sa kawu, kyautar sabuwar mota yayi mata aranar Saida tayi kuka don murna.

Bayan shekara biyu na hango suwaiba da safnah suna wasa cikin nishadi fuskokin su na kalla nace,"wow gaskiya suna da mattukar kyau.

Ten years

Wow har sunkai shekaru goma ina zaune guri daya,da murmushi Naga Hashim ya shigo cikin dakin su Yana cewa,"ya'yan daddy Ina kuka shiga ne aunty ikram sai niman KU take?

Yatsina fuska safnah tayi tace,"danla daddy barta ta cika shishigi wallahi Ni fa bason t..

Tsawa Hashim ya daka Mata,"ke bana hanaki fadan irin wannan maganar ba?ita ba yayar ki  bace?
To wallahi saina sassa ba Miki idan na Kara jin irin haka.

Kuka ta fashe dashi ta nufi gun maryam,haryanzu tana Nan yanda kuka Santa saidai yanzu tadan Kara jiki tayi kyau gwanin ban sha'awa.

Da harara Naga take kallon ta tace,"abinda ya sakaki kuka kenan?

"Nod da Kai tayi tana mararrace fuska.

To yanzu kin kwasu jiki kinzo gurina me kike so nayi miki?

Kuka sosai safnah keyi ganin anan ma Bata Samu goyan baya ba.

"Fitar min daga daki idan baza kiyi shiru ba.

Fita tayi can lambon gida ta zauna tana zancan zuci kamar Haka,"wallahi ikram tunda Kika saka daddy da mummy suka min fada wallahi saina Miki abinda kema saikin yi kuka da idanuwan ki.

Tashi tayi ta nufi bangaran Zainab tana kwalla Kiran ikram bayan ta saka bawun ayaba abakin kofar ikram,jin murya safnah yasa ikram bude kofar ta cikin sauri Dan aiya tsayin rayuwar su yauce Rana ta farko da tafara Jin safnah ta kira ta da aunty.

Aikuwa tana fitowa ta zame jikake dam akasa,wata uwar Kara ta sake tana kallon kafarta, safnah kuwa dariyar mugunta ta saki tana cewa," Wallahi bakiga komai koba muddun baki fita safgar muba, sannan ki sani duk Abinda umman ki tayiwa mummy na duk mun samu labari Dan Haka wallahi sai mun Rama akanki ki jir..

Wani wawan Mari Maryam ta dauke ta dashi tana cewa,"Anya ke jini nane kuwa wallahi albasa batayi halin ruwa ba,janta tashiga yi,har suka shiga ciki.

Zaneta sosai Hashim yayi daga bisani ya sata ta baiwa ikram hakuri.

Yusuf da yumna komai ya koma daidai sanin cewa,"Maryam ta riga da tayi masa nisa na har'abadan, ashraf kuwa tuni ya hakura ya barwa yarimar daura jawairiya ganin ta koina yafishi, daidaita was sukayi da ikram kawar maryam.lol

Ba'adau lokaci Mai tsayi ba aka daura musu aure...

Alhamdulillah

*Nanna kawo KARSHAN LILTAFINA MAI SUNA YA'YA NANE KO MIJINA,kuskuran dake ciki ALLAH ya gafarta Mana,sakon da muka isar Kuma muna fatan ALLAH yasa ya Isa inda muke fata,*
